FILE: scripts\inspectFunc649.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 649 RAW INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Searching CALL references to FUNC649...");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 2; p++) {
  if (b[p] === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p + 1);

    if (r.value === 649) {
      hits++;

      console.log("");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
      console.log(`CALL FUNC649 @ 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      for (
        let q = Math.max(0, p - 32);
        q <= Math.min(b.length - 1, p + 16);
        q++
      ) {
        console.log(
          `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[q]
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`TOTAL CALLERS OF FUNC649 = ${hits}`);

console.log("");
console.log("NOTE:");
console.log("This scan is raw byte based.");
console.log("No WASM execution.");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc649Body.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function readWasmSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections: any[] = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const r = readULEB(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + size,
      size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = readWasmSections();

const funcSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Function/code section not found");
}

// Function section: vector of type indices for DEFINED functions.
// Imported functions are excluded from this vector.
let p = funcSection.start;
const fr = readULEB(p);
const funcCount = fr.value;
p = fr.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {
  const r = readULEB(p);
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
  p = r.next;
}

const targetGlobalFunc = 649;
const importedFunctions = 83;

if (targetGlobalFunc < importedFunctions) {
  throw new Error("FUNC649 is imported, unexpected");
}

const codeOrdinal = targetGlobalFunc - importedFunctions;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 649 BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC     = ${targetGlobalFunc}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCS  = ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL    = ${codeOrdinal}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX      = ${typeIndices[codeOrdinal]}`);
console.log("");

// Code section
p = codeSection.start;

const cr = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = cr.value;
p = cr.next;

let targetStart = -1;
let targetEnd = -1;
let targetSize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sr = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = sr.value;
  p = sr.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  if (i === codeOrdinal) {
    targetStart = bodyStart;
    targetEnd = bodyEnd;
    targetSize = bodySize;
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (targetStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("FUNC649 body not found");
}

console.log(
  `BODY = 0x${targetStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${(
    targetEnd - 1
  ).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(`SIZE = ${targetSize}`);
console.log("");
console.log("RAW BODY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let q = targetStart; q < targetEnd; q++) {
  console.log(
    `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[q]
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc7006.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempPath = path.join(dir, '__inspectFunc7006_decoder_temp.mjs');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'inspectFunc7006_output.txt');

const source = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = source.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');
}

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempPath,
        source.slice(0, cut) +
            '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + '?inspectFunc7006'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;
const target = 7006;

if (!bodies.has(target)) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${target} has no defined body`);
}

const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(target);
const selected = new Map();
const calls = [];

const patterns = [
    /195348/i,
    /250f5c/i,
    /\bcall\s+(1533|2034|3315|3336)\b/,
    /\bcall_indirect\b/
];

function add(index, reason) {
    if (!selected.has(index)) selected.set(index, new Set());
    selected.get(index).add(reason);
}

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const text = String(instructions[i].text);

    const matched = patterns.filter(pattern => pattern.test(text));
    const direct = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);
    const indirect = /\bcall_indirect\b/.test(text);

    if (direct) {
        calls.push(`${hex(instructions[i].start)}  call ${direct[1]}`);
    } else if (indirect) {
        calls.push(`${hex(instructions[i].start)}  ${text}`);
    }

    if (matched.length > 0) {
        for (const pattern of matched) add(i, `MATCH ${pattern}`);

        for (
            let j = Math.max(0, i - 8);
            j <= Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 8);
            j++
        ) {
            add(j, 'CONTEXT');
        }
    }
}

const lines = [];
const out = value => lines.push(value ?? '');

out('ROOSTER WASM — FUNCTION 7006');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`FUNCTION: ${target}`);
out(`BODY START: ${hex(body.start)}`);
out(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${instructions.length}`);
out('====================================================');
out('TARGETED INSTRUCTIONS WITH CONTEXT');
out('====================================================');

let previous = -2;

for (const [index, reasons] of [...selected.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {
    if (index > previous + 1) out('...');

    const ins = instructions[index];
    const labels = [...reasons]
        .filter(reason => reason !== 'CONTEXT')
        .join(', ');

    out(
        `${labels ? '[' + labels + ']' : '[context]'} ` +
        `${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw}  ${ins.text}`
    );

    previous = index;
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('ALL CALLS IN FUNCTION 7006');
out('====================================================');
for (const call of calls) out(call);

out('');
out('Inspection complete. No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log('FUNCTION 7006 INSPECTION COMPLETE');
console.log('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log(`Instruction count: ${instructions.length}`);
console.log(`Selected lines: ${selected.size}`);
console.log(`Calls found: ${calls.length}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc7006.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 7006;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
  return (
    "0x" +
    (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0")
  );
}

function readMemArg(pos: number) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections: {
  id: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  sections.push({
    id,
    start: p,
    end: p + size.value,
  });

  p += size.value;
}

const importSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION/CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const moduleLen = readULEB(p);
    p = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

    const nameLen = readULEB(p);
    p = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      importedCount++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p++;

      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else if (kind === 0x04) {
      p++;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.start;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND TARGET BODY
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

let targetBodyStart = -1;
let targetBodyEnd = -1;
let targetCodeStart = -1;
let targetOrdinal = -1;
let targetType = -1;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

  const localDeclCount = readULEB(p);
  p = localDeclCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount.value; i++) {
    const num = readULEB(p);
    p = num.next;

    p++;
  }

  const codeStart = p;

  const globalFunc = importedCount + ordinal;

  if (globalFunc === TARGET_FUNC) {
    targetBodyStart = bodyStart;
    targetBodyEnd = bodyEnd;
    targetCodeStart = codeStart;
    targetOrdinal = ordinal;
    targetType = functionTypes[ordinal];
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (targetBodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// RAW BYTE + SIMPLE DISASSEMBLY
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RAW BODY`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${targetOrdinal}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${targetType}`);
console.log(
  `BODY = ${hex(targetBodyStart)} .. ${hex(targetBodyEnd)}`
);
console.log(
  `SIZE = ${targetBodyEnd - targetBodyStart}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = targetCodeStart;

while (q < targetBodyEnd) {
  const addr = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];

  let text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02: {
      const r = readSLEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `block type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const r = readSLEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `loop type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: {
      const r = readSLEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `if type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      text = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `call func=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const m = readMemArg(q);
      q = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const m = readMemArg(q);
      q = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const m = readMemArg(q);
      q = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const m = readMemArg(q);
      q = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(q);
      q = r.next;
      text = `i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      text = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      text = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      text = "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      text = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      text = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      text = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      text = "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      text = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      text = "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      text = "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      text = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      text = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      text = "i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      text = "i32.shl";
      break;

    case 0x75:
      text = "i32.shr_s";
      break;

    case 0x76:
      text = "i32.shr_u";
      break;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(addr)}  ${text}`);
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempPath = path.join(dir, '__inspectFunc7006Full_decoder_temp.mjs');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'inspectFunc7006Full_output.txt');

const source = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = source.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempPath,
        source.slice(0, cut) +
            '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + '?inspectFunc7006Full'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;
const target = 7006;

if (!bodies.has(target)) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${target} has no defined body`);
}

const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(target);
const lines = [];

const out = value => lines.push(value ?? '');

out('ROOSTER WASM — FUNCTION 7006 FULL BODY');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`FUNCTION: ${target}`);
out(`BODY START: ${hex(body.start)}`);
out(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${instructions.length}`);
out('====================================================');

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];
    const text = String(ins.text);

    let tag = '    ';

    if (/call\s+1533\b/.test(text)) tag = '[ALLOC]';
    else if (/call\s+215\b/.test(text)) tag = '[CLEANUP?]';
    else if (/call\s+270\b/.test(text)) tag = '[CALL270]';
    else if (/br_if|br\b/.test(text)) tag = '[BRANCH]';
    else if (/250f48|250f58|250f5c|195348/i.test(text)) tag = '[STATE]';

    out(`${tag.padEnd(12)} ${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw}  ${text}`);
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('CALL SUMMARY');
out('====================================================');

for (const ins of instructions) {
    const text = String(ins.text);
    const match = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);

    if (match) out(`${hex(ins.start)}  call ${match[1]}`);
}

out('');
out('No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log('Full function inspection complete.');
console.log(`Instruction count: ${instructions.length}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc725Raw.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 725;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** shift;
  return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

// Find Code Section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

let target = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(q);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    target = {
      start,
      end: end - 1,
      size: sz.value
    };
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (!target) throw new Error("FUNC725 not found");

console.log("FUNC 725 EXACT RAW + BASIC DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${target.size}`);
console.log(
  `BODY      : 0x${target.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
  `0x${target.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW BODY");

for (let x = target.start; x <= target.end; x += 16) {
  const e = Math.min(x + 15, target.end);
  console.log(
    `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
    [...b.slice(x, e + 1)]
      .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("I32.CONST / CALL / CALL_INDIRECT / MEMORY OPS");

let x = target.start;

while (x <= target.end) {
  const at = x;
  const op = b[x++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(x);
    console.log(
      `I32.CONST @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `VALUE=${r.value} HEX=0x${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
    );
    x = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(x);
    console.log(
      `CALL @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} FUNC=${r.value}`
    );
    x = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const r1 = uleb(x);
    const r2 = uleb(r1.next);
    console.log(
      `CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `TYPE=${r1.value} TABLE=${r2.value}`
    );
    x = r2.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const a = uleb(x);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    const names = {
      0x28: "I32.LOAD",
      0x29: "I64.LOAD",
      0x36: "I32.STORE",
      0x37: "I64.STORE",
      0x38: "F32.STORE",
      0x39: "F64.STORE",
      0x3a: "I32.STORE8"
    };

    console.log(
      `${names[op] || "MEMORY"} @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    );

    x = o.next;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc787.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const buf = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_GLOBAL = 787;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const ORDINAL = TARGET_GLOBAL - IMPORTED;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
}

const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!codeSec)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let c = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(c);
c = count.next;

let target: {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    instrStart: number;
} | null = null;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {

    const bodySize = readULEB(c);
    c = bodySize.next;

    const start = c;
    const end = c + bodySize.value;

    let x = start;

    const localGroups = readULEB(x);
    x = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;

        x++; // value type
    }

    if (ordinal === ORDINAL) {
        target = {
            start,
            end,
            instrStart: x
        };
        break;
    }

    c = end;
}

if (!target)
    throw new Error("TARGET FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 787 RAW BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ORDINAL}`);
console.log(`BODY = ${hex(target.start)} .. ${hex(target.end)}`);
console.log(`INSTR = ${hex(target.instrStart)}`);
console.log(`SIZE = ${target.end - target.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (
    let x = target.start;
    x < target.end;
    x += 16
) {
    const end = Math.min(x + 16, target.end);

    let line = `${hex(x)}  `;

    for (let i = x; i < end; i++) {
        line += buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(line);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc810.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const TARGET = 810;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

let p = 8;
let funcSec = null;
let codeSec = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 3)
    funcSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

const ordinal = TARGET - IMPORTED;

let pFunc = funcSec.start;
const fc = uleb(pFunc);
pFunc = fc.next;

let typeIndex = null;

for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {
  const x = uleb(pFunc);
  pFunc = x.next;

  if (i === ordinal)
    typeIndex = x.value;
}

let pCode = codeSec.start;
const cc = uleb(pCode);
pCode = cc.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(pCode);
  const size = z.value;
  pCode = z.next;

  const start = pCode;
  const end = pCode + size;

  if (i === ordinal) {
    body = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  pCode = end;
}

function decode(p) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  const R = (next, s) => ({
    next,
    text:
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${s}`
  });

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return R(p, "UNREACHABLE");
    case 0x01: return R(p, "NOP");
    case 0x0b: return R(p, "END");
    case 0x0f: return R(p, "RETURN");
    case 0x1a: return R(p, "DROP");
    case 0x1b: return R(p, "SELECT");

    case 0x02:
      return R(p + 1, `BLOCK type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x03:
      return R(p + 1, `LOOP type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x04:
      return R(p + 1, `IF type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x05:
      return R(p, "ELSE");

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR_IF ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;

      const tb = uleb(p);
      p = tb.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `I64.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);

      return R(
        x.next,
        `I32.CONST ${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
      );
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `I64.CONST ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x45: return R(p, "I32.EQZ");
    case 0x46: return R(p, "I32.EQ");
    case 0x47: return R(p, "I32.NE");
    case 0x48: return R(p, "I32.LT_S");
    case 0x49: return R(p, "I32.LT_U");
    case 0x4a: return R(p, "I32.GT_S");
    case 0x4b: return R(p, "I32.GT_U");
    case 0x4c: return R(p, "I32.LE_S");

    case 0x6a: return R(p, "I32.ADD");
    case 0x6b: return R(p, "I32.SUB");
    case 0x71: return R(p, "I32.AND");
    case 0x72: return R(p, "I32.OR");
    case 0x73: return R(p, "I32.XOR");
    case 0x74: return R(p, "I32.SHL");
    case 0x75: return R(p, "I32.SHR_S");
    case 0x76: return R(p, "I32.SHR_U");

    default:
      return R(
        p,
        `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 810 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${typeIndex}`);
console.log(
  `BODY           : 0x${body.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(`SIZE           : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = body.start;

while (q < body.end) {
  const x = decode(q);
  console.log(x.text);
  q = x.next;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc813_1079.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGETS = [813, 1079];
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readI32Const(pos: number): {
  value: number;
  next: number;
} {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
  align: number;
  offset: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

function readString(pos: number): {
  value: string;
  next: number;
} {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const end = r.next + r.value;

  return {
    value: wasm.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections: {
  id: number;
  payloadStart: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const end = p + size.value;

  sections.push({
    id,
    payloadStart,
    end,
  });

  p = end;
}

const importSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION or CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = readString(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    switch (kind) {
      case 0x00: {
        const type = readULEB(p);
        p = type.next;
        importedCount++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x01: {
        p++;

        const flags = wasm[p++];

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x02: {
        const flags = wasm[p++];

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x03:
        p += 2;
        break;

      case 0x04:
        p++;
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        break;

      default:
        throw new Error(
          `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`
        );
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.payloadStart;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Body {
  globalFunc: number;
  ordinal: number;
  typeIndex: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

p = codeSec.payloadStart;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

    const localDecls = readULEB(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    bodies.push({
      globalFunc: importedCount + ordinal,
      ordinal,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[ordinal],
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd: bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND TARGETS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const targetFunc of TARGETS) {
  const body = bodies.find(
    (b) => b.globalFunc === targetFunc
  );

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${targetFunc} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${targetFunc} RAW BODY`);
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${body.globalFunc}`);
  console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${body.ordinal}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${body.typeIndex}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY        = ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `SIZE        = ${body.bodyEnd - body.bodyStart}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let q = body.codeStart;

  while (q < body.codeEnd) {
    const at = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let line = `${hex(at)}  `;

    switch (op) {
      case 0x00:
        line += "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        line += "nop";
        break;

      case 0x02: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `block type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `loop type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `if type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        line += "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        line += "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const count = readULEB(q);
        q = count.next;

        const labels: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
          const r = readULEB(q);
          q = r.next;
          labels.push(r.value);
        }

        const def = readULEB(q);
        q = def.next;

        line +=
          `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        line += "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `call func=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const type = readULEB(q);
        q = type.next;

        const table = readULEB(q);
        q = table.next;

        line +=
          `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        line += "drop";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `local.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        line += `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line +=
          `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line +=
          `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x2c: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line +=
          `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x2d: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line +=
          `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line +=
          `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x3a: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        line +=
          `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readI32Const(q);
        q = r.next;

        line += `i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        line += "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        line += "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        line += "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        line += "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        line += "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        line += "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        line += "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        line += "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        line += "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        line += "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        line += "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        line += "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        line += "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        line += "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        line += "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        line += "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        line += "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        line += "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        line += "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        line += "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      default:
        line += `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
        break;
    }

    console.log(line);
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("END FUNC");
}

console.log();

console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("TARGET FUNCTIONS = 813, 1079");

import fs from "fs";

const buf = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_GLOBAL = 83;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
}

const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 83 INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED COUNT     = ${IMPORTED}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (!codeSec) {
    console.log("FUNC 83 IS IMPORTED / NOT IN CODE SECTION");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
    console.log("============================================================");
    process.exit(0);
}

let c = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(c);
c = count.next;

let targetStart = -1;
let targetEnd = -1;
let targetOrdinal = -1;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const bs = readULEB(c);
    c = bs.next;

    const start = c;
    const end = c + bs.value;

    const global = ordinal + IMPORTED;

    if (global === TARGET_GLOBAL) {
        targetOrdinal = ordinal;
        targetStart = start;
        targetEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    c = end;
}

if (targetStart < 0) {
    console.log("FUNC 83 IS IMPORTED / NO DEFINED BODY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
    console.log("============================================================");
    process.exit(0);
}

console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${targetOrdinal}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`);
console.log(`BODY          = ${hex(targetStart)} .. ${hex(targetEnd)}`);
console.log(`SIZE          = ${targetEnd - targetStart}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let pos = targetStart; pos < targetEnd; pos += 16) {
    const end = Math.min(pos + 16, targetEnd);

    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let i = pos; i < end; i++) {
        bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(`${hex(pos)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
}

console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const scannerPath = path.join(here, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempDecoderPath = path.join(here, '__inspectFunc83Targets_decoder_temp.mjs');
const outputPath = path.join(here, 'inspectFunc83Targets_output.txt');

const scannerSource = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = scannerSource.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');
}

const decoderBootstrap = scannerSource.slice(0, cut);
let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempDecoderPath,
        decoderBootstrap +
            '\nexport { wasm, bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempDecoderPath).href + '?inspectFunc83Targets'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempDecoderPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;
const target = 83;

if (!bodies.has(target)) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${target} has no defined body`);
}

const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(target);

const targetAddresses = [
    0x195348,
    0x195354,
    0x250f30,
    0x250f5c,
    0x25587c,
    0x255880,
    0x255884,
    0x255888,
    0x255890,
    0x255894,
    0x25589c,
    0x2558a4,
    0x2559ac
];

const targetDecimals = new Set(targetAddresses.map(x => String(x)));
const selected = new Map();
const counts = {
    stores: 0,
    directCalls: 0,
    indirectCalls: 0,
    targetAddressHits: 0
};

function addReason(index, reason) {
    if (!selected.has(index)) selected.set(index, new Set());
    selected.get(index).add(reason);
}

function isTargetAddress(text) {
    const lower = text.toLowerCase();

    if (targetAddresses.some(addr =>
        lower.includes('0x' + addr.toString(16))
    )) {
        return true;
    }

    const numbers = text.match(/\b\d+\b/g) ?? [];
    return numbers.some(n => targetDecimals.has(n));
}

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const text = String(instructions[i].text);
    const lower = text.toLowerCase();

    const isStore = /\b(?:i32|i64|f32|f64)\.store(?:8|16|32)?\b/.test(lower);
    const isDirectCall = /\bcall\s+\d+\b/.test(text);
    const isIndirectCall = /\bcall_indirect\b/.test(text);
    const addressHit = isTargetAddress(text);

    if (isStore) counts.stores++;
    if (isDirectCall) counts.directCalls++;
    if (isIndirectCall) counts.indirectCalls++;
    if (addressHit) counts.targetAddressHits++;

    const reasons = [];
    if (isStore) reasons.push('STORE');
    if (isDirectCall) reasons.push('CALL');
    if (isIndirectCall) reasons.push('CALL_INDIRECT');
    if (addressHit) reasons.push('TARGET_ADDRESS');

    if (reasons.length) {
        for (const reason of reasons) addReason(i, reason);

        // Include surrounding instructions to retain local dataflow context.
        for (let j = Math.max(0, i - 3);
             j <= Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 3);
             j++) {
            addReason(j, 'CONTEXT');
        }
    }
}

const lines = [];

function out(s = '') {
    lines.push(s);
}

out('ROOSTER WASM — FUNCTION 83 TARGETED INSPECTION');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`FUNCTION: ${target}`);
out(`BODY START: ${hex(body.start)}`);
out(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${instructions.length}`);
out(`STORE INSTRUCTIONS: ${counts.stores}`);
out(`DIRECT CALL INSTRUCTIONS: ${counts.directCalls}`);
out(`INDIRECT CALL INSTRUCTIONS: ${counts.indirectCalls}`);
out(`TARGET-ADDRESS INSTRUCTIONS: ${counts.targetAddressHits}`);
out('====================================================');
out('TARGET ADDRESSES:');
for (const addr of targetAddresses) {
    out(`  0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(6, '0')} (${addr})`);
}
out('====================================================');
out('FILTERED INSTRUCTIONS WITH LOCAL CONTEXT');
out('====================================================');

let previous = -2;

for (const [index, reasons] of [...selected.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {
    if (index > previous + 1) out('...');

    const ins = instructions[index];
    const tags = [...reasons].filter(x => x !== 'CONTEXT').join(',');
    const label = tags ? `[${tags}]` : '[context]';

    out(
        `${label.padEnd(25)} ${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw}  ${ins.text}`
    );

    previous = index;
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('DIRECT CALL TARGET SUMMARY');
out('====================================================');

for (const ins of instructions) {
    const text = String(ins.text);
    const match = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);

    if (match) {
        out(`${hex(ins.start)}  call ${match[1]}`);
    }
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('INDIRECT CALL SUMMARY');
out('====================================================');

for (const ins of instructions) {
    if (/\bcall_indirect\b/.test(String(ins.text))) {
        out(`${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}`);
    }
}

out('');
out('Inspection complete. No WASM execution performed.');

console.log(`Stores       : ${counts.stores}`);
console.log(`Direct calls : ${counts.directCalls}`);
console.log(`Indirect     : ${counts.indirectCalls}`);
console.log(`Address hits : ${counts.targetAddressHits}`);
console.log(`Output       : ${outputPath}`);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT FUNC 84 / 85");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let pos = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = pos;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payload;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

let p = codeStart;

const definedCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedCount}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL RANGE = 83 .. ${83 + definedCount - 1}`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedCount; ordinal++) {

    const globalFunc = 83 + ordinal;

    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (globalFunc !== 84 && globalFunc !== 85) {
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${globalFunc}`);
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${globalFunc}`);
    console.log(`BODY          = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE          = ${bodySize}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let x = bodyStart;

    const localInfo = readULEB(x);
    const localGroups = localInfo.value;
    x = localInfo.next;

    console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS  = ${localGroups}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
        const countInfo2 = readULEB(x);
        const count = countInfo2.value;
        x = countInfo2.next;

        const type = wasm[x++];

        console.log(`  group ${i}: count=${count} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("RAW BODY");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let q = bodyStart; q < bodyEnd; q += 16) {

        const end = Math.min(q + 16, bodyEnd);

        let bytes = "";

        for (let r = q; r < end; r++) {
            bytes += wasm[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(`${hex(q)}  ${bytes}`);
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("DIRECT CALLS TO FUNC 91");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    x = bodyStart;

    // Skip local declarations again
    const localInfo2 = readULEB(x);
    const localGroups2 = localInfo2.value;
    x = localInfo2.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups2; i++) {
        const c = readULEB(x);
        x = c.next;
        x++;
    }

    while (x < bodyEnd) {

        const opOffset = x;
        const op = wasm[x++];

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const targetInfo = readULEB(x);
            x = targetInfo.next;

            const target = targetInfo.value;

            if (target === 91) {

                console.log("");
                console.log(`CALL FUNC 91 @ ${hex(opOffset)}`);

                const from = Math.max(bodyStart, opOffset - 48);
                const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, opOffset + 48);

                console.log(
                    `CONTEXT ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
                );

                for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

                    const end = Math.min(q + 16, to);

                    let bytes = "";

                    for (let r = q; r < end; r++) {
                        bytes += wasm[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
                    }

                    console.log(`${hex(q)}  ${bytes}`);
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            const a = readULEB(x);
            x = a.next;

            const b = readULEB(x);
            x = b.next;

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            x++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            while (true) {
                const b = wasm[x++];
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x43) {
            x += 4;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x44) {
            x += 8;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const countInfo3 = readULEB(x);
            const count = countInfo3.value;
            x = countInfo3.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const r = readULEB(x);
                x = r.next;
            }

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const sub = readULEB(x);
            x = sub.next;

            if (
                sub.value === 8 ||
                sub.value === 9 ||
                sub.value === 10 ||
                sub.value === 11
            ) {
                const a = readULEB(x);
                x = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(x);
                x = b.next;
            }

            continue;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc87.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 87;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readVec(pos: number) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  let p = n.next;
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    out.push(x.value);
    p = x.next;
  }
  return { values: out, next: p };
}

// Find sections
let p = 8;
let typeSec: { start: number; end: number } | null = null;
let funcSec: { start: number; end: number } | null = null;
let codeSec: { start: number; end: number } | null = null;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 1) typeSec = { start, end };
  if (id === 3) funcSec = { start, end };
  if (id === 10) codeSec = { start, end };

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 87 - EXACT BODY / TYPE INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

if (!funcSec || !codeSec || !typeSec) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section: defined function -> type index
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let fp = funcSec.start;
const funcCount = uleb(fp);
fp = funcCount.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount.value; i++) {
  const x = uleb(fp);
  typeIndices.push(x.value);
  fp = x.next;
}

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const definedIndex = TARGET - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

console.log(`TARGET FUNC        : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS}`);
console.log(`DEFINED INDEX      : ${definedIndex}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX         : ${typeIndices[definedIndex]}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Type section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let tp = typeSec.start;
const typeCount = uleb(tp);
tp = typeCount.next;

const types: any[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
  const form = wasm[tp++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    types.push({ form });
    continue;
  }

  const params = readVec(tp);
  tp = params.next;

  const results = readVec(tp);
  tp = results.next;

  types.push({
    form,
    params: params.values,
    results: results.values
  });
}

const ti = typeIndices[definedIndex];
const t = types[ti];

function vt(x: number) {
  switch (x) {
    case 0x7f: return "i32";
    case 0x7e: return "i64";
    case 0x7d: return "f32";
    case 0x7c: return "f64";
    case 0x70: return "funcref";
    case 0x6f: return "externref";
    default: return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TYPE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (t?.params) {
  console.log(
    `PARAMS  : ${t.params.map(vt).join(", ") || "(none)"}`
  );
  console.log(
    `RESULTS : ${t.results.map(vt).join(", ") || "(none)"}`
  );
} else {
  console.log("TYPE COULD NOT BE DECODED");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let cp = codeSec.start;
const bodyCount = uleb(cp);
cp = bodyCount.next;

let bodyStart = 0;
let bodyEnd = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {
  const bs = uleb(cp);
  cp = bs.next;

  const s = cp;
  const e = cp + bs.value;

  const globalFunc = IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i;

  if (globalFunc === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = s;
    bodyEnd = e;
    break;
  }

  cp = e;
}

console.log("");
console.log("BODY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`START : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`END   : 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`SIZE  : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locals + raw instructions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (bodyStart) {
  let q = bodyStart;

  const localGroups = uleb(q);
  q = localGroups.next;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS : ${localGroups.value}`);

  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const count = uleb(q);
    q = count.next;

    const type = wasm[q++];

    console.log(
      `  GROUP ${i}: count=${count.value} type=${vt(type)}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("INSTRUCTION BYTES");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let shown = 0;

  while (q < bodyEnd && shown < 300) {
    const op = wasm[q];

    console.log(
      `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );

    q++;
    shown++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [87, 122];

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readBytes(pos: number, n: number) {
  return [...buf.subarray(pos, pos + n)];
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 87 / FUNC 122 TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGETS   :", TARGETS.join(", "));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;

let typeSection: {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  count: number;
} | null = null;

let importFuncCount = 0;
let functionTypes: number[] = [];

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionStart = p;
  const id = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = p + size;

  if (id === 1) {
    const countInfo = uleb(p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    typeSection = {
      start: payloadStart,
      end: payloadEnd,
      count,
    };

    console.log("");
    console.log("TYPE SECTION");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("START :", hex(payloadStart));
    console.log("END   :", hex(payloadEnd));
    console.log("COUNT :", count);
  }

  if (id === 2) {
    let q = p;

    const countInfo = uleb(q);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const moduleLen = uleb(q);
      q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

      const nameLen = uleb(q);
      q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

      const kind = buf[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        const typeInfo = uleb(q);
        q = typeInfo.next;
        importFuncCount++;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        q++; // elemtype
        const flags = buf[q++];
        if (flags & 1) q = uleb(q).next;
        q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        q++;
        const flags = buf[q++];
        q = uleb(q).next;
        if (flags & 1) q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 3) {
    let q = p;

    const countInfo = uleb(q);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    functionTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const t = uleb(q);
      q = t.next;
      functionTypes.push(t.value);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTION SECTION");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", count);
    console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS:", importFuncCount);
    console.log("TOTAL FUNCTION IDX :", importFuncCount + count);
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!typeSection) {
  console.log("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("FUNCTION TYPE INDICES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const func of TARGETS) {
  const codeIndex = func - importFuncCount;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log("CODE INDEX :", codeIndex);

  if (codeIndex < 0 || codeIndex >= functionTypes.length) {
    console.log("TYPE INDEX : OUT OF RANGE");
    continue;
  }

  const typeIndex = functionTypes[codeIndex];

  console.log("TYPE INDEX :", typeIndex);

  // Re-scan type section to locate the target type.
  let q = typeSection.start;
  const countInfo = uleb(q);
  q = countInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {
    const typeStart = q;
    const form = buf[q++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      console.log("Unexpected type form at", hex(typeStart));
      break;
    }

    const paramInfo = uleb(q);
    const paramCount = paramInfo.value;
    q = paramInfo.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++)
      params.push(buf[q++]);

    const resultInfo = uleb(q);
    const resultCount = resultInfo.value;
    q = resultInfo.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++)
      results.push(buf[q++]);

    if (i === typeIndex) {
      const typeName = (v: number) => {
        switch (v) {
          case 0x7f: return "i32";
          case 0x7e: return "i64";
          case 0x7d: return "f32";
          case 0x7c: return "f64";
          case 0x70: return "funcref";
          case 0x6f: return "externref";
          default: return hex(v);
        }
      };

      console.log("PARAM COUNT :", params.length);
      console.log(
        "PARAMS      :",
        params.map(typeName).join(", ") || "(none)"
      );

      console.log("RESULT COUNT:", results.length);
      console.log(
        "RESULTS     :",
        results.map(typeName).join(", ") || "(none)"
      );

      console.log(
        "SIGNATURE   :",
        `(${params.map(typeName).join(", ")}) -> (${results.map(typeName).join(", ")})`
      );

      console.log(
        "TYPE RAW    :",
        readBytes(typeStart, q - typeStart)
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(decodeURIComponent(new URL(import.meta.url).pathname).replace(/^\/([A-Za-z]:)/, '$1'));
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempDecoderPath = path.join(
    dir,
    '__traceFunc91_decoder_temp.mjs'
);

const scannerSource = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker =
    "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

const cut = scannerSource.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');
}

const decoderBootstrap = scannerSource.slice(0, cut);

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempDecoderPath,
        decoderBootstrap +
            '\nexport { wasm, bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempDecoderPath).href +
        '?inspectFunc91'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempDecoderPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;
const target = 91;

if (!bodies.has(target)) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${target} has no defined body`);
}

const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(target);

console.log('ROOSTER WASM — FUNCTION 91');
console.log('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log('====================================================');
console.log(`FUNCTION: ${target}`);
console.log(`BODY START: ${hex(body.start)}`);
console.log(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${instructions.length}`);
console.log('====================================================');

const interestingCalls = new Set([91, 104, 813, 1079, 1533, 2034]);

for (const ins of instructions) {
    const text = String(ins.text);
    const callMatch = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);
    const callTarget = callMatch ? Number(callMatch[1]) : null;

    const addressHit =
        /0x0*(?:195348|250f30|250f5c)\b/i.test(text) ||
        /\b(?:1659720|2428720|2428764)\b/.test(text);

    const importantCall =
        callTarget !== null && interestingCalls.has(callTarget);

    const prefix =
        addressHit ? '>>> ' :
        importantCall ? '*** ' :
        '    ';

    console.log(`${prefix}${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw}  ${text}`);
}

console.log('\n====================================================');
console.log('TARGETED CALL SUMMARY');
console.log('====================================================');

for (const ins of instructions) {
    const text = String(ins.text);
    const callMatch = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);

    if (callMatch && interestingCalls.has(Number(callMatch[1]))) {
        console.log(`${hex(ins.start)}  ${text}`);
    }
}

console.log('\nInspection complete. No WASM execution performed.');

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 91 INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

let functionEnd = -1;

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = pos;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (sectionId === 1) {
        typeStart = payload;
        typeEnd = end;
    }

    if (sectionId === 3) {
        functionStart = payload;
        functionEnd = end;
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payload;
        codeEnd = end;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (functionStart < 0) throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");
if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

    if (form !== 0x60) {

    p = resultInfo.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
        results.push(wasm[p++]);
