    const end = Math.min(q + 16, bodyEnd);

    let bytes = "";

    for (let r = q; r < end; r++) {
        bytes += wasm[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(`${hex(q)}  ${bytes}`);
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CALL / CALL_INDIRECT / MEMORY OPERATIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let x = bodyStart;

const localInfo = readULEB(x);
const localGroups = localInfo.value;
x = localInfo.next;

console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS = ${localGroups}`);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {

    const countInfo = readULEB(x);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    x = countInfo.next;

    const type = wasm[x++];

    console.log(
        `  local group ${i}: count=${count} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
}

while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const opOffset = x;
    const op = wasm[x++];

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const info = readULEB(x);
        x = info.next;

        console.log(
            `CALL @ ${hex(opOffset)} -> FUNC ${info.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {

        const info = readULEB(x);
        x = info.next;

        console.log(
            `CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(opOffset)} -> TYPE ${info.value}`
        );

        x++;

        continue;
    }

    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24
    ) {
        const info = readULEB(x);
        x = info.next;

        console.log(
            `LOCAL/GLOBAL op=0x${op.toString(16)} @ ${hex(opOffset)} index=${info.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

        const a = readULEB(x);
        x = a.next;

        const b = readULEB(x);
        x = b.next;

        console.log(
            `MEMORY op=0x${op.toString(16)} @ ${hex(opOffset)}` +
            ` align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const info = readULEB(x);
        x = info.next;

        console.log(
            `I32.CONST @ ${hex(opOffset)} value=${info.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {

        while (true) {
            const b = wasm[x++];

            if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        }

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x43) {
        x += 4;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x44) {
        x += 8;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

        const info = readULEB(x);
        x = info.next;

        console.log(
            `BRANCH op=0x${op.toString(16)} @ ${hex(opOffset)} depth=${info.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {

        const countInfo = readULEB(x);
        const count = countInfo.value;
        x = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
        }

        const def = readULEB(x);
        x = def.next;

        console.log(
            `BR_TABLE @ ${hex(opOffset)} count=${count} default=${def.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {

        const sub = readULEB(x);
        x = sub.next;

        console.log(
            `PREFIX FC @ ${hex(opOffset)} sub=${sub.value}`
        );

        if (
            sub.value === 8 ||
            sub.value === 9 ||
            sub.value === 10 ||
            sub.value === 11
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(x);
            x = a.next;

            const b = readULEB(x);
            x = b.next;
        }

        continue;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");

console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc9106Structure.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 9106;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 9106 EXACT BODY / STRUCTURE ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${IMPORTED}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function readName(pos: number) {
  const n = u32leb(pos);
  const start = n.next;
  const end = start + n.value;

  return {
    name: buf.subarray(start, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readSectionHeader(pos: number) {
  const id = buf[pos];
  const size = u32leb(pos + 1);

  return {
    id,
    size: size.value,
    payloadStart: size.next,
    payloadEnd: size.next + size.value,
  };
}

console.log("SCANNING WASM SECTIONS...");

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;
let functionSectionStart = -1;
let functionSectionEnd = -1;

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sec = readSectionHeader(p);

  if (sec.id === 3) {
    functionSectionStart = sec.payloadStart;
    functionSectionEnd = sec.payloadEnd;
  }

  if (sec.id === 10) {
    codeStart = sec.payloadStart;
    codeEnd = sec.payloadEnd;
  }

  p = sec.payloadEnd;
}

if (codeStart < 0 || functionSectionStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Function section or code section not found");
}

console.log(`FUNCTION SECTION = ${hex(functionSectionStart)} .. ${hex(functionSectionEnd)}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION     = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

//
// Function section:
// vector of type indices for defined functions.
// Global function N maps to code body ordinal N - IMPORTED.
//

let fp = functionSectionStart;
const fnCount = u32leb(fp);
fp = fnCount.next;

const definedCount = fnCount.value;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT = ${definedCount}`);

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
  const r = u32leb(fp);
  fp = r.next;
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
}

const codeOrdinal = TARGET_FUNC - IMPORTED;

if (codeOrdinal < 0 || codeOrdinal >= definedCount) {
  throw new Error(
    `Invalid code ordinal ${codeOrdinal}; defined count=${definedCount}`
  );
}

console.log(`TARGET CODE ORDINAL = ${codeOrdinal}`);
console.log(`TARGET TYPE INDEX   = ${typeIndices[codeOrdinal]}`);

//
// Code section:
// vector of function bodies.
// Each body = size + body bytes.
//

let cp = codeStart;

const bodyCount = u32leb(cp);
cp = bodyCount.next;

console.log(`CODE BODY COUNT = ${bodyCount.value}`);

if (bodyCount.value !== definedCount) {
  console.log(
    `WARNING: code/function count mismatch: ${bodyCount.value} vs ${definedCount}`
  );
}

let targetBodyStart = -1;
let targetBodyEnd = -1;
let targetBodySize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = u32leb(cp);
  const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

  if (i === codeOrdinal) {
    targetBodyStart = bodyStart;
    targetBodyEnd = bodyEnd;
    targetBodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    break;
  }

  cp = bodyEnd;
}

if (targetBodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Target body not found");
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`BODY START = ${hex(targetBodyStart)}`);
console.log(`BODY END   = ${hex(targetBodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE  = ${targetBodySize}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

//
// Parse local declarations.
//

let q = targetBodyStart;

const localDecl = u32leb(q);
q = localDecl.next;

console.log(`LOCAL DECL GROUPS = ${localDecl.value}`);

let totalLocals = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
  const count = u32leb(q);
  q = count.next;

  const type = buf[q++];

  totalLocals += count.value;

  console.log(
    `  GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${count.value} TYPE=0x${type.toString(16)}`
  );
}

console.log(`TOTAL EXTRA LOCALS = ${totalLocals}`);
console.log(`INSTRUCTION START  = ${hex(q)}`);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

const FIELD_OFFSETS = new Set([
  0x04,
  0x0c,
  0x14,
  0x18,
  0x1c,
  0x24,
  0x2c,
  0x30,
  0x34,
  0x38,
]);

function fieldTag(offset: number) {
  if (FIELD_OFFSETS.has(offset)) {
    return `   <<< STRUCT FIELD +${offset.toString(16)}`;
  }
  return "";
}

function print(pos: number, bytes: string, text: string) {
  console.log(`${hex(pos)}  ${bytes.padEnd(18)} ${text}`);
}

let instr = q;

while (instr < targetBodyEnd) {
  const pos = instr;
  const op = buf[instr++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `20 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.GET ${r.value}${r.value === 3 ? "   <<< STRUCT BASE" : ""}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `21 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.SET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `22 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.TEE ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // global.get
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `23 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // global.set
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `24 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = s32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `41 ${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16)}`,
      `I32.CONST ${r.value < 0 ? r.value : hex(r.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = u32leb(instr);
    instr = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(instr);
    instr = o.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `28 ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}${fieldTag(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load8_u
  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const a = u32leb(instr);
    instr = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(instr);
    instr = o.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `2d ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD8_U align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.load16_u
  if (op === 0x2f) {
    const a = u32leb(instr);
    instr = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(instr);
    instr = o.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `2f ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD16_U align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = u32leb(instr);
    instr = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(instr);
    instr = o.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `36 ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}${fieldTag(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.store8
  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = u32leb(instr);
    instr = a.next;

    const o = u32leb(instr);
    instr = o.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `3a ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE8 align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    print(pos, "6a", "I32.ADD");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    print(pos, "6b", "I32.SUB");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.and
  if (op === 0x71) {
    print(pos, "71", "I32.AND");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.or
  if (op === 0x72) {
    print(pos, "72", "I32.OR");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.xor
  if (op === 0x73) {
    print(pos, "73", "I32.XOR");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.shr_u
  if (op === 0x76) {
    print(pos, "76", "I32.SHR_U");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.eqz
  if (op === 0x45) {
    print(pos, "45", "I32.EQZ");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.eq
  if (op === 0x46) {
    print(pos, "46", "I32.EQ");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.ne
  if (op === 0x47) {
    print(pos, "47", "I32.NE");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.lt_u
  if (op === 0x49) {
    print(pos, "49", "I32.LT_U");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.gt_u
  if (op === 0x4b) {
    print(pos, "4b", "I32.GT_U");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.lt_s
  if (op === 0x48) {
    print(pos, "48", "I32.LT_S");
    continue;
  }

  // i32.gt_s
  if (op === 0x4a) {
    print(pos, "4a", "I32.GT_S");
    continue;
  }

  // block
  if (op === 0x02) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    print(pos, `02 ${type.toString(16)}`, `BLOCK type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  // loop
  if (op === 0x03) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    print(pos, `03 ${type.toString(16)}`, `LOOP type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  // if
  if (op === 0x04) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    print(pos, `04 ${type.toString(16)}`, `IF type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  // else
  if (op === 0x05) {
    print(pos, "05", "ELSE");
    continue;
  }

  // end
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    print(pos, "0b", "END");
    continue;
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `0c ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `0d ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR_IF ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // return
  if (op === 0x0f) {
    print(pos, "0f", "RETURN");
    continue;
  }

  // drop
  if (op === 0x1a) {
    print(pos, "1a", "DROP");
    continue;
  }

  // select
  if (op === 0x1b) {
    print(pos, "1b", "SELECT");
    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(pos, `10 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `CALL FUNC${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = u32leb(instr);
    instr = t.next;

    const table = u32leb(instr);
    instr = table.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `11 ${t.value.toString(16)} ${table.value.toString(16)}`,
      `CALL_INDIRECT TYPE${t.value} TABLE${table.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // memory.size
  if (op === 0x3f) {
    const x = buf[instr++];
    print(pos, `3f ${x.toString(16)}`, "MEMORY.SIZE");
    continue;
  }

  // memory.grow
  if (op === 0x40) {
    const x = buf[instr++];
    print(pos, `40 ${x.toString(16)}`, "MEMORY.GROW");
    continue;
  }

  // extended opcode prefix
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    print(
      pos,
      `fc ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `EXTENDED OPCODE 0xfc ${r.value.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  print(
    pos,
    op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
    `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x00007c36;
const END   = 0x00007c5b;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** shift;
  return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

console.log("FUNC 91 RAW INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : 0x${START.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. 0x${END.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(
  [...b.slice(START, END + 1)]
    .map((x,i) =>
      `0x${(START+i).toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ${x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    )
    .join("\n")
);

console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("I32.CONST / CALL / INDIRECT CANDIDATES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;

while (p <= END) {
  const opPos = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readSLEB(p);
    console.log(
      `I32.CONST @ 0x${opPos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `VALUE=${r.value} HEX=0x${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
    );
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    console.log(
      `CALL @ 0x${opPos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} FUNC=${r.value}`
    );
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const r1 = readULEB(p);
    const r2 = readULEB(r1.next);
    console.log(
      `CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${opPos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `TYPE=${r1.value} TABLE=${r2.value}`
    );
    p = r2.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x29 || op === 0x3a) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    const offset = readULEB(align.next);
    console.log(
      `${op === 0x28 ? "I32.LOAD" : "MEMORY OP"} @ ` +
      `0x${opPos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`
    );
    p = offset.next;
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const ranges = [
  { func: 3628, start: 0x119488, end: 0x11ed78 },
  { func: 3634, start: 0x12472c, end: 0x12e375 },
];

const targets = new Set([0x11aaa1, 0x129f71]);

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  for (;;) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: shift / 7 + 1 };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

for (const r of ranges) {
  console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(64));
  console.log(`FUNC ${r.func}`);
  console.log("=".repeat(64));

  for (let p = r.start; p < r.end; p++) {
    if (b[p] !== 0x41) continue;

    const x = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (x.value !== 5232) continue;

    console.log(`\n5232 @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);

    const s = Math.max(r.start, p - 16);
    const e = Math.min(r.end, p + 32);

    for (let q = s; q < e; q++) {
      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ${b[q].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
      );
    }
  }

  let calls87 = 0;
  let indirect = 0;

  for (let p = r.start; p < r.end; p++) {
    if (b[p] === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p + 1);
      if (x.value === 87) calls87++;
    }

    if (b[p] === 0x11) indirect++;
  }

  console.log(`\nCALL 87 COUNT        : ${calls87}`);
  console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT COUNT  : ${indirect}`);
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x14000;
const MAX_BYTES = 0x3000;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 1020 INSPECTION v27");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid SLEB32");
}

function hex(x: number) {
    return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function raw(pos: number, n = 32) {
    return Array.from(
        wasm.subarray(pos, Math.min(pos + n, wasm.length))
    )
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function mem(
    addr: number,
    name: string,
    p: number
) {
    const a = readULEB(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = readULEB(p);
    p = o.next;

    console.log(
        `${name.padEnd(14)} @ ${hex(addr)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
    );

    return p;
}

const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x1a: "drop",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x41: "i32.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x6d: "i32.div_s",
    0x6e: "i32.div_u",
    0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
    0x70: "i32.rem_u",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    0x77: "i32.rotl",
    0x78: "i32.rotr"
};

let found: number[] = [];

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find call 1020
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (let p = START; p < START + MAX_BYTES; p++) {

    if (wasm[p] !== 0x10)
        continue;

    try {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 1020) {
            found.push(p);
        }
    } catch {
        // ignore
    }
}

console.log("CALL 1020 REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const p of found) {
    console.log(`call 1020 @ ${hex(p)} RAW: ${raw(p, 24)}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log(`Total references found: ${found.length}`);
console.log("");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// NOTE:
//
// We cannot assume function body offset == function index.
// Therefore this script also scans for the characteristic
// function body only after the existing code metadata is found.
// For now print the surrounding caller bytes.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const call of found) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`CALLER CONTEXT @ ${hex(call)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const from = Math.max(START, call - 0x50);
    const to = Math.min(
        wasm.length,
        call + 0x90
    );

    let p = from;

    while (p < to) {

        const addr = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `${names[op].padEnd(14)} @ ${hex(addr)} ` +
                `local=${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = readSLEB32(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `i32.const`.padEnd(14) +
                ` @ ${hex(addr)} value=${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x28) {
            p = mem(addr, "i32.load", p);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x29) {
            p = mem(addr, "i64.load", p);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x2d) {
            p = mem(addr, "i32.load8_u", p);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x2f) {
            p = mem(addr, "i32.load16_u", p);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x36) {
            p = mem(addr, "i32.store", p);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x37) {
            p = mem(addr, "i64.store", p);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `call`.padEnd(14) +
                ` @ ${hex(addr)} function=${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"}`.padEnd(14) +
                ` @ ${hex(addr)} depth=${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {

            const r = readSLEB32(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `${op === 0x02 ? "block" :
                   op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if"}`.padEnd(14) +
                ` @ ${hex(addr)} ` +
                `type=${r.value === 64 ? "empty" : r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x05) {
            console.log(`else`.padEnd(14) + ` @ ${hex(addr)}`);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            console.log(`end`.padEnd(14) + ` @ ${hex(addr)}`);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x1b) {
            console.log(`select`.padEnd(14) + ` @ ${hex(addr)}`);
            continue;
        }

        if (names[op]) {
            console.log(
                `${names[op].padEnd(14)} @ ${hex(addr)}`
            );
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `UNKNOWN`.padEnd(14) +
            ` @ ${hex(addr)} opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search exact known state addresses near function code.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 1020 / STATE ADDRESS REFERENCES");
console.log("============================================================");

const targets = [
    0x255a2c,
    0x255a34,
    0x255a38,
    0x255a54,
    0x255a58,
    0x255a5c,
    0x255a60,
    0x255a68
];

for (const target of targets) {

    const bytes = [
        target & 0xff,
        (target >>> 8) & 0xff,
        (target >>> 16) & 0xff,
        (target >>> 24) & 0xff
    ];

    for (let p = START; p < START + MAX_BYTES - 4; p++) {

        if (
            wasm[p] === bytes[0] &&
            wasm[p + 1] === bytes[1] &&
            wasm[p + 2] === bytes[2] &&
            wasm[p + 3] === bytes[3]
        ) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(target)} reference candidate @ ${hex(p)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");

FILE: scripts\inspectFunction122V25.ts

import fs from "fs";

// ============================================================
// FUNCTION 122 / 259 INSPECTOR v25
// READ ONLY - OFFLINE
//
// Target chain:
//
//   Segment 669
//        ↓
//   Function 656
//        ↓
//   Function 122
//        ↓
//   Function 259
//        ↓
//   Functions 83 / 84 / 85 / 86
//
// NO TON
// NO RPC
// NO WALLET
// NO TRANSACTION
// NO SIGNING
// ============================================================

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTIONS = [
    656,
    122,
    259,
    83,
    84,
    85,
    86,
];

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    off: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 8 && off < buf.length; i++) {
        const b = buf[off++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: off };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: -1, next: off };
}

function readName(
    buf: Buffer,
    off: number
): { value: string; next: number } {
    const n = readULEB(buf, off);

    if (n.value < 0) {
        return {
            value: "",
            next: buf.length,
        };
    }

    const end = n.next + n.value;

    if (end > buf.length) {
        return {
            value: "",
            next: buf.length,
        };
    }

    return {
        value: buf.subarray(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function hex(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
): string {
    return Array.from(
        buf.subarray(start, end)
    )
        .map((b) =>
            b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        )
        .join(" ");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    let off = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (off < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[off++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(
            buf,
            off
        );

        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const payloadStart = off;
        const payloadEnd =
            payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

        if (
            sizeInfo.value < 0 ||
            payloadEnd > buf.length
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid section id=${id}`
            );
        }

        sections.push({
            id,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
        });

        off = payloadEnd;
    }

    return sections;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORTS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function countImportedFunctions(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo =
        readULEB(buf, off);

    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    let importedFunctions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const mod = readName(buf, off);
        off = mod.next;

        const name = readName(buf, off);
        off = name.next;

        const kind = buf[off++];

        switch (kind) {
            // function
            case 0:
                {
                    const type =
                        readULEB(buf, off);

                    off = type.next;
                    importedFunctions++;
                }
                break;

            // table
            case 1:
                {
                    off += 1;

                    const flags = buf[off++];

                    const min =
                        readULEB(buf, off);

                    off = min.next;

                    if (flags & 1) {
                        const max =
                            readULEB(buf, off);

                        off = max.next;
                    }
                }
                break;

            // memory
            case 2:
                {
                    const flags = buf[off++];

                    const min =
                        readULEB(buf, off);

                    off = min.next;

                    if (flags & 1) {
                        const max =
                            readULEB(buf, off);

                        off = max.next;
                    }
                }
                break;

            // global
            case 3:
                off += 2;
                break;

            default:
                throw new Error(
                    `Unknown import kind: ${kind}`
                );
        }
    }

    return importedFunctions;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseFunctionTypes(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo =
        readULEB(buf, off);

    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const types: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const info =
            readULEB(buf, off);

        types.push(info.value);
        off = info.next;
    }

    return types;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseCodeBodies(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo =
        readULEB(buf, off);

    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: {
        bodyIndex: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        codeStart: number;
        bodySize: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (
        let bodyIndex = 0;
        bodyIndex < count;
        bodyIndex++
    ) {
        const sizeInfo =
            readULEB(buf, off);

        const bodySize =
            sizeInfo.value;

        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = off;
        const bodyEnd =
            bodyStart + bodySize;

        let p = bodyStart;

        const localCountInfo =
            readULEB(buf, p);

        p = localCountInfo.next;

        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < localCountInfo.value;
            j++
        ) {
            const n =
                readULEB(buf, p);

            p = n.next;

            // value type
            p += 1;
        }

        bodies.push({
            bodyIndex,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: p,
            bodySize,
        });

        off = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// EXPORTS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseExports(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    } | undefined
) {
    const result = new Map<
        number,
        string[]
    >();

    if (!section) {
        return result;
    }

    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo =
        readULEB(buf, off);

    off = countInfo.next;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < countInfo.value;
        i++
    ) {
        const nameInfo =
            readName(buf, off);

        off = nameInfo.next;

        const kind = buf[off++];

        const indexInfo =
            readULEB(buf, off);

        off = indexInfo.next;

        if (kind !== 0) {
            continue;
        }

        const arr =
            result.get(indexInfo.value) ??
            [];

        arr.push(nameInfo.value);

        result.set(
            indexInfo.value,
            arr
        );
    }

    return result;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CALL SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function scanCalls(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    const calls: {
        offset: number;
        target: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (
        let p = start;
        p < end;
        p++
    ) {
        if (buf[p] !== 0x10) {
            continue;
        }

        const info =
            readULEB(buf, p + 1);

        if (
            info.value >= 0 &&
            info.next <= end
        ) {
            calls.push({
                offset: p,
                target: info.value,
            });
        }
    }

    return calls;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CONSTANT SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function scanI32Consts(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    const result: {
        offset: number;
        value: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (
        let p = start;
        p < end;
        p++
    ) {
        // i32.const
        if (buf[p] !== 0x41) {
            continue;
        }

        const info =
            readULEB(buf, p + 1);

        if (
            info.value >= 0 &&
            info.next <= end
        ) {
            result.push({
                offset: p,
                value: info.value,
            });
        }
    }

    return result;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION REPORT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function reportFunction(
    wasm: Buffer,
    realFunctionIndex: number,
    importedFunctions: number,
    typeIndices: number[],
    bodies: ReturnType<
        typeof parseCodeBodies
    >,
    exports: Map<number, string[]>
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        ` FUNCTION ${realFunctionIndex}`
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    if (
        realFunctionIndex <
        importedFunctions
    ) {
        console.log(
            "This is an IMPORTED function."
        );

        return;
    }

    const bodyIndex =
        realFunctionIndex -
        importedFunctions;

    const body =
        bodies[bodyIndex];

    if (!body) {
        console.log(
            `Body ${bodyIndex} NOT FOUND`
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `real function index : ${realFunctionIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body index          : ${bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `type index           : ${
            typeIndices[bodyIndex]
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `body start           : 0x${body.bodyStart
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code start           : 0x${body.codeStart
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body end             : 0x${body.bodyEnd
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body size            : ${body.bodySize}`
    );

    const exp =
        exports.get(realFunctionIndex);

    console.log(
        `exports              : ${
            exp?.join(", ") ??
            "none"
        }`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("I32.CONST");

    const constants =
        scanI32Consts(
            wasm,
            body.codeStart,
            body.bodyEnd
        );

    for (const c of constants) {
        console.log(
            `  @0x${c.offset
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}` +
            ` → ${c.value}` +
            ` (0x${c.value
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")})`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALLS");

    const calls =
        scanCalls(
            wasm,
            body.codeStart,
            body.bodyEnd
        );

    for (const c of calls) {
        console.log(
            `  @0x${c.offset
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}` +
            ` → function ${c.target}`
        );
    }

    if (!calls.length) {
        console.log("  none");
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("BODY HEX");

    console.log(
        hex(
            wasm,
            body.bodyStart,
            body.bodyEnd
        )
    );
}

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " FUNCTION 122 / 259 INSPECTOR v25"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(
            `WASM not found:\n${WASM_PATH}`
        );
    }

    const wasm =
        fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    const sections =
        parseSections(wasm);

    const importSection =
        sections.find(
            (s) => s.id === 2
        );

    const functionSection =
        sections.find(
            (s) => s.id === 3
        );

    const exportSection =
        sections.find(
            (s) => s.id === 7
        );

    const codeSection =
        sections.find(
            (s) => s.id === 10
        );

    if (
        !importSection ||
        !functionSection ||
        !codeSection
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Required WASM sections missing"
        );
    }

    const importedFunctions =
        countImportedFunctions(
            wasm,
            importSection
        );

    const typeIndices =
        parseFunctionTypes(
            wasm,
            functionSection
        );

    const bodies =
        parseCodeBodies(
            wasm,
            codeSection
        );

    const exports =
        parseExports(
            wasm,
            exportSection
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " MODULE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        `imported functions : ${importedFunctions}`
    );

    console.log(
        `defined functions  : ${typeIndices.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code bodies        : ${bodies.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `total function space: ${
            importedFunctions +
            typeIndices.length
        }`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " TARGET CHAIN"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "Segment 669"
    );

    console.log(
        "    ↓"
    );

    console.log(
        "Function 656"
    );

    console.log(
        "    ↓"
    );

    console.log(
        "Function 122"
    );

    console.log(
        "    ↓"
    );

    console.log(
        "Function 259 / 83 / 84 / 85 / 86"
    );

    for (
        const fn of TARGET_FUNCTIONS
    ) {
        reportFunction(
            wasm,
            fn,
            importedFunctions,
            typeIndices,
            bodies,
            exports
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " DONE"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY: no TON"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY: no RPC"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY: no wallet"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY: no transaction"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY: no signing"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
}

if (require.main === module) {
    run().catch((err) => {
        console.error(err);
        process.exitCode = 1;
    });
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunction1566.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 1566;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = null;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionStart = pos;
  const id = wasm[pos++];

  const sz = readULEB(pos);
  pos = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = pos;
    break;
  }

  pos = sectionStart + 1 + (sz.next - (sectionStart + 1)) + sz.value;
}

if (codeStart === null) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

const count = readULEB(codeStart);
const bodyCount = count.value;

let p = count.next;

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (bodyStart === null) {
  throw new Error("TARGET BODY NOT FOUND");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BLOCKTYPE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readBlockType(pos) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  // Empty block type
  if (b === 0x40) {
    return {
      value: "0x40",
      next: pos + 1
    };
  }

  // Value types
  const valueTypes = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64"
  };

  if (valueTypes[b]) {
    return {
      value: valueTypes[b],
      next: pos + 1
    };
  }

  // Otherwise it is a signed type index
  return readSLEB(pos);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decode(start, end) {
  let p = start;

  const decl = readULEB(p);
  const declCount = decl.value;
  p = decl.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < declCount; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    const type = wasm[p++];

    for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
      locals.push(type);
    }
  }

  const out = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CONTROL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      const bt = readBlockType(p);
      p = bt.next;

      const name =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
        "if";

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `${name} type=${bt.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "else"
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "end"
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text:
          op === 0x0c
            ? `br ${x.value}`
            : `br_if ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "return"
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCALS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        local: x.value,
        text: `${name} local${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CONSTANTS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        value: x.value,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      let q = p;
      let result = 0n;
      let shift = 0n;
      let b;

      while (true) {
        b = wasm[q++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7n;
      }

      p = q;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i64.const ${result}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        target: x.value,
        text: `call ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const memNames = {
      0x28: "i32.load",
      0x29: "i64.load",
      0x2a: "f32.load",
      0x2b: "f64.load",
      0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
      0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
      0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
      0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
      0x30: "i64.load8_s",
      0x31: "i64.load8_u",
      0x32: "i64.load16_s",
      0x33: "i64.load16_u",
      0x34: "i64.load32_s",
      0x35: "i64.load32_u",
      0x36: "i32.store",
      0x37: "i64.store",
      0x38: "f32.store",
      0x39: "f64.store",
      0x3a: "i32.store8",
      0x3b: "i32.store16",
      0x3c: "i64.store8",
      0x3d: "i64.store16",
      0x3e: "i64.store32"
    };

    if (memNames[op]) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        offset: o.value,
        text:
          `${memNames[op]} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // INTEGER OPERATIONS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const names = {
      0x45: "i32.eqz",

      0x46: "i32.eq",
      0x47: "i32.ne",

      0x48: "i32.lt_s",
      0x49: "i32.lt_u",
      0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
      0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
      0x4c: "i32.le_s",
      0x4d: "i32.le_u",
      0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
      0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

      0x50: "i64.eqz",
      0x51: "i64.eq",
      0x52: "i64.ne",

      0x67: "i32.clz",
      0x68: "i32.ctz",
      0x69: "i32.popcnt",

      0x6a: "i32.add",
      0x6b: "i32.sub",
      0x6c: "i32.mul",

      0x71: "i32.and",
      0x72: "i32.or",
      0x73: "i32.xor",
      0x74: "i32.shl",
      0x75: "i32.shr_s",
      0x76: "i32.shr_u",

      0x7c: "i64.add",
      0x7d: "i64.sub",
      0x7e: "i64.mul",

      0x83: "i64.and",
      0x84: "i64.or",
      0x85: "i64.xor",
      0x86: "i64.shl",
      0x87: "i64.shr_s",
      0x88: "i64.shr_u",

      0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64"
    };

    out.push({
      at,
      op,
      text: names[op] || `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    });
  }

  return {
    locals,
    instructions: out
  };
}

const decoded = decode(bodyStart, bodyEnd);
const ins = decoded.instructions;

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TYPE_SECTION_START = 0x00000008;
const TYPE_SECTION_END   = 0x0000039b;

const FUNCTION_SECTION_START = 0x000005ae;
const FUNCTION_SECTION_END   = 0x00002a69;

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 205;

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let q = p;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[q++];

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: q };
}

function valType(x: number): string {
    switch (x) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default: return `type(${hex(x, 2)})`;
    }
}

function main() {
    const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 205 SIGNATURE INSPECTOR V1");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * TYPE SECTION
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    let p = TYPE_SECTION_START;

    if (b[p++] !== 0x01) {
        throw new Error("Type section ID mismatch");
    }

    const typePayload = readULEB(b, p);
    p = typePayload.next;

    const typeEnd = p + typePayload.value;

    const typeCount = readULEB(b, p);
    p = typeCount.next;

    console.log(`Type count : ${typeCount.value}`);
    console.log();

    type TypeInfo = {
        params: string[];
        results: string[];
    };

    const types: TypeInfo[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {

        const form = b[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported type form ${hex(form, 2)} at ${hex(p - 1)}`
            );
        }

        const pc = readULEB(b, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
            params.push(valType(b[p++]));
        }

        const rc = readULEB(b, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
            results.push(valType(b[p++]));
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    if (p !== typeEnd) {
        console.log(
            `WARNING: type parser ended at ${hex(p)}, expected ${hex(typeEnd)}`
        );
    }

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * FUNCTION SECTION
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Function section contains type indices for defined functions.
     *
     * Function 83 is the first defined function because there are
     * 83 imported functions.
     */

    p = FUNCTION_SECTION_START;

    if (b[p++] !== 0x03) {
        throw new Error("Function section ID mismatch");
    }

    const fnPayload = readULEB(b, p);
    p = fnPayload.next;

    const fnEnd = p + fnPayload.value;

    const fnCount = readULEB(b, p);
    p = fnCount.next;

    console.log(`Defined functions : ${fnCount.value}`);
    console.log(`Import count      : ${IMPORT_COUNT}`);
    console.log();

    const targetBodyIndex = TARGET_FUNC - IMPORT_COUNT;

    if (targetBodyIndex < 0 || targetBodyIndex >= fnCount.value) {
        throw new Error("Target function is not a defined function");
    }

    let targetTypeIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < fnCount.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(b, p);
        p = r.next;

        const realFunc = IMPORT_COUNT + i;

        if (realFunc === TARGET_FUNC) {
            targetTypeIndex = r.value;

            console.log(
                `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} body index : ${i}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} type index : ${r.value}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (p !== fnEnd) {
        console.log(
            `WARNING: function parser ended at ${hex(p)}, expected ${hex(fnEnd)}`
        );
    }

    if (targetTypeIndex < 0) {
        throw new Error("Function 205 type index not found");
    }

    const t = types[targetTypeIndex];

    if (!t) {
        throw new Error(
            `Type index ${targetTypeIndex} does not exist`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 205 EXACT SIGNATURE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `(${t.params.join(", ")}) -> (${t.results.join(", ")})`
    );

    console.log();
    console.log(`Parameter count : ${t.params.length}`);
    console.log(`Result count    : ${t.results.length}`);

    console.log();
    console.log("PARAMETERS");

    t.params.forEach((x, i) => {
        console.log(`  arg${i} : ${x}`);
    });

    console.log();
    console.log("RESULTS");

    t.results.forEach((x, i) => {
        console.log(`  result${i} : ${x}`);
    });

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

main();

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
                v |= (~0 << shift);
            }
            break;
        }
    }

    return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 TYPE / SIGNATURE INSPECTOR");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sz.value;

    console.log(
        `SECTION id=${id} start=0x${start.toString(16)} size=${sz.value}`
    );

    if (id === 1) {
        console.log("TYPE SECTION");
        console.log("type section start:", "0x" + start.toString(16));

        let q = start;
        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.next;

        console.log("type count =", count.value);

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
            const form = b[q++];

            if (form !== 0x60) {
                console.log(`type ${i}: form=0x${form.toString(16)}`);
                continue;
            }

            const np = uleb(q);
            q = np.next;

            const params: number[] = [];

            for (let j = 0; j < np.value; j++) {
                params.push(b[q++]);
            }

            const nr = uleb(q);
            q = nr.next;

            const results: number[] = [];

            for (let j = 0; j < nr.value; j++) {
                results.push(b[q++]);
            }

            if (i === 9) {
                console.log("");
                console.log("TARGET TYPE INDEX = 9");
                console.log("params =", params.map(x =>
                    `0x${x.toString(16)}`
                ));
                console.log("results =", results.map(x =>
                    `0x${x.toString(16)}`
                ));
            }
        }
    }

    p = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("END");

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x00095ea0;
const END   = 0x00096ab8;

const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x00095fcc, // L7 tee
    0x000964d1, // L7 tee
    0x0009698a, // L6 tee
    0x00096a4f, // L6 tee
]);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let q = p;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[q++];

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: q };
}

function readSLEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let q = p;
    let x = 0;

    while (true) {
        x = b[q++];

        value |= BigInt(x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7n;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (x & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
    }

    return { value, next: q };
}

function opName(op: number) {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x30: "i64.load8_u",
        0x31: "i64.load8_s",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x74: "i32.shl",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
        0xac: "i64.extend_i32_s",
        0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u",

        0x10: "call",
    };

    return m[op] ?? `op=${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function decodeOne(b: Buffer, pc: number) {
    const start = pc;
    const op = b[pc++];

    const name = opName(op);

    let imm = "";

    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24 ||
        op === 0x10
    ) {
        const r = readULEB(b, pc);
        pc = r.next;

        imm = String(r.value);
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(b, pc);
        pc = r.next;

        imm = r.value.toString();
    }

    else if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(b, pc);
        pc = r.next;

        imm = r.value.toString();
    }

    else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
        const a = readULEB(b, pc);
        pc = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(b, pc);
        pc = o.next;

        imm = `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    else if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = readULEB(b, pc);
        pc = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(b, pc);
        pc = o.next;

        imm = `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    return {
        start,
        end: pc,
        op,
        name,
        imm,
    };
}

function main() {
    const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 LOCAL 6/7 PRODUCER WINDOWS V1");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log(`Window    : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
    console.log();

    let p = START;

    const decoded: any[] = [];

    while (p < END) {
        const ins = decodeOne(b, p);

        decoded.push(ins);

        p = ins.end;
    }

    for (const target of [...TARGETS].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {

        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`TARGET @ ${hex(target)}`);
        console.log("============================================================");

        const index = decoded.findIndex(x => x.start === target);

        if (index < 0) {
            console.log("TARGET NOT FOUND ON INSTRUCTION BOUNDARY");
            console.log();
            continue;
        }

        const from = Math.max(0, index - 12);
        const to = Math.min(decoded.length, index + 5);

        for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
            const x = decoded[i];

            const mark =
                x.start === target ? "  <<< TARGET" : "";

            console.log(
                `${hex(x.start)}  ${x.name.padEnd(16)} ${x.imm}${mark}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("RAW BYTES AROUND TARGETS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const target of [...TARGETS].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {

        console.log();
        console.log(`TARGET ${hex(target)}`);

        const from = target - 24;
        const to = target + 32;

        for (let p = from; p < to; p += 8) {

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let j = 0; j < 8 && p + j < b.length; j++) {
                bytes.push(
                    b[p + j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

main();

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 248;

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  do {
    b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * LOCATE CODE SECTION
 * ============================================================
 */

let p = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const payloadStart = size.next;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = payloadStart;
    codeEnd = payloadEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!codeStart)
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * LOCATE FUNCTION BODY
 * ============================================================
 */

p = codeStart;

const bodyCount = readULEB(p);

p = bodyCount.next;

let targetBody = null;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const bodyStart = size.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

  const funcIndex = IMPORT_COUNT + bodyIndex;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {

    targetBody = {
      bodyIndex,
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    };

    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!targetBody)
  throw new Error(
    "FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND: " + TARGET_FUNC
  );

/*
 * ============================================================
 * HEADER
 * ============================================================
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT FUNCTION 248");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("FUNCTION :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("BODY INDEX :", targetBody.bodyIndex);
console.log(
  "BODY :",
  hex(targetBody.start),
  "..",
  hex(targetBody.end)
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * LOCAL DECLARATIONS
 * ============================================================
 */

p = targetBody.start;

const localDecl = readULEB(p);

p = localDecl.next;

console.log(
  "LOCAL DECLARATION GROUPS :",
  localDecl.value
);

for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {

  const count = readULEB(p);

  p = count.next;

  const type = wasm[p++];

  console.log(
    "LOCAL GROUP",
    i,
    "COUNT",
    count.value,
    "TYPE",
    hex(type)
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * RAW INSTRUCTION DUMP
 *
 * Only decode the instructions we need.
 * Unknown opcodes are shown as raw bytes.
 * ============================================================
 */

let q = p;

let callFound = false;

while (q < targetBody.end) {

  const off = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];

  let text = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(q);
      q = offset.next;

      text =
        `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(q);
      q = offset.next;

      text =
        `i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;

      text = `call ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {

      const type = readULEB(q);
      q = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(q);
      q = table.next;

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;

      if (type.value === 3) {
        callFound = true;
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x6a:
      text = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      text = "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x45:
      text = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      break;

    default:

      text =
        `UNKNOWN ${hex(op)}`;

      /*
       * Do NOT stop.
       *
       * Print raw byte and continue.
       */

      break;
  }

  console.log(
    hex(off),
    text
  );

  if (callFound)
    break;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (callFound) {

  console.log(
    "TYPE-3 CALL FOUND : YES"
  );

  console.log(
    "IMPORTANT: inspect stack immediately before call_indirect."
  );

} else {

  console.log(
    "TYPE-3 CALL FOUND : NO"
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");
