import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 259;

const TARGET_LOADS = new Set([
    0x144fb,
    0x14634,
    0x146e2,
]);

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readSections(b: Buffer) {
    const result: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < b.length) {
        const id = b[p++];

        const size = readULEB(b, p);
        p = size.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + size.value;

        result.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size: size.value,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return result;
}

function section(
    sections: any[],
    id: number
) {
    const s = sections.find(x => x.id === id);

    if (!s) {
        throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
    }

    return s;
}

function countImportedFunctions(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    let functions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const ml = readULEB(b, p);
        p = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = readULEB(b, p);
        p = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = b[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const t = readULEB(b, p);
            p = t.next;
            functions++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {

            const flags = b[p++];

            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const y = readULEB(b, p);
                p = y.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 2) {

            const flags = b[p++];

            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const y = readULEB(b, p);
                p = y.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;

        } else if (kind === 4) {

            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;

            const y = readULEB(b, p);
            p = y.next;
        }
    }

    return functions;
}

function getFunctionTypes(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(b, p);
        p = x.next;

        result.push(x.value);
    }

    return result;
}

function getBodies(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(b, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

        const localCount = readULEB(b, p);
        p = localCount.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {

            const count = readULEB(b, p);
            p = count.next;

            p += 1;
        }

        const codeStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            bodyStart,
            codeStart,
            bodyEnd,
            bodySize: sizeInfo.value,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",

        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x38: "f32.store",
        0x39: "f64.store",

        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x79: "i32.rotl",
        0x7a: "i32.rotr",

        0x10: "call",

        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",

        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
    };

    return m[op] ?? `opcode_${hex8(op)}`;
}

type Instr = {
    pos: number;
    abs: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    args: string;
};

function decode(
    code: Buffer,
    codeStart: number
): Instr[] {

    const result: Instr[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < code.length) {

        const start = p;
        const op = code[p++];

        let args = "";

        try {

            switch (op) {

                case 0x20:
                case 0x21:
                case 0x22:
                case 0x23:
                case 0x24: {
                    const x = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = x.next;

                    args = `${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {
                    const x = readSLEB(code, p);
                    p = x.next;

                    args = `${x.value} (${hex8(x.value >>> 0)})`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {
                    const x = readSLEB(code, p);
                    p = x.next;

                    args = `${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x10: {
                    const x = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = x.next;

                    args = `function ${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: {
                    const x = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = x.next;

                    const y = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = y.next;

                    args = `type ${x.value}, table ${y.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x2c:
                case 0x2d:
                case 0x2e:
                case 0x2f:
                case 0x30:
                case 0x31:
                case 0x32:
                case 0x33:
                case 0x34:
                case 0x35:
                case 0x36:
                case 0x37:
                case 0x38:
                case 0x39:
                case 0x3a:
                case 0x3b:
                case 0x3c:
                case 0x3d:
                case 0x3e: {

                    const align = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = align.next;

                    const off = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = off.next;

                    args =
                        `align=${align.value} offset=${hex8(off.value)}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x3f:
                case 0x40: {
                    p += 1;
                    args = "memory 0";
                    break;
                }

                /*
                 * ref.null
                 */
                case 0xd0:
                    p += 1;
                    args = "heaptype";
                    break;

                /*
                 * ref.func
                 */
                case 0xd2: {
                    const x = readULEB(code, p);
                    p = x.next;

                    args = `function ${x.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                /*
                 * select typed / misc prefixes are intentionally
                 * handled conservatively.
                 */
                default:
                    break;
            }

        } catch {
            break;
        }

        result.push({
            pos: start,
            abs: codeStart + start,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            args,
        });
    }

    return result;
}

function printInstruction(
    x: Instr
) {
    console.log(
        `${hex8(x.abs)}  ${x.name.padEnd(15)} ${x.args}`
    );
}

function printWindow(
    instructions: Instr[],
    center: number,
    radius = 22
) {

    const index = instructions.findIndex(
        x => x.abs === center
    );

    if (index < 0) {
        console.log(`Instruction ${hex8(center)} not found`);
        return;
    }

    const a = Math.max(0, index - radius);
    const z = Math.min(
        instructions.length,
        index + radius + 1
    );

    for (let i = a; i < z; i++) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        const marker =
            x.abs === center ? " >>> " : "     ";

        console.log(
            marker +
            `${hex8(x.abs)}  ` +
            `${x.name.padEnd(15)} ` +
            x.args
        );
    }
}

function printMemoryReferences(
    instructions: Instr[]
) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("STATIC MEMORY REFERENCES");
    console.log("");

    for (const x of instructions) {

        if (
            x.op >= 0x28 &&
            x.op <= 0x3e
        ) {

            console.log(
                `${hex8(x.abs)}  ` +
                `${x.name.padEnd(15)} ` +
                x.args
            );
        }
    }
}

function printCalls(
    instructions: Instr[]
) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("REAL CALL INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log("");

    let count = 0;

    for (const x of instructions) {

        if (x.op === 0x10) {

            console.log(
                `${hex8(x.abs)} → ${x.args}`
            );

            count++;
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`CALL COUNT : ${count}`);
}

async function main() {

    console.log(
        "Using file: inspectFunction259DataFlowV28"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " FUNCTION 259 DATA-FLOW / ADDRESS TRACE v28"
    );

    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    const sections = readSections(wasm);

    const imported =
        countImportedFunctions(
            wasm,
            section(sections, 2)
        );

    const functionTypes =
        getFunctionTypes(
            wasm,
            section(sections, 3)
        );

    const bodies =
        getBodies(
            wasm,
            section(sections, 10)
        );

    const bodyIndex =
        TARGET_FUNCTION - imported;

    const body = bodies[bodyIndex];

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${TARGET_FUNCTION} not found`
        );
    }

    const code =
        wasm.subarray(
            body.codeStart,
            body.bodyEnd
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTION");

    console.log(
        `real function index : ${TARGET_FUNCTION}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body index          : ${bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `type index           : ${functionTypes[bodyIndex]}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body start           : ${hex8(body.bodyStart)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code start           : ${hex8(body.codeStart)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body end             : ${hex8(body.bodyEnd)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body size            : ${body.bodySize}`
    );

    const instructions =
        decode(
            code,
            body.codeStart
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `DECODED INSTRUCTIONS : ${instructions.length}`
    );

    printMemoryReferences(
        instructions
    );

    printCalls(
        instructions
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " TARGET LOAD #1 : 0x144fb"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    printWindow(
        instructions,
        0x144fb,
        30
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " TARGET LOAD #2 : 0x14634"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    printWindow(
        instructions,
        0x14634,
        30
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " TARGET LOAD #3 : 0x146e2"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    printWindow(
        instructions,
        0x146e2,
        30
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "DONE"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no TON"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no RPC"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no wallet"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no transaction"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no signing"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
}

main().catch(err => {
    console.error("");
    console.error("ERROR");
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
});

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    0x00250fb8,
    0x0025587c,
    0x00255880,
    0x00255884,
    0x00255888,
    0x0025588c,
    0x00255890,
    0x00255894,
    0x00255898,
    0x0025589c,
    0x002558a0,
    0x002558a4,
    0x002559ac,
    0x00255a5c,
];

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function readSections(b: Buffer) {
    const out: {
        id: number;
        payload: number;
        size: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < b.length) {
        const id = b[p++];

        const s = readULEB(b, p);
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + s.value;

        out.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size: s.value,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return out;
}

function getSection(
    sections: ReturnType<typeof readSections>,
    id: number
) {
    const s = sections.find(x => x.id === id);

    if (!s) {
        throw new Error(`Missing section ${id}`);
    }

    return s;
}

function parseImportFunctionCount(
    b: Buffer,
    section: ReturnType<typeof getSection>
) {
    let p = section.payload;

    const c = readULEB(b, p);
    p = c.next;

    let functions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const ml = readULEB(b, p);
        p = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = readULEB(b, p);
        p = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = b[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const t = readULEB(b, p);
            p = t.next;
            functions++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            const flags = b[p++];

            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const m = readULEB(b, p);
                p = m.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = b[p++];

            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const m = readULEB(b, p);
                p = m.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else if (kind === 4) {
            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;

            const b2 = readULEB(b, p);
            p = b2.next;
        }
    }

    return functions;
}

function parseFunctionSection(
    b: Buffer,
    section: ReturnType<typeof getSection>
) {
    let p = section.payload;

    const c = readULEB(b, p);
    p = c.next;

    const types: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(b, p);
        p = x.next;
        types.push(x.value);
    }

    return types;
}

function parseBodies(
    b: Buffer,
    section: ReturnType<typeof getSection>
) {
    let p = section.payload;

    const c = readULEB(b, p);
    p = c.next;

    const bodies: {
        start: number;
        codeStart: number;
        end: number;
        bytes: Buffer;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const size = readULEB(b, p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p - (size.next - (p - size.next));

        const bodyStart = size.next;

        const localCount = readULEB(b, p);
        p = localCount.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(b, p);
            p = n.next;

            p += 1;
        }

        const codeStart = p;
        const end = bodyStart + size.value;

        bodies.push({
            start: bodyStart,
            codeStart,
            end,
            bytes: b.subarray(bodyStart, end),
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function ulebAt(
    b: Buffer,
    p: number
) {
    return readULEB(b, p);
}

function signedLEBAt(
    b: Buffer,
    p: number
) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function isMemoryLoad(op: number) {
    return (
        op === 0x28 || // i32.load
        op === 0x29 || // i64.load
        op === 0x2a || // f32.load
        op === 0x2b || // f64.load
        op === 0x2c || // i32.load8_s
        op === 0x2d || // i32.load8_u
        op === 0x2e || // i32.load16_s
        op === 0x2f || // i32.load16_u
        op === 0x30 || // i64.load8_s
        op === 0x31 || // i64.load8_u
        op === 0x32 || // i64.load16_s
        op === 0x33 || // i64.load16_u
        op === 0x34 || // i64.load32_s
        op === 0x35    // i64.load32_u
    );
}

function isMemoryStore(op: number) {
    return (
        op === 0x36 || // i32.store
        op === 0x37 || // i64.store
        op === 0x38 || // f32.store
        op === 0x39 || // f64.store
        op === 0x3a || // i32.store8
        op === 0x3b || // i32.store16
        op === 0x3c || // i64.store8
        op === 0x3d || // i64.store16
        op === 0x3e    // i64.store32
    );
}

function memoryName(op: number) {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",
        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x38: "f32.store",
        0x39: "f64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
}

function context(
    code: Buffer,
    pos: number,
    radius = 18
) {
    const a = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
    const z = Math.min(code.length, pos + radius + 12);

    return code
        .subarray(a, z)
        .toString("hex")
        .match(/.{1,2}/g)
        ?.join(" ") ?? "";
}

function scanMemoryInstructions(
    code: Buffer,
    codeStart: number
) {
    const hits: {
        pos: number;
        opcode: number;
        align: number;
        offset: number;
        context: string;
    }[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < code.length) {
        const pos = p;
        const op = code[p++];

        try {
            if (op === 0x41) {
                const x = signedLEBAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x42) {
                const x = signedLEBAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x43) {
                p += 4;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x44) {
                p += 8;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x10) {
                const x = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;
                continue;
            }

            if (
                op === 0x11
            ) {
                const x = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                const y = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = y.next;

                continue;
            }

            if (isMemoryLoad(op) || isMemoryStore(op)) {
                const align = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = off.next;

                hits.push({
                    pos,
                    opcode: op,
                    align: align.value,
                    offset: off.value,
                    context: context(code, pos),
                });

                continue;
            }

            /*
             * Memory.size / memory.grow
             */
            if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
                p += 1;
                continue;
            }

            /*
             * Simple opcodes with no immediate are left alone.
             * This decoder is intentionally conservative.
             */
        } catch {
            break;
        }
    }

    return hits.map(x => ({
        ...x,
        absolute: codeStart + x.pos,
    }));
}

function scanCalls(
    code: Buffer,
    codeStart: number
) {
    const calls: {
        absolute: number;
        target: number;
    }[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < code.length) {
        const pos = p;
        const op = code[p++];

        try {
            if (op === 0x10) {
                const x = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                calls.push({
                    absolute: codeStart + pos,
                    target: x.value,
                });

                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x11) {
                const x = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                const y = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = y.next;

                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
                const x = signedLEBAt(code, p);
                p = x.next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x43) {
                p += 4;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x44) {
                p += 8;
                continue;
            }

            if (isMemoryLoad(op) || isMemoryStore(op)) {
                const a = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = ulebAt(code, p);
                p = o.next;

                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
                p += 1;
                continue;
            }
        } catch {
            break;
        }
    }

    return calls;
}

function inspectTargetAccesses(
    code: Buffer,
    codeStart: number,
    hits: ReturnType<typeof scanMemoryInstructions>
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGET MEMORY ACCESSES");
    console.log("");

    let found = 0;

    for (const h of hits) {
        const absoluteOffset = h.offset;

        for (const target of TARGETS) {
            /*
             * A wasm memory instruction uses:
             * effective_address = stack_address + immediate_offset
             *
             * We can only identify a direct absolute access here
             * when the offset itself equals the target.
             */
            if (absoluteOffset === target) {
                console.log(
                    `@0x${h.absolute.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                    `${memoryName(h.opcode)} ` +
                    `align=${h.align} ` +
                    `offset=0x${absoluteOffset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                );

                console.log(`  context: ${h.context}`);

                found++;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`DIRECT TARGET OFFSETS FOUND : ${found}`);
}

async function main() {
    console.log("Using file: inspectFunction259StateV27");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 259 MEMORY / STATE TRACE v27");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    const sections = readSections(wasm);

    const importSection = getSection(sections, 2);
    const functionSection = getSection(sections, 3);
    const codeSection = getSection(sections, 10);

    const imported = parseImportFunctionCount(
        wasm,
        importSection
    );

    const functionTypes = parseFunctionSection(
        wasm,
        functionSection
    );

    const bodies = parseBodies(
        wasm,
        codeSection
    );

    const targetFunction = 259;
    const bodyIndex = targetFunction - imported;

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTION");

    console.log(`imported functions : ${imported}`);
    console.log(`target function    : ${targetFunction}`);
    console.log(`body index         : ${bodyIndex}`);
    console.log(`type index         : ${functionTypes[bodyIndex]}`);

    const body = bodies[bodyIndex];

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error("Function 259 body not found");
    }

    const code = body.bytes.subarray(
        body.codeStart - body.start
    );

    console.log(
        `body start         : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code start         : 0x${body.codeStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body end           : 0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(`body size          : ${body.bytes.length}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGETS");

    for (const x of TARGETS) {
        console.log(
            `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} = ${x}`
        );
    }

    const memory = scanMemoryInstructions(
        code,
        body.codeStart
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("MEMORY INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log(`count : ${memory.length}`);

    for (const m of memory) {
        console.log(
            `@0x${m.absolute.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
            `${memoryName(m.opcode)} ` +
            `align=${m.align} ` +
            `offset=0x${m.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
    }

    inspectTargetAccesses(
        code,
        body.codeStart,
        memory
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALLS");

    const calls = scanCalls(
        code,
        body.codeStart
    );

    for (const c of calls) {
        console.log(
            `@0x${c.absolute.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} → function ${c.target}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no TON");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no RPC");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no signing");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

main().catch(err => {
    console.error("");
    console.error("ERROR");
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
});

import fs from "fs";

const path =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x001243ce;
const END   = 0x00124729;

let p = START;

function hx(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(): number {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            return v;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function valtype(x: number): string {
    switch (x) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default:
            return `UNKNOWN(0x${x.toString(16)})`;
    }
}

        `GROUP ${i}: ` +

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 3837;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

function readULEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                result |= (~0 << shift);

            return { value: result | 0, next: p };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function opName(op) {
    const m = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x1b: "select",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul"
    };

    return m[op] || `opcode_0x${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function decodeInstruction(buf, p, end) {
    const start = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
            value: r.value >>> 0
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `i64.const ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `call func=${r.value}`,
            callTarget: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        const table = buf[r.next];

        return {
            start,
            end: r.next + 2,
            text: `call_indirect type=${r.value} table=${table}`
        };
    }

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `${opName(op)} local=${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `${opName(op)} global=${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2c ||
        op === 0x2d ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37
    ) {
        const align = readULEB(buf, p);
        const offset = readULEB(buf, align.next);

        return {
            start,
            end: offset.next,
            text:
                `${opName(op)} ` +
                `align=${align.value} ` +
                `offset=${offset.value}`
        };
    }

    return {
        start,
        end: p,
        text: opName(op)
    };
}

console.log();

console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS);
console.log();

// Find Code Section
//

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

let targetFound = false;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("TARGET");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("FUNCTION :", funcIndex);
    console.log("BODY     :", bodyIndex);
    console.log(
        `RANGE    : 0x${hex(bodyStart)} .. 0x${hex(bodyEnd)}`
    );
    console.log("SIZE     :", bodySize);
    console.log();

    //
    // Local declarations
    //
    let q = bodyStart;

    const localInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
    const localCount = localInfo.value;
    q = localInfo.next;

    console.log("LOCAL DECL GROUPS :", localCount);
    console.log();

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(wasm, q);
        q = n.next;

        const type = wasm[q++];

        console.log(
            `LOCAL GROUP ${i} COUNT=${n.value} TYPE=0x${hex(type, 2)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const instructions = [];

    while (q < bodyEnd) {
        const ins = decodeInstruction(wasm, q, bodyEnd);

        instructions.push(ins);

        console.log(
            `0x${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}`
        );

        q = ins.end;

        if (ins.text === "end" && q >= bodyEnd)
            break;
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("STRUCTURAL HIGHLIGHTS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {

        const a = instructions[i];
        const b = instructions[i + 1];
        const c = instructions[i + 2];

        //
        // Detect:
        // local.get X
        // i32.const VALUE
        // i32.store offset=...
        //
        if (
            a.text.startsWith("local.get local=") &&
            b &&
            b.value !== undefined &&
            c &&
            c.text.startsWith("i32.store")
        ) {
            console.log(
                `STORE PATTERN @ 0x${hex(a.start)} :`
            );

            console.log(
                `  ${a.text}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  ${b.text}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  ${c.text}`
            );

            if (
                b.value === 1613172 ||
                b.value === 1613200 ||
                b.value === 1613248 ||
                b.value === 1613268 ||
                b.value === 1613316 ||
                b.value === 1613344
            ) {
                console.log(
                    "  *** TARGET STATIC CONSTANT ***"
                );
            }

            console.log();
        }

        //
        // Detect direct calls
        //
        if (a.callTarget !== undefined) {
            console.log(
                `DIRECT CALL @ 0x${hex(a.start)} -> FUNC ${a.callTarget}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");

    p = bodyEnd;
    break;
}

if (!targetFound) {
    console.log("TARGET FUNCTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 446;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 0;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function skipBlockType(pos) {

    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b ||
        b === 0x70 ||
        b === 0x6f
    ) {
        return pos + 1;
    }

    return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

function memArg(pos) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function decode(body) {

    const out = [];

    let p = body.start;

    while (p < body.end) {

        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text;

        try {

            switch (op) {

                case 0x00:
                    text = "unreachable";
                    break;

                case 0x01:
                    text = "nop";
                    break;

                case 0x02:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "block";
                    break;

                case 0x03:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "loop";
                    break;

                case 0x04:
                    p = skipBlockType(p);
                    text = "if";
                    break;

                case 0x05:
                    text = "else";
                    break;

                case 0x0b:
                    text = "end";
                    break;

                case 0x0c: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `br ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0d: {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0e: {

                    const n = readULEB(p);
                    p = n.next;

                    const labels = [];

                    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                        const r = readULEB(p);
                        p = r.next;
                        labels.push(r.value);
                    }

                    const d = readULEB(p);
                    p = d.next;

                    text =
                        `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x0f:
                    text = "return";
                    break;

                case 0x10: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `call ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: {

                    const t = readULEB(p);
                    p = t.next;

                    const table = readULEB(p);
                    p = table.next;

                    text =
                        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x1a:
                    text = "drop";
                    break;

                case 0x1b:
                    text = "select";
                    break;

                case 0x20: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `local.get ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x21: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `local.set ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x22: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x23: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `global.get ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x24: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `global.set ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x2c:
                case 0x2d:
                case 0x2e:
                case 0x2f:
                case 0x30:
                case 0x31:
                case 0x32:
                case 0x33:
                case 0x34:
                case 0x35:
                case 0x36:
                case 0x37:
                case 0x38:
                case 0x39:
                case 0x3a:
                case 0x3b:
                case 0x3c:
                case 0x3d:
                case 0x3e: {

                    const m = memArg(p);
                    p = m.next;

                    const names = {
                        0x28: "i32.load",
                        0x29: "i64.load",
                        0x2a: "f32.load",
                        0x2b: "f64.load",
                        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
                        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
                        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
                        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
                        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
                        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
                        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
                        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
                        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
                        0x35: "i64.load32_u",
                        0x36: "i32.store",
                        0x37: "i64.store",
                        0x38: "f32.store",
                        0x39: "f64.store",
                        0x3a: "i32.store8",
                        0x3b: "i32.store16",
                        0x3c: "i64.store8",
                        0x3d: "i64.store16",
                        0x3e: "i64.store32"
                    };

                    text =
                        `${names[op]} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x3f: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `memory.size ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x40: {

                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `memory.grow ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {

                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {

                    const r = readSLEB(p);
                    p = r.next;

                    text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;

                    break;
                }

                default:

                    text =
                        `opcode 0x${op
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")}`;

                    break;
            }

        } catch {

            text = "DECODE ERROR";
        }

        out.push({
            pos: at,
            op,
            text
        });
    }

    return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
const bodies = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        const count = readULEB(pos);
        pos = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

            const b = readULEB(pos);
            pos = b.next;

            const start = pos;
            const finish = start + b.value;

            bodies.push({
                index: i,
                start,
                end: finish
            });

            pos = finish;
        }

        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

const body = bodies[TARGET];

if (!body) {
    console.log("FUNC 446 NOT FOUND");
    process.exit(1);
}

const ins = decode(body);

    body.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

    );

    if (!ins[i].text.includes("offset=4")) continue;

    console.log("");

    const start = Math.max(0, i - 8);
    const end = Math.min(ins.length, i + 9);

    for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {

        const marker =
            j === i ? ">>> " : "    ";

        console.log(
            marker +
            "0x" +
            ins[j].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +

            ins[j].text
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunction66SignatureV1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 66;
const START = 0x126c0;
const END = 0x126fa;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p.v++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return result >>> 0;
}

function readName(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): string {
    const len = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    const s = buf.subarray(p.v, p.v + len).toString("utf8");
    p.v += len;
    return s;
}

function valType(b: number): string {
    switch (b) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default: return `0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }
}

interface FuncType {
    params: string[];
    results: string[];
}

const types: FuncType[] = [];
const functionTypeIndices: number[] = [];

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

interface Section {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    payloadEnd: number;
}

const sections: Section[] = [];

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];

    const p = { v: pos };
    const size = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    const payloadStart = p.v;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: pos - 1,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
    });

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

/* ============================================================
   TYPE SECTION
   ============================================================ */

const typeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 1);

if (!typeSec) {
    throw new Error("Type section not found");
}

{
    const p = { v: typeSec.payloadStart };
    const count = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p.v++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at type ${i}`
            );
        }

        const paramCount = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
        const params: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(valType(wasm[p.v++]));
        }

        const resultCount = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
        const results: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(valType(wasm[p.v++]));
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
    }
}

/* ============================================================
   IMPORT SECTION
   ============================================================ */

const importSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

if (importSec) {
    const p = { v: importSec.payloadStart };
    const count = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const moduleName = readName(wasm, p);
        const fieldName = readName(wasm, p);
        const kind = wasm[p.v++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {
            const typeIndex = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            importedFunctionCount++;

            console.log(
                `IMPORT FUNC #${importedFunctionCount - 1}` +
                ` ${moduleName}.${fieldName}` +
                ` type=${typeIndex}`
            );
        } else if (kind === 0x01) {
            p.v += 1; // elem type
            const flags = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            if (flags & 1) {
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            }

            readU32LEB(wasm, p);
        } else if (kind === 0x02) {
            const flags = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            if (flags & 1) {
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x03) {
            p.v += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }
}

/* ============================================================
   FUNCTION SECTION
   ============================================================ */

const funcSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

if (!funcSec) {
    throw new Error("Function section not found");
}

{
    const p = { v: funcSec.payloadStart };
    const count = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        functionTypeIndices.push(readU32LEB(wasm, p));
    }
}

/* ============================================================
   SIGNATURE OF FUNCTION 66
   ============================================================ */

const definedIndex = TARGET_FUNC - importedFunctionCount;

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 66 SIGNATURE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Target function index : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`Imported functions   : ${importedFunctionCount}`);
console.log(`Defined-function idx : ${definedIndex}`);

if (definedIndex < 0 || definedIndex >= functionTypeIndices.length) {
    console.log("Function 66 is not a defined function.");
    process.exit(0);
}

const typeIndex = functionTypeIndices[definedIndex];
const ft = types[typeIndex];

console.log(`Type index            : ${typeIndex}`);
console.log(
    `Signature             : (${ft.params.join(", ")}) -> (${ft.results.join(", ")})`
);

console.log(`Parameter count       : ${ft.params.length}`);
console.log(`Result count          : ${ft.results.length}`);

if (ft.params.length) {
    console.log(`Params                : ${ft.params.join(", ")}`);
}

if (ft.results.length) {
    console.log(`Results               : ${ft.results.join(", ")}`);
} else {
    console.log("Results               : NONE");
}

/* ============================================================
   CODE SECTION
   ============================================================ */

const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

const bodyOffsets: {
    funcIndex: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodySize: number;
    codeStart: number;
    codeEnd: number;
}[] = [];

{
    const p = { v: codeSec.payloadStart };
    const count = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    const firstDefined = importedFunctionCount;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const bodySizeStart = p.v;
        const bodySize = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        const bodyStart = p.v;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        const q = { v: bodyStart };

        const localDeclCount = readU32LEB(wasm, q);

        for (let j = 0; j < localDeclCount; j++) {
            readU32LEB(wasm, q); // count
            q.v++;                // valtype
        }

        bodyOffsets.push({
            funcIndex: firstDefined + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodySize,
            codeStart: q.v,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd,
        });

        p.v = bodyEnd;

        void bodySizeStart;
    }
}

const targetBody = bodyOffsets.find(x => x.funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 66 BODY");
console.log("============================================================");

if (targetBody) {
    console.log(`Body start            : 0x${targetBody.bodyStart.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`Body size             : ${targetBody.bodySize}`);
    console.log(`Code start            : 0x${targetBody.codeStart.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`Code end              : 0x${targetBody.codeEnd.toString(16)}`);
} else {
    console.log("Function 66 body not found.");
}

/* ============================================================
   RAW WINDOW
   ============================================================ */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RAW WINDOW 0x126c0 .. 0x126fa");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let a = START; a < END; a += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(a + 16, END);

    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let x = a; x < e; x++) {
        bytes.push(wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(
        `0x${a.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
}

/* ============================================================
   LIMITED DECODER
   ============================================================ */

function readS32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = buf[p.v++];
        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readS64LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = buf[p.v++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7n;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return result;
}

function fmtI64(v: bigint): string {
    return v.toString();
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LIMITED DECODE");
console.log("============================================================");

const p = { v: START };

const stack: string[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, string>();

function pop(): string {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return "STACK_UNDERFLOW";
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(x: string) {
    stack.push(x);
}

while (p.v < END) {
    const pc = p.v;
    const op = wasm[p.v++];

    let text = "";

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
            text = "unreachable";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            text = "end";
            break;

        case 0x1a: {
            const x = pop();
            text = `drop ${x}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const v = locals.get(idx) ?? `L${idx}`;
            push(v);
            text = `local.get ${idx} -> ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const v = pop();
            locals.set(idx, v);
            text = `local.set ${idx} = ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const v = pop();
            locals.set(idx, v);
            push(v);
            text = `local.tee ${idx} = ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const v = readS32LEB(wasm, p);
            push(`i32.const(${v})`);
            text = `i32.const ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const v = readS64LEB(wasm, p);
            push(`i64.const(${fmtI64(v)})`);
            text = `i64.const ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x7c: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} + ${b})`);
            text = `i64.add (${a}) + (${b})`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x7d: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} - ${b})`);
            text = `i64.sub (${a}) - (${b})`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x7e: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} * ${b})`);
            text = `i64.mul`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x83: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} & ${b})`);
            text = `i64.and`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x84: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} | ${b})`);
            text = `i64.or`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x86: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} << ${b})`);
            text = `i64.shl`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x88: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} >>u ${b})`);
            text = `i64.shr_u`;
            break;
        }

        case 0xad: {
            const a = pop();
            push(`i64.extend32_s(${a})`);
            text = `i64.extend32_s`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x54: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} <u ${b})`);
            text = `i64.lt_u`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x56: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();
            push(`(${a} >u ${b})`);
            text = `i64.gt_u`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const align = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const offset = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const addr = pop();

            const expr = `MEM64[${addr} + ${offset}]`;

            push(expr);

            text =
                `i64.load align=${align} offset=${offset} ` +
                `=> ${expr}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const offset = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const value = pop();
            const addr = pop();

            text =
                `i64.store align=${align} offset=${offset} ` +
                `MEM64[${addr} + ${offset}] = ${value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            const sig =
                idx < importedFunctionCount
                    ? "IMPORTED"
                    : (() => {
                        const di = idx - importedFunctionCount;
                        const ti = functionTypeIndices[di];
                        const t = types[ti];
                        return `(${t.params.join(",")}) -> (${t.results.join(",")})`;
                    })();

            text = `CALL ${idx} ${sig}`;

            let resultCount = 0;

            if (idx >= importedFunctionCount) {
                const di = idx - importedFunctionCount;

                if (di >= 0 && di < functionTypeIndices.length) {
                    const ti = functionTypeIndices[di];
                    resultCount = types[ti].results.length;
                }
            }

            /*
             * IMPORTANT:
             * We intentionally do not invent argument names here.
             * We only model result arity.
             */
            if (resultCount === 1) {
                push(`CALL${idx}_RESULT`);
            } else if (resultCount > 1) {
                for (let i = 0; i < resultCount; i++) {
                    push(`CALL${idx}_RESULT${i}`);
                }
            }

            break;
        }

        default:
            text = `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            break;
    }

    console.log(
        `0x${pc.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${text}`
    );

    if (op === 0x0b) {
        break;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FINAL STACK");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Depth : ${stack.length}`);

for (let i = 0; i < stack.length; i++) {
    console.log(`  [${i}] ${stack[i]}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DONE - READ ONLY / OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x0001295c;

// Function 87 outputs:
// S0..S29, each 16 bytes.
const slots = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => ({
    name: `S${i}`,
    base: i * 16,
}));

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function u32le(p: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",
        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",

        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x25: "table.get",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",
        0x51: "i64.eq",
        0x52: "i64.ne",
        0x53: "i64.lt_s",
        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x55: "i64.gt_s",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",
        0x57: "i64.le_s",
        0x58: "i64.le_u",
        0x59: "i64.ge_s",
        0x5a: "i64.ge_u",

        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",

        0x79: "i64.clz",
        0x7a: "i64.ctz",
        0x7b: "i64.popcnt",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        0x89: "i64.rotl",
        0x8a: "i64.rotr",

        0xa7: "i64.extend_i32_s",
        0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u",
    };

    return m[op] ?? `UNKNOWN(0x${hex(op, 2)})`;
}

// Read ULEB128, returning [value,nextPosition].
function readULEB(pos: number): [number, number] {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return [value >>> 0, pos];
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return [value >>> 0, pos];
}

// Decode only enough immediate operands to keep instruction boundaries.
// This is intentionally conservative.
function instructionLength(pos: number): number {
    const op = wasm[pos];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x20: // local.get
        case 0x21: // local.set
        case 0x22: // local.tee
        case 0x23: // global.get
        case 0x24: // global.set
        case 0x0c: // br
        case 0x0d: // br_if
        case 0x10: { // call
            const [, next] = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return next - pos;
        }

        case 0x25: { // table.get
            const [, next] = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return next - pos;
        }

        case 0x28: // i32.load
        case 0x29: // i64.load
        case 0x2a: // f32.load
        case 0x2b: // f64.load
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37: {
            // memory immediate = align + offset
            let p = pos + 1;
            const [, p1] = readULEB(p);
            const [, p2] = readULEB(p1);
            return p2 - pos;
        }

        case 0x41: { // i32.const
            let p = pos + 1;
            while (p < wasm.length) {
                const b = wasm[p++];
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            }
            return p - pos;
        }

        case 0x42: { // i64.const
            let p = pos + 1;
            while (p < wasm.length) {
                const b = wasm[p++];
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            }
            return p - pos;
        }

        default:
            return 1;
    }
}

function readMemImmediate(pos: number): { align: number; offset: number } {
    let p = pos + 1;

    const [align, p1] = readULEB(p);
    const [offset, p2] = readULEB(p1);

    return {
        align,
        offset,
    };
}

function slotForOffset(offset: number): string | null {
    for (const s of slots) {
        if (offset === s.base) return `${s.name} word0`;
        if (offset === s.base + 8) return `${s.name} word1`;
    }

    return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 CONSUMER CONTEXT v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function 122 : 0x${hex(FUNC_START)} .. 0x${hex(FUNC_END)}`);
console.log("");

const hits: {
    pos: number;
    slot: string;
    offset: number;
}[] = [];

let p = FUNC_START;

while (p < FUNC_END) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (op === 0x29) {
        const mem = readMemImmediate(p);
        const slot = slotForOffset(mem.offset);

        if (slot) {
            hits.push({
                pos: p,
                slot,
                offset: mem.offset,
            });
        }
    }

    const len = instructionLength(p);

    if (len <= 0) {
        console.log(`STOP: invalid instruction length at 0x${hex(p)}`);
        break;
    }

    p += len;
}

console.log(`CONSUMER LOADS FOUND = ${hits.length}`);
console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < hits.length; i++) {
    const h = hits[i];

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `#${i + 1}  ${h.slot}  LOAD @ 0x${hex(h.pos)}  offset=${h.offset}`
    );

    const start = Math.max(FUNC_START, h.pos - 12);
    const end = Math.min(FUNC_END, h.pos + 28);

    let q = start;

    while (q < end) {
        const op = wasm[q];
        const len = instructionLength(q);

        if (len <= 0 || q + len > wasm.length) {
            console.log(`0x${hex(q)}  INVALID`);
            break;
        }

        let extra = "";

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x37) {
            const mem = readMemImmediate(q);
            extra = ` align=${mem.align} offset=${mem.offset}`;

            const s = slotForOffset(mem.offset);
            if (s) extra += `  [${s}]`;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const [fn] = readULEB(q + 1);
            extra = ` function=${fn}`;
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${hex(q)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(22)}${extra}`
        );

        q += len;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");
