import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FN = 87;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 INSPECTOR v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" WASM SECTION / CODE BODY EXTRACTION");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Target fn : ${TARGET_FN}`);
console.log("");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;

  while (true) {
    if (pos >= wasm.length)
      throw new Error("ULEB out of range");

    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7n;

    if (shift > 70n)
      throw new Error("ULEB too long");
  }
}

function H(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function sectionName(id: number) {
  switch (id) {
    case 0: return "custom";
    case 1: return "type";
    case 2: return "import";
    case 3: return "function";
    case 4: return "table";
    case 5: return "memory";
    case 6: return "global";
    case 7: return "export";
    case 8: return "start";
    case 9: return "element";
    case 10: return "code";
    case 11: return "data";
    case 12: return "data_count";
    default: return `section_${id}`;
  }
}

interface CodeBody {
  fn: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  localsStart: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
  size: number;
  bytes: Buffer;
}

let p = 8;

if (
  wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
  wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
  wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
  wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
  throw new Error("Not a WASM binary");
}

console.log("WASM header : OK");
console.log("");

let importedFunctions = 0;
let definedFunctions = 0;

const functionTypeIndices: number[] = [];
let targetCodeBody: CodeBody | null = null;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SECTIONS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const sectionAt = p;

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
  const sectionSize = Number(sizeInfo.value);
  const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  if (payloadEnd > wasm.length)
    throw new Error(`Section exceeds WASM @ ${H(sectionAt)}`);

  console.log(
    `${H(sectionAt)}  id=${id}  ${sectionName(id)}  size=${sectionSize}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // IMPORT SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (id === 2) {
    let q = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = uleb(q);
    const count = Number(countInfo.value);
    q = countInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const moduleLen = uleb(q);
      q = moduleLen.next + Number(moduleLen.value);

      const nameLen = uleb(q);
      q = nameLen.next + Number(nameLen.value);

      const kind = wasm[q++];

      switch (kind) {

        case 0x00: {
          // function import
          const typeInfo = uleb(q);
          q = typeInfo.next;

          importedFunctions++;
          break;
        }

        case 0x01: {
          // table
          q += 1;

          const flags = wasm[q++];

          const minInfo = uleb(q);
          q = minInfo.next;

          if (flags & 0x01) {
            const maxInfo = uleb(q);
            q = maxInfo.next;
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x02: {
          // memory
          const flags = wasm[q++];

          const minInfo = uleb(q);
          q = minInfo.next;

          if (flags & 0x01) {
            const maxInfo = uleb(q);
            q = maxInfo.next;
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x03: {
          // global
          q += 2;
          break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
          // tag
          const attr = wasm[q++];
          void attr;

          const typeInfo = uleb(q);
          q = typeInfo.next;

          break;
        }

        default:
          throw new Error(
            `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)} @ ${H(q - 1)}`
          );
      }
    }

    console.log(`  imported functions : ${importedFunctions}`);
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // FUNCTION SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (id === 3) {
    let q = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = uleb(q);
    const count = Number(countInfo.value);
    q = countInfo.next;

    definedFunctions = count;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const typeInfo = uleb(q);
      q = typeInfo.next;

      functionTypeIndices.push(Number(typeInfo.value));
    }

    console.log(`  defined functions  : ${definedFunctions}`);
    console.log(
      `  total functions    : ${importedFunctions + definedFunctions}`
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // CODE SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (id === 10) {
    let q = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = uleb(q);
    const count = Number(countInfo.value);
    q = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`  code bodies        : ${count}`);

    if (count !== definedFunctions) {
      console.log(
        `  WARNING: code bodies (${count}) != defined functions (${definedFunctions})`
      );
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const fn = importedFunctions + i;

      const bodySizeInfo = uleb(q);
      const bodySize = Number(bodySizeInfo.value);

      const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

      if (bodyEnd > payloadEnd)
        throw new Error(
          `Function body exceeds code section: fn=${fn}`
        );

      const body = wasm.subarray(bodyStart, bodyEnd);

      // local declarations:
      let r = bodyStart;

      const localDeclInfo = uleb(r);
      const localDeclCount = Number(localDeclInfo.value);
      r = localDeclInfo.next;

      for (let d = 0; d < localDeclCount; d++) {
        const nInfo = uleb(r);
        r = nInfo.next;

        // value type
        r++;
      }

      const codeStart = r;

      if (fn === TARGET_FN) {
        targetCodeBody = {
          fn,
          bodyStart,
          bodyEnd,
          localsStart: bodyStart,
          codeStart,
          codeEnd: bodyEnd,
          size: bodySize,
          bytes: Buffer.from(body)
        };
      }

      q = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

if (!targetCodeBody) {
  console.log("TARGET FUNCTION : NOT FOUND");
  console.log("");
  console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFunctions}`);
  console.log(`Defined functions  : ${definedFunctions}`);
  console.log("");
  throw new Error("Function 87 not found");
}

console.log(`Function          : ${targetCodeBody.fn}`);
console.log(`Body start        : ${H(targetCodeBody.bodyStart)}`);
console.log(`Body end          : ${H(targetCodeBody.bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`Body size         : ${targetCodeBody.size}`);
console.log(`Code start        : ${H(targetCodeBody.codeStart)}`);
console.log(`Code end          : ${H(targetCodeBody.codeEnd)}`);
console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 CODE BYTES");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

const code = wasm.subarray(
  targetCodeBody.codeStart,
  targetCodeBody.codeEnd
);

for (let i = 0; i < code.length; i += 16) {
  const addr = targetCodeBody.codeStart + i;

  const row = Array.from(
    code.subarray(i, Math.min(i + 16, code.length))
  )
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log(`${H(addr)}  ${row}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DIRECT CALL SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let q = targetCodeBody.codeStart;
let directCalls = 0;

while (q < targetCodeBody.codeEnd) {

  const at = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    const fn = Number(x.value);

    directCalls++;

    console.log(
      `CALL #${String(directCalls).padStart(2, " ")} @ ${H(at)} -> function ${fn}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.get/set/tee
  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // global.get/set
  if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    let x = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
      const b = wasm[q++];

      x |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

      if (!(b & 0x80))
        break;

      shift += 7;
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i64.const
  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // memory load/store memarg
  if (
    (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e)
  ) {
    const a = uleb(q);
    q = a.next;

    const o = uleb(q);
    q = o.next;

    continue;
  }

  // br_table
  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < Number(n.value) + 1; i++) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
    }

    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(q);
    q = a.next;

    const b = uleb(q);
    q = b.next;

    continue;
  }

  // memory.size / memory.grow
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    q++;
    continue;
  }

  // prefixed opcode
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    console.log(
      `PREFIX 0xfc @ ${H(at)} subopcode=${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // 0xfd SIMD prefix
  if (op === 0xfd) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    console.log(
      `PREFIX 0xfd @ ${H(at)} subopcode=${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // 0xfe atomic prefix
  if (op === 0xfe) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    console.log(
      `PREFIX 0xfe @ ${H(at)} subopcode=${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`Defined functions  : ${definedFunctions}`);
console.log(`Function 87 body   : ${targetCodeBody.size} bytes`);
console.log(`Direct CALLs       : ${directCalls}`);
console.log("");
console.log("STATUS             : FUNCTION 87 EXTRACTED");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunction9046Dispatch.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 9046;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT FUNC 9046 DISPATCH OBJECT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET FUNC :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function uleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
      }

      return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
    }

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readBytes(start, end) {
  const out = [];

  for (let i = start; i < end && i < buf.length; i++) {
    out.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return out.join(" ");
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Locate Code Section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;

let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const size = uleb(p);

  if (!size) {
    throw new Error("Failed reading section size at " + hex(p));
  }

  p = size.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + size.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log("CODE SECTION :", hex(codeStart), "..", hex(codeEnd));

  console.log("WARNING: expected 9398 bodies");

let target = null;

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {
  const sizeInfo = uleb(q);

  if (!sizeInfo) {

  }

  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  const bodySizePos = q;

  q = bodyEnd;

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Conservative instruction decoder
 *
 * We only need enough decoding to identify:
 *   local.get
 *   local.set
 *   local.tee
 *   i32.const
 *   i32.add
 *   i32.sub
 *   i32.load
 *   i32.store
 *   call
 *   call_indirect
 *   branches
 *
 * Unknown instructions are printed but do not terminate the
 * entire function. For every target store we also preserve
 * raw bytes.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function decodeOne(pos) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = buf[pos++];

  const ins = {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text: "unknown"
  };

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    const name =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.text = `${name} local=${a.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const a = sleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i32.const ${a.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const a = sleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i64.const ${a.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.text = `call ${a.value}`;
    ins.callTarget = a.value;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.text = `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    ins.text =
      `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = uleb(pos);
    if (!n) return null;

    pos = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
      const a = uleb(pos);
      if (!a) return null;
      pos = a.next;
    }

    ins.end = pos;
    ins.text = "br_table";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (pos >= target.bodyEnd) return null;

    /*
     * blocktype can be one byte for the forms expected here.
     */
    pos++;

    ins.end = pos;

    ins.text =
      op === 0x02 ? "block" :
      op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
      "if";

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    ins.text = "else";
    ins.end = pos;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.text = "end";
    ins.end = pos;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0f) {
    ins.text = "return";
    ins.end = pos;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x29) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x2c) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i32.load8_s align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    ins.isStore = true;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x37) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    ins.isStore = true;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i32.store8 align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    ins.isStore = true;
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x3b) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text = `i32.store16 align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
    ins.isStore = true;
    return ins;
  }

  const simple = {
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x67: "i32.clz",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    0x1b: "select",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u"
  };

  if (simple[op]) {
    ins.end = pos;
    ins.text = simple[op];
    return ins;
  }

  /*
   * Other common no-immediate instructions.
   */
  if (
    (op >= 0x1a && op <= 0x1c) ||
    (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0xc4)
  ) {
    ins.end = pos;
    ins.text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
    return ins;
  }

  /*
   * Unknown opcode: consume only opcode.
   */
  ins.end = pos;
  ins.text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;

  return ins;
}

/*
 * Decode function.
 */
const instructions = [];

x = x;

while (x < target.bodyEnd) {
  const ins = decodeOne(x);

  if (!ins) {
    console.log("DECODER FAILURE @", hex(x));
    break;
  }

  instructions.push(ins);
  x = ins.end;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Target stores
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const stores = instructions.filter(
  i => i.isStore &&
       (i.offset === 0 || i.offset === 4 || i.offset === 36)
);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET STORES");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("TOTAL +0/+4/+36 :", stores.length);

for (const s of stores) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    hex(s.start),
    s.text
  );

  const idx = instructions.indexOf(s);

  const from = Math.max(0, idx - 8);
  const to = Math.min(instructions.length, idx + 2);

  console.log("CONTEXT:");

  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
    const z = instructions[i];

    console.log(
      " ",
      hex(z.start),
      z.text
    );
  }

  const rawFrom = Math.max(target.bodyStart, s.start - 24);
  const rawTo = Math.min(target.bodyEnd, s.end + 8);

  console.log("RAW:");
  console.log(
    " ",
    readBytes(rawFrom, rawTo)
  );
}

  const constants = [];

  );

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32LE(b: Buffer, p: number): number {
    return b.readUInt32LE(p);
}

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }
}

function hex(b: Buffer, start: number, end: number): string {
    return b.subarray(start, end).toString("hex").match(/.{1,2}/g)?.join(" ") ?? "";
}

function readSections(b: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;
    const sections: {
        id: number;
        payload: number;
        size: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < b.length) {
        const id = b[p++];
        const s = readULEB(b, p);
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + s.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size: s.value,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function getSection(
    sections: ReturnType<typeof readSections>,
    id: number
) {
    const s = sections.find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!s) throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
    return s;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(b: Buffer, typeSection: ReturnType<typeof getSection>) {
    let p = typeSection.payload;

    const count = readULEB(b, p);
    p = count.next;

    const types: {
        params: number[];
        results: number[];
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const form = b[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)}`);
        }

        const pc = readULEB(b, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [...b.subarray(p, p + pc.value)];
        p += pc.value;

        const rc = readULEB(b, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [...b.subarray(p, p + rc.value)];
        p += rc.value;

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImportFunctionCount(
    b: Buffer,
    importSection: ReturnType<typeof getSection>
) {
    let p = importSection.payload;

    const count = readULEB(b, p);
    p = count.next;

    let functions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const ml = readULEB(b, p);
        p = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = readULEB(b, p);
        p = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = b[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const t = readULEB(b, p);
            p = t.next;
            functions++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 4;
            const limits = b[p - 1];

            if (limits & 1) {
                const a = readULEB(b, p);
                p = a.next;

                const m = readULEB(b, p);
                p = m.next;
            } else {
                const a = readULEB(b, p);
                p = a.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = b[p++];

            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const m = readULEB(b, p);
                p = m.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else if (kind === 4) {
            const a = readULEB(b, p);
            p = a.next;
            const b2 = readULEB(b, p);
            p = b2.next;
        }
    }

    return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypeIndices(
    b: Buffer,
    functionSection: ReturnType<typeof getSection>
) {
    let p = functionSection.payload;

    const count = readULEB(b, p);
    p = count.next;

    const types: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(b, p);
        p = x.next;
        types.push(x.value);
    }

    return types;
}

function parseCodeBodies(
    b: Buffer,
    codeSection: ReturnType<typeof getSection>
) {
    let p = codeSection.payload;

    const count = readULEB(b, p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: {
        start: number;
        codeStart: number;
        end: number;
        size: number;
        bytes: Buffer;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const sizePos = p;

        const s = readULEB(b, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = sizePos;
        const end = p + s.value;

        const localCount = readULEB(b, p);
        p = localCount.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(b, p);
            p = n.next;
            p += 1;
        }

        const codeStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            start,
            codeStart,
            end,
            size: s.value,
            bytes: b.subarray(start, end),
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function readI32Const(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    const x = readULEB(b, p);
    return {
        value: x.value | 0,
        next: x.next,
    };
}

function scanCalls(body: Buffer, codeStart: number) {
    const calls: {
        offset: number;
        target: number;
    }[] = [];

    /*
     * For this first pass we only report CALL opcodes.
     * The immediate following CALL index is decoded as ULEB.
     *
     * We also print the surrounding bytes so any ambiguous
     * occurrence can be checked directly against the body.
     */
    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
        if (body[i] !== 0x10) continue;

        try {
            const x = readULEB(body, i + 1);

            calls.push({
                offset: codeStart + i,
                target: x.value,
            });
        } catch {
            // ignore malformed candidate
        }
    }

    return calls;
}

function scanI32Consts(body: Buffer, codeStart: number) {
    const out: {
        offset: number;
        value: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
        if (body[i] !== 0x41) continue;

        try {
            const x = readI32Const(body, i + 1);

            out.push({
                offset: codeStart + i,
                value: x.value,
            });
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function inspectFunction(
    realIndex: number,
    importedFunctions: number,
    typeIndices: number[],
    bodies: ReturnType<typeof parseCodeBodies>,
    types: ReturnType<typeof parseFunctionTypes>
) {
    const bodyIndex = realIndex - importedFunctions;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` FUNCTION ${realIndex}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(`real function index : ${realIndex}`);
    console.log(`body index          : ${bodyIndex}`);

    if (bodyIndex < 0 || bodyIndex >= bodies.length) {
        console.log("ERROR: body index outside code section");
        return;
    }

    const typeIndex = typeIndices[bodyIndex];
    console.log(`type index           : ${typeIndex}`);

    if (types[typeIndex]) {
        console.log(`params               : ${types[typeIndex].params.length}`);
        console.log(`results              : ${types[typeIndex].results.length}`);
    }

    const body = bodies[bodyIndex];

    console.log(`body start           : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
    console.log(`code start           : 0x${body.codeStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
    console.log(`body end             : 0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
    console.log(`body size            : ${body.size}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("I32.CONST");

    const constants = scanI32Consts(
        body.bytes.subarray(body.codeStart - body.start),
        body.codeStart
    );

    for (const c of constants) {
        console.log(
            `  @0x${c.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} → ` +
            `${c.value} (0x${(c.value >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")})`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALL CANDIDATES");

    const calls = scanCalls(
        body.bytes.subarray(body.codeStart - body.start),
        body.codeStart
    );

    for (const c of calls) {
        console.log(
            `  @0x${c.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} → function ${c.target}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("BODY HEX");

    const code = body.bytes.subarray(body.codeStart - body.start);
    console.log(hex(code, 0, code.length));
}

async function main() {
    console.log("Using file: inspectFunctions122_1021V26");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 122 + 1021 INSPECTION v26");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    if (
        wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
        wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
        wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
        wasm[3] !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error("Not a WASM file");
    }

    const sections = readSections(wasm);

    console.log("");
    console.log("WASM SECTIONS");

    for (const s of sections) {
        console.log(
            `id=${s.id} payload=0x${s.payload.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            ` end=0x${s.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            ` size=${s.size}`
        );
    }

    const typeSection = getSection(sections, 1);
    const importSection = getSection(sections, 2);
    const functionSection = getSection(sections, 3);
    const codeSection = getSection(sections, 10);

    const types = parseFunctionTypes(wasm, typeSection);
    const importedFunctions = parseImportFunctionCount(
        wasm,
        importSection
    );

        wasm,
        functionSection
    );
    const bodies = parseCodeBodies(
        wasm,
        codeSection
    );

    console.log(`types               : ${types.length}`);
    console.log(`imported functions  : ${importedFunctions}`);
    console.log(`defined functions   : ${typeIndices.length}`);
    console.log(`code bodies         : ${bodies.length}`);

    console.log("");

    inspectFunction(
        122,

    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no TON");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no RPC");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no signing");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 0;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sid = wasm[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size.value
            };
        }

        p += size.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

p = count.next;

    index: number;
    type: number;
    mutable: boolean;
    init: string;
}

const globals: GlobalInfo[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const valueType = wasm[p++];

    const mut = wasm[p++];

    const mutable = mut === 1;

    const initStart = p;

    let init = "UNKNOWN";

    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        init = `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`;

    } else if (op === 0x42) {

        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        init = `i64.const ${x.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x43) {

        p += 4;

        init = "f32.const";

    } else if (op === 0x44) {

        p += 8;

        init = "f64.const";

    } else if (op === 0x23) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        init = `global.get ${x.value}`;

    } else {

        init = `opcode ${hex(op)}`;

        while (wasm[p] !== 0x0b && p < globalSec.end) {
            p++;
        }
    }

    if (wasm[p] === 0x0b) {
        p++;
    }

    globals.push({
        index: i,
        type: valueType,
        mutable,
        init
    });
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_IMPORT = 23;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORT FUNCTION #23 EXACT IDENTITY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function readName(pos: number) {
    const r = u32leb(pos);
    const start = r.next;
    const end = start + r.value;

    return {
        text: buf.subarray(start, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

let p = 8;

let importStart = -1;
let importEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p];

    const sizeInfo = u32leb(p + 1);

    const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === 2) {
        importStart = payloadStart;
        importEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (importStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("IMPORT SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(
    `IMPORT SECTION = ${hex(importStart)} .. ${hex(importEnd)}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = importStart;

const countInfo = u32leb(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const count = countInfo.value;

console.log(`IMPORT COUNT = ${count}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let functionIndex = 0;
let functionImports = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const module = readName(q);
    q = module.next;

    const name = readName(q);
    q = name.next;

    const kind = buf[q++];

    let detail = "";

    if (kind === 0x00) {
        const typeInfo = u32leb(q);
        q = typeInfo.next;

        detail = `FUNC TYPE INDEX = ${typeInfo.value}`;

        if (functionImports === TARGET_IMPORT) {
            console.log(`IMPORT ARRAY INDEX = ${i}`);
            console.log(`FUNCTION IMPORT # = ${functionImports}`);
            console.log(`GLOBAL FUNCTION   = ${functionImports}`);
            console.log(`MODULE            = ${module.text}`);
            console.log(`NAME              = ${name.text}`);
            console.log(`KIND              = FUNCTION`);
            console.log(detail);
            console.log(
                `RAW KIND BYTE     = 0x${kind.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

            break;
        }

        functionImports++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
        const elemType = buf[q++];

        const limitsFlag = buf[q++];

        const minInfo = u32leb(q);
        q = minInfo.next;

        let maxText = "none";

        if (limitsFlag & 0x01) {
            const maxInfo = u32leb(q);
            q = maxInfo.next;
            maxText = String(maxInfo.value);
        }

        detail =
            `TABLE elemType=0x${elemType.toString(16)} ` +
            `min=${minInfo.value} max=${maxText}`;
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
        const limitsFlag = buf[q++];

        const minInfo = u32leb(q);
        q = minInfo.next;

        let maxText = "none";

        if (limitsFlag & 0x01) {
            const maxInfo = u32leb(q);
            q = maxInfo.next;
            maxText = String(maxInfo.value);
        }

        detail =
            `MEMORY min=${minInfo.value} max=${maxText}`;
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
        const valueType = buf[q++];
        const mutability = buf[q++];

        detail =
            `GLOBAL type=0x${valueType.toString(16)} ` +
            `mutable=${mutability}`;
    } else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)} at import ${i}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectImport3.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 3;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readString(p) {
  const x = uleb(p);

  const start = x.next;
  const end = start + x.value;

  return {
    value: Buffer.from(bytes.subarray(start, end)).toString("utf8"),
    next: end
  };
}

function findImportSection() {

  let p = 8;

  while (p < bytes.length) {

    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + len.value;

    if (id === 2)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Import section not found");
}

function inspect() {

  const sec = findImportSection();

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functionIndex = 0;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("IMPORT FUNCTION INSPECTOR");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
  console.log(`TARGET    : Function ${TARGET}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const mod = readString(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {

      const type = uleb(p);
      p = type.next;

      console.log(
        `IMPORT #${functionIndex}  module=${mod.value}  name=${name.value}  type=${type.value}`
      );

      if (functionIndex === TARGET) {

        console.log("");
        console.log("TARGET FOUND");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`FUNCTION : ${functionIndex}`);
        console.log(`MODULE   : ${mod.value}`);
        console.log(`NAME     : ${name.value}`);
        console.log(`TYPE     : ${type.value}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("END");
        console.log("============================================================");

        return;
      }

      functionIndex++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {

      p++;

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      let x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      let x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {

      p += 2;
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    `Imported Function ${TARGET} not found`
  );
}

inspect();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectImport3Callers.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const TARGET_FUNCTION = 96;
const TARGET_CALL = 3;
const CONTEXT = 10;

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function uleb(p, end = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < end) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value, next: p };

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB at ' + hex(p));
    }

    throw new Error('Unexpected EOF at ' + hex(p));
}

function sleb(p, end) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    do {
        if (p >= end) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF at ' + hex(p));
        b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid SLEB at ' + hex(p));
    } while (b & 0x80);

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) value -= 2 ** shift;

    return { value, next: p };
}

function bytes(a, b) {
    return [...wasm.subarray(a, b)]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(' ');
}

function readSection(idWanted) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const size = uleb(p);
        const start = size.next;
        const end = start + size.value;

        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error('Invalid section bounds');

        if (id === idWanted) return { start, end };
        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error('Section not found: ' + idWanted);
}

const code = readSection(10);
const count = uleb(code.start, code.end);
let p = count.next;
const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(p, code.end);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > code.end) throw new Error('Invalid body ' + ordinal);

    bodies.set(IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal, { start, end });
    p = end;
}

const body = bodies.get(TARGET_FUNCTION);
if (!body) throw new Error('Function body not found');

const names = {
    0x00: 'unreachable', 0x01: 'nop',
    0x02: 'block', 0x03: 'loop', 0x04: 'if',
    0x05: 'else', 0x0b: 'end',
    0x0c: 'br', 0x0d: 'br_if', 0x0e: 'br_table',
    0x0f: 'return', 0x10: 'call', 0x11: 'call_indirect',
    0x1a: 'drop', 0x1b: 'select',
    0x20: 'local.get', 0x21: 'local.set', 0x22: 'local.tee',
    0x23: 'global.get', 0x24: 'global.set',
    0x28: 'i32.load', 0x29: 'i64.load',
    0x2a: 'f32.load', 0x2b: 'f64.load',
    0x36: 'i32.store', 0x37: 'i64.store',
    0x3f: 'memory.size', 0x40: 'memory.grow',
    0x41: 'i32.const', 0x42: 'i64.const',
    0x43: 'f32.const', 0x44: 'f64.const',
    0x45: 'i32.eqz', 0x46: 'i32.eq',
    0x47: 'i32.ne', 0x48: 'i32.lt_s',
    0x49: 'i32.lt_u', 0x4a: 'i32.gt_s',
    0x4b: 'i32.gt_u', 0x4c: 'i32.le_s',
    0x4d: 'i32.le_u', 0x4e: 'i32.ge_s',
    0x4f: 'i32.ge_u',
    0x6a: 'i32.add', 0x6b: 'i32.sub',
    0x6c: 'i32.mul', 0x71: 'i32.and',
    0x72: 'i32.or', 0x73: 'i32.xor',
    0x74: 'i32.shl', 0x75: 'i32.shr_s',
    0x76: 'i32.shr_u'
};

const memoryOps = new Set([
    0x28, 0x29, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x36, 0x37
]);

let q = body.start;
const localGroups = uleb(q, body.end);
q = localGroups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(q, body.end);
    q = n.next + 1;
    if (q > body.end) throw new Error('Invalid local declaration');
}

const instructions = [];

while (q < body.end) {
    const start = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];
    let text = names[op];

    
    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const sub = uleb(q, body.end);
        q = sub.next;

        const fcNames = {
            0: 'i32.trunc_sat_f32_s',
            1: 'i32.trunc_sat_f32_u',
            2: 'i32.trunc_sat_f64_s',
            3: 'i32.trunc_sat_f64_u',
            4: 'i64.trunc_sat_f32_s',
            5: 'i64.trunc_sat_f32_u',
            6: 'i64.trunc_sat_f64_s',
            7: 'i64.trunc_sat_f64_u',
            8: 'memory.init',
            9: 'data.drop',
            10: 'memory.copy',
            11: 'memory.fill',
            12: 'table.init',
            13: 'elem.drop',
            14: 'table.copy',
            15: 'table.grow',
            16: 'table.size',
            17: 'table.fill'
        };

        text = `0xfc.${sub.value} ${fcNames[sub.value] ?? 'unknown'}`;

        const readImm = () => {
            const v = uleb(q, body.end);
            q = v.next;
            return v.value;
        };

        if ([8, 10, 12, 14].includes(sub.value)) {
            text += ` ${readImm()} ${readImm()}`;
        } else if ([9, 11, 13, 15, 16, 17].includes(sub.value)) {
            text += ` ${readImm()}`;
        } else if (sub.value > 17) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported 0xfc subopcode ${sub.value} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
        }
    } else if (text === undefined) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported opcode 0x${op.toString(16)} at ${hex(start)}`
        );
    }


    if ([0x02, 0x03, 0x04].includes(op)) {
        const bt = wasm[q++];
        text += bt === 0x40 ? ' void' : ` blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`;
    } else if (
        [0x0c, 0x0d, 0x10, 0x20, 0x21, 0x22, 0x23, 0x24].includes(op)
    ) {
        const imm = uleb(q, body.end);
        q = imm.next;
        text += ' ' + imm.value;
    } else if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = uleb(q, body.end);
        const table = uleb(t.next, body.end);
        q = table.next;
        text += ` type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
    } else if (memoryOps.has(op)) {
        const align = uleb(q, body.end);
        const offset = uleb(align.next, body.end);
        q = offset.next;
        text += ` align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
    } else if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        const mem = uleb(q, body.end);
        q = mem.next;
        text += ` memory=${mem.value}`;
    } else if (op === 0x41) {
        const imm = sleb(q, body.end);
        q = imm.next;
        text += ` ${imm.value} (${hex(imm.value >>> 0)})`;
    } else if (op === 0x42) {
        const imm = sleb(q, body.end);
        q = imm.next;
        text += ` ${imm.value}`;
    } else if (op === 0x43) {
        text += ` bytes=${bytes(q, q + 4)}`;
        q += 4;
    } else if (op === 0x44) {
        text += ` bytes=${bytes(q, q + 8)}`;
        q += 8;
    }

    if (q > body.end) throw new Error('Instruction exceeds body at ' + hex(start));

    instructions.push({
        start,
        end: q,
        text,
        raw: bytes(start, q)
    });

    if (op === 0x0b && q === body.end) break;
}

console.log('ROOSTER WASM - IMPORT 3 CALL TRACE');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log('FUNCTION:', TARGET_FUNCTION);
console.log('BODY:', hex(body.start), '..', hex(body.end));
console.log('INSTRUCTIONS:', instructions.length);
console.log('----------------------------------------');

const matches = [];

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    if (ins.text === `call ${TARGET_CALL}`) {
        matches.push(i);
    }
}

if (matches.length === 0) {
    console.log('No call 3 found in this function.');
} else {
    for (const index of matches) {
        const from = Math.max(0, index - CONTEXT);

        console.log(`\nCALL 3 AT ${hex(instructions[index].start)}`);

        for (let j = from; j <= index; j++) {
            const ins = instructions[j];
            const marker = j === index ? ' >>>' : '    ';

            console.log(
                `${marker} ${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}` +
                `  | ${ins.raw}`
            );
        }

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
    }
}

console.log('\nTOTAL CALL 3 MATCHES:', matches.length);

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readString(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    const len = readULEB(buf, pos);
    const start = len.next;
    const end = start + len.value;

    return {
        value: buf.subarray(start, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" IMPORT #60 CONTEXT v36");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size: ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log();

let pos = 8;

let importSectionStart = -1;
let importSectionPayload = -1;
let importSectionSize = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    console.log(
        `section id=${sectionId} payload=0x${payloadStart.toString(16)} size=${sectionSize}`
    );

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        importSectionStart = payloadStart;
        importSectionPayload = payloadStart;
        importSectionSize = sectionSize;
        break;
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

if (importSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("Import section not found");
}

console.log();
console.log(
    `Import section: offset=0x${importSectionStart.toString(16)} size=${importSectionSize}`
);
console.log();

let p = importSectionPayload;

const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
const importCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`Import count: ${importCount}`);
console.log();

let functionImportIndex = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
    const moduleInfo = readString(buf, p);
    p = moduleInfo.next;

    const nameInfo = readString(buf, p);
    p = nameInfo.next;

    const kind = buf[p++];

    let detail = "";

    if (kind === 0x00) {
        const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = typeInfo.next;

        detail = `func type=${typeInfo.value}`;

        if (
            functionImportIndex >= 50 &&
            functionImportIndex <= 70
        ) {
            console.log(
                `import #${functionImportIndex}` +
                ` wasmImportEntry=${i}` +
                ` module="${moduleInfo.value}"` +
                ` name="${nameInfo.value}"` +
                ` kind=func` +
                ` type=${typeInfo.value}`
            );
        }

        functionImportIndex++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
        const elemType = buf[p++];
        const limitsFlag = buf[p++];

        const minInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = minInfo.next;

        let max = "";

        if (limitsFlag & 1) {
            const maxInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = maxInfo.next;
            max = ` max=${maxInfo.value}`;
        }

        detail =
            `table elemType=0x${elemType.toString(16)}` +
            ` min=${minInfo.value}${max}`;
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
        const limitsFlag = buf[p++];

        const minInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = minInfo.next;

        let max = "";

        if (limitsFlag & 1) {
            const maxInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = maxInfo.next;
            max = ` max=${maxInfo.value}`;
        }

        detail =
            `memory min=${minInfo.value}${max}`;
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
        const valueType = buf[p++];
        const mutability = buf[p++];

        detail =
            `global type=0x${valueType.toString(16)}` +
            ` mutable=${mutability}`;
    } else {
        detail = `unknown kind=0x${kind.toString(16)}`;
    }

    if (
        i < 5 ||
        (kind === 0x00 &&
            functionImportIndex >= 50 &&
            functionImportIndex <= 70)
    ) {
        // Already printed function imports above.
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `Function imports decoded: ${functionImportIndex}`
);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectImport66TypeV1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_IMPORT = 66;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p.v++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return result;
}

function readName(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): string {
    const len = readU32LEB(buf, p);

    const s = buf
        .subarray(p.v, p.v + len)
        .toString("utf8");

    p.v += len;

    return s;
}

function typeName(b: number): string {
    switch (b) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default:
            return `0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }
}

interface FuncType {
    params: string[];
    results: string[];
}

/* ============================================================
   FIND SECTIONS
   ============================================================ */

const sections: {
    id: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    payloadEnd: number;
}[] = [];

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];

    const p = { v: pos };
    const size = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    const payloadStart = p.v;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size;

    sections.push({
        id,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
    });

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

/* ============================================================
   TYPE SECTION
   ============================================================ */

const typeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);

if (!typeSection) {
    throw new Error("Type section not found");
}

const types: FuncType[] = [];

{
    const p = { v: typeSection.payloadStart };

    const count = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p.v++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported type form at ${i}: 0x${form.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const paramCount = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        const params: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(typeName(wasm[p.v++]));
        }

        const resultCount = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        const results: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(typeName(wasm[p.v++]));
        }

        types.push({
            params,
            results,
        });
    }
}

/* ============================================================
   IMPORT SECTION
   ============================================================ */

const importSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);

if (!importSection) {
    throw new Error("Import section not found");
}

const p = { v: importSection.payloadStart };

const importCount = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

let functionImportIndex = 0;

let targetTypeIndex = -1;
let targetModule = "";
let targetName = "";

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
    const moduleName = readName(wasm, p);
    const fieldName = readName(wasm, p);

    const kind = wasm[p.v++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
        const typeIndex = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        if (functionImportIndex === TARGET_IMPORT) {
            targetTypeIndex = typeIndex;
            targetModule = moduleName;
            targetName = fieldName;
        }

        functionImportIndex++;

    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
        /*
         * table
         */
        p.v++; // elem type

        const flags = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        if (flags & 1) {
            readU32LEB(wasm, p);
        }

    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
        /*
         * memory
         */
        const flags = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        readU32LEB(wasm, p);

        if (flags & 1) {
            readU32LEB(wasm, p);
        }

    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
        /*
         * global
         */
        p.v += 2;

    } else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unknown import kind: 0x${kind.toString(16)}`
        );
    }
}

/* ============================================================
   RESULT
   ============================================================ */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" IMPORT FUNCTION 66 - EXACT SIGNATURE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Import function index : ${TARGET_IMPORT}`);
console.log(`Module                : ${targetModule}`);
console.log(`Name                  : ${targetName}`);
console.log(`Type index            : ${targetTypeIndex}`);

if (targetTypeIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Target import not found.");
}

const sig = types[targetTypeIndex];

console.log(
    `Signature             : (${sig.params.join(", ")}) -> (${sig.results.join(", ")})`
);

console.log(`Parameter count       : ${sig.params.length}`);
console.log(`Result count          : ${sig.results.length}`);

console.log("");

if (sig.params.length === 0) {
    console.log("PARAMS : NONE");
} else {
    console.log("PARAMS:");

    for (let i = 0; i < sig.params.length; i++) {
        console.log(`  [${i}] ${sig.params[i]}`);
    }
}

console.log("");

if (sig.results.length === 0) {
    console.log("RESULTS : NONE");
} else {
    console.log("RESULTS:");

    for (let i = 0; i < sig.results.length; i++) {
        console.log(`  [${i}] ${sig.results[i]}`);
    }
}

console.log("");

console.log(" DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 65;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function str(pos) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  const start = n.next;
  const end = start + n.value;

  return {
    value: new TextDecoder().decode(b.slice(start, end)),
    next: end
  };
}

function findImportSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {

    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 2) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("IMPORT section not found");
}

const sec = findImportSection();

let p = sec.start;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 65 IMPORT INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`IMPORT COUNT : ${count.value}`);
console.log(`TARGET FUNC  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let funcIndex = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const module = str(p);
  p = module.next;

  const name = str(p);
  p = name.next;

  const kind = b[p++];

  if (kind === 0x00) {

    const type = uleb(p);
    p = type.next;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${funcIndex}  MODULE="${module.value}"  NAME="${name.value}"  TYPE=${type.value}`
    );

    if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {

      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("TARGET FOUND");
      console.log("============================================================");

      console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${funcIndex}`);
      console.log(`MODULE         : ${module.value}`);
      console.log(`IMPORT NAME    : ${name.value}`);
      console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${type.value}`);

      console.log("");
      console.log("IMPORTANT:");
      console.log("FUNC65 is IMPORTED.");
      console.log("Its implementation is NOT present in the WASM Code section.");
      console.log("");
      console.log("Therefore:");
      console.log("  FUNC107");
      console.log("    ↓");
      console.log("  CALL FUNC65");
      console.log("    ↓");
      console.log("  imported runtime function");
      console.log("    ↓");
      console.log("  return value");
      console.log("    ↓");
      console.log("  FUNC106");
      console.log("");
    }

    funcIndex++;
  }
  else if (kind === 0x01) {
    p += 1;
  }
  else if (kind === 0x02) {
    const q = uleb(p);
    p = q.next;

    const lim = b[p++];
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    if (lim === 1) {
      const m = uleb(p);
      p = m.next;
    }
  }
  else if (kind === 0x03) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next;

    const btype = b[p++];
  }
  else {
    throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
  }
}

FILE: scripts\inspectIndirect4790.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 91;
const TARGET_TABLE = 4790;
const TARGET_TYPE = 6;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error(`ULEB too large @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionStart = p;
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    if (sid === id) {
      return {
        sectionStart,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
        size,
      };
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

function readVectorCount(start: number) {
  return readU32Leb(wasm, start);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const importSection = findSection(2);
const functionSection = findSection(3);
const codeSection = findSection(10);

console.log("INSPECT INDIRECT TABLE[4790]");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET TABLE = ${TARGET_TABLE}`);
console.log(`TARGET FUNC  = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`TARGET TYPE  = ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
console.log();

let p = importSection.payloadStart;

let r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
const importCount = r.value;
p = r.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
  // module name
  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  const moduleLen = r.value;
  p = r.next + moduleLen;

  // field name
  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  const fieldLen = r.value;
  p = r.next + fieldLen;

  const kind = wasm[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    importedFunctions++;

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;
  } else if (kind === 1) {
    // table
    p += 1;

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;

    const flags = wasm[p - 1];

    if (flags & 1) {
      r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
      p = r.next;
    }

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;
  } else if (kind === 2) {
    // memory
    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    const flags = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = r.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
      r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
      p = r.next;
    }
  } else if (kind === 3) {
    // global
    p += 2;
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
  }
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION SECTION   = 0x${hex(functionSection.payloadStart)} .. 0x${hex(functionSection.payloadEnd)}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION        = 0x${hex(codeSection.payloadStart)} .. 0x${hex(codeSection.payloadEnd)}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION TYPE INDEX
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = functionSection.payloadStart;

r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
const definedCount = r.value;
p = r.next;

const definedOrdinal = TARGET_FUNC - importedFunctions;

if (definedOrdinal < 0 || definedOrdinal >= definedCount) {
  throw new Error(`Invalid defined ordinal ${definedOrdinal}`);
}

let targetType = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);

  if (i === definedOrdinal) {
    targetType = r.value;
  }

  p = r.next;
}

console.log("TARGET FUNCTION MAPPING");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC      = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`DEFINED ORDINAL  = ${definedOrdinal}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX       = ${targetType}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE BODIES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSection.payloadStart;

r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
const bodyCount = r.value;
p = r.next;

if (bodyCount !== definedCount) {
  throw new Error(
    `FUNCTION/CODE count mismatch: ${definedCount} vs ${bodyCount}`
  );
}

let targetBodyStart = -1;
let targetBodyEnd = -1;
let targetBodySize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const bodySizePos = p;

  r = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  const bodySize = r.value;
  p = r.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  if (i === definedOrdinal) {
    targetBodyStart = bodyStart;
    targetBodyEnd = bodyEnd;
    targetBodySize = bodySize;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (targetBodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Target body not found");
}

console.log("TARGET BODY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`BODY START = 0x${hex(targetBodyStart)}`);
console.log(`BODY END   = 0x${hex(targetBodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE  = ${targetBodySize}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// RAW BODY
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const body = wasm.subarray(targetBodyStart, targetBodyEnd);

console.log("RAW BODY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i += 16) {
  const chunk = body.subarray(i, Math.min(i + 16, body.length));

  const bytes = [...chunk]
    .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log(
    `+0x${i.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")}  ` +
    `0x${hex(targetBodyStart + i)}  ${bytes}`
  );
}

console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SIMPLE INSTRUCTION SCAN
// We only recognize enough opcodes to locate call_indirect.
// We DO NOT attempt to fully decode the WASM here.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("CALL_INDIRECT TYPE-6 CANDIDATES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let found = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
  if (body[i] !== 0x11) continue;

  let q = i + 1;

  try {
    const typeRead = readU32Leb(body, q);
    q = typeRead.next;

    const tableRead = readU32Leb(body, q);

    if (typeRead.value === TARGET_TYPE) {
      found++;

      console.log(
        `MATCH #${found}: ` +
        `body+0x${i.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")} ` +
        `ABS=0x${hex(targetBodyStart + i)} ` +
        `type=${typeRead.value} ` +
        `table=${tableRead.value}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(0, i - 24);
      const to = Math.min(body.length, q + 8);

      console.log("  CONTEXT:");

      for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
        const mark = j === i ? " <-- call_indirect" : "";

        console.log(
          `    +0x${j.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")} ` +
          `0x${hex(targetBodyStart + j)} ` +
          `${body[j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}${mark}`
        );
      }

      console.log();
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore bytes that are not safely decodable as call_indirect immediates.
  }
}

console.log(`TOTAL TYPE-6 CANDIDATES = ${found}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORTANT NOTE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("INTERPRETATION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("call_indirect does NOT contain the target function index.");
console.log("Its table index is taken from the WASM operand stack.");
console.log(`Therefore table[${TARGET_TABLE}] must be traced through the producer`);
console.log("instructions immediately before the matching call_indirect.");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectIndirect7969.ts
