import { readFileSync } from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_INDEX = 7969;

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    args?: any;
};

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number, bits = 32) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = buf[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < bits && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function nameOf(op: number) {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    };

    return m[op] ??
        `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeBody(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];

    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const offset = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let args: any = undefined;

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);

            args = {
                local: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);

            args = {
                global: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            args = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: b.value,
            };

            p = b.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, p, 32);

            args = {
                value: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, p, 64);

            args = {
                value: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);

            args = {
                func: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            args = {
                type: a.value,
                table: b.value,
            };

            p = b.next;
        }

        out.push({
            offset,
            op,
            name: nameOf(op),
            args,
        });

        if (op === 0x0b) break;
    }

    return out;
}

function readBodies(buf: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + size;

        if (id === 10) {
            let q = payload;

            const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            q = countInfo.next;

            const bodies: any[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
                const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
                q = sizeInfo.next;

                const bodyStart = q;
                const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

                let x = bodyStart;

                const localInfo = readULEB(buf, x);
                const localCount = localInfo.value;
                x = localInfo.next;

                for (let j = 0; j < localCount; j++) {
                    const n = readULEB(buf, x);
                    x = n.next;

                    x++;
                }

                bodies.push({
                    func: 83 + i,
                    instrStart: x,
                    instrEnd: bodyEnd,
                });

                q = bodyEnd;
            }

            if (q !== end) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Code mismatch: 0x${q.toString(16)} != 0x${end.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            return bodies;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found.");
}

function fmt(i: Instr) {
    let s =
        `0x${i.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
        i.name;

    if (!i.args) return s;

    if (
        i.name === "local.get" ||
        i.name === "local.set" ||
        i.name === "local.tee"
    ) {
        s += ` ${i.args.local}`;
    }

    else if (i.name === "call") {
        s += ` ${i.args.func}`;
    }

    else if (
        i.name === "i32.const" ||
        i.name === "i64.const"
    ) {
        s += ` ${i.args.value}`;
    }

    else if (i.name === "call_indirect") {
        s +=
            ` type=${i.args.type}` +
            ` table=${i.args.table}`;
    }

    else if (
        i.name === "i32.load" ||
        i.name === "i64.load" ||
        i.name === "i32.load8_s" ||
        i.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
        i.name === "i32.load16_s" ||
        i.name === "i32.load16_u"
    ) {
        s +=
            ` align=${i.args.align}` +
            ` offset=${i.args.offset}`;
    }

    return s;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" INSPECT ALL TYPE0/TABLE0 INDIRECT DISPATCHES");
    console.log(" TARGET TABLE INDEX = 7969");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    const wasm = readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target    : ${TARGET_INDEX}`);
    console.log("");

    const bodies = readBodies(wasm);

    let n = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const instr = decodeBody(
            wasm,
            body.instrStart,
            body.instrEnd
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < instr.length; i++) {
            const x = instr[i];

            if (
                x.name !== "call_indirect" ||
                x.args?.type !== 0 ||
                x.args?.table !== 0
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            n++;

            console.log("============================================================");
            console.log(`DISPATCH #${n}`);
            console.log("============================================================");

            console.log(
                `Function : ${body.func}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Offset   : 0x${x.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
            );

            console.log("");

            /*
             * Show a wider producer window.
             *
             * We deliberately do NOT claim this proves the value.
             * It only identifies the immediate computation feeding
             * the indirect call.
             */

            const from = Math.max(0, i - 14);

            for (let j = from; j <= i; j++) {
                console.log(
                    (j === i ? ">>> " : "    ") +
                    fmt(instr[j])
                );
            }

            console.log("");

            /*
             * Look for especially interesting instructions.
             */

            const window =
                instr.slice(from, i);

            const interesting = window.filter(
                z =>
                    z.name === "i32.const" ||
                    z.name === "i32.load" ||
                    z.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
                    z.name === "i32.load8_s" ||
                    z.name === "i32.load16_u" ||
                    z.name === "i32.load16_s" ||
                    z.name === "local.get" ||
                    z.name === "call" ||
                    z.name === "i32.add" ||
                    z.name === "i32.sub" ||
                    z.name === "i32.mul"
            );

            console.log("PRODUCER CANDIDATES:");

            if (interesting.length === 0) {
                console.log("  none");
            } else {
                for (const z of interesting) {
                    console.log(
                        `  ${fmt(z)}`
                    );
                }
            }

            console.log("");

            /*
             * Exact 7969 nearby.
             */

            const exact = window.filter(
                z =>
                    z.name === "i32.const" &&
                    z.args?.value === TARGET_INDEX
            );

            console.log(
                `Nearby CONST(7969) : ${exact.length}`
            );

            console.log("");
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `Type0/table0 dispatches inspected : ${n}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target table index                : ${TARGET_INDEX}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target function                   : 4815`
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "IMPORTANT:"
    );

    console.log(
        "This report identifies candidate producer paths."
    );

    console.log(
        "It does not yet prove runtime index = 7969."
    );

    console.log(
        "The next step is to dataflow the strongest candidates."
    );
}

FILE: scripts\inspectIndirectCallFunctions.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const FILE = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS_EXPECTED = 83;
const TARGETS = [4095, 4096, 4097, 4098, 4099, 4100, 4101, 4102, 4103, 4104, 4105, 4106, 4107, 4108, 4109, 4110, 4111, 4112, 4113, 4114, 4115];

function uleb(buf, s) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (s.pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[s.pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) return value;
    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 49) {
      throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF reading ULEB");
}

function sleb(buf, s) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  do {
    if (s.pos >= buf.length) throw new Error("Unexpected EOF reading SLEB");
    b = buf[s.pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return value;
}

function readName(buf, s) {
  const len = uleb(buf, s);
  const end = s.pos + len;

  if (end > buf.length) throw new Error("Invalid name length");

  const value = buf.toString("utf8", s.pos, end);
  s.pos = end;
  return value;
}

function valueType(t) {
  const names = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
    0x7b: "v128",
    0x70: "funcref",
    0x6f: "externref"
  };

  return names[t] ?? `type_0x${t.toString(16)}`;
}

function parseSections(buf) {
  const sections = new Map();
  const s = { pos: 8 };

  while (s.pos < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[s.pos++];
    const size = uleb(buf, s);
    const start = s.pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (end > buf.length) throw new Error("Section exceeds file");

    sections.set(id, { start, end, size });
    s.pos = end;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseTypes(buf, section) {
  if (!section) return [];

  const s = { pos: section.start };
  const count = uleb(buf, s);
  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const form = buf[s.pos++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unsupported function type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at ${s.pos - 1}`
      );
    }

    const paramCount = uleb(buf, s);
    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
      params.push(valueType(buf[s.pos++]));
    }

    const resultCount = uleb(buf, s);
    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
      results.push(valueType(buf[s.pos++]));
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }

  return types;
}

function parseImportedFunctionTypes(buf, section) {
  if (!section) return [];

  const s = { pos: section.start };
  const count = uleb(buf, s);
  const functionTypes = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    readName(buf, s);
    readName(buf, s);

    const kind = buf[s.pos++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      functionTypes.push(uleb(buf, s));
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      s.pos++; // element type
      const flags = uleb(buf, s);
      uleb(buf, s); // minimum
      if (flags & 1) uleb(buf, s); // maximum
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = uleb(buf, s);
      uleb(buf, s); // minimum
      if (flags & 1) uleb(buf, s); // maximum
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      s.pos += 2; // value type and mutability
    } else if (kind === 4) {
      uleb(buf, s); // tag attribute
      uleb(buf, s); // tag type index
    } else {
      throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
    }
  }

  return functionTypes;
}

function parseDefinedFunctionTypes(buf, section) {
  if (!section) return [];

  const s = { pos: section.start };
  const count = uleb(buf, s);
  const indices = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    indices.push(uleb(buf, s));
  }

  return indices;
}

function parseBodies(buf, section, importedCount) {
  if (!section) return [];

  const s = { pos: section.start };
  const count = uleb(buf, s);
  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const bodySize = uleb(buf, s);
    const start = s.pos;
    const end = start + bodySize;

    if (end > section.end) throw new Error("Function body exceeds code section");

    bodies.push({
      fn: importedCount + i,
      start,
      end,
      size: bodySize
    });

    s.pos = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function dumpBytes(buf, start, end) {
  for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
    const q = Math.min(p + 16, end);
    const bytes = [];

    for (let i = p; i < q; i++) {
      bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(
      `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
  }
}

function main() {
  const buf = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

  if (
    buf.subarray(0, 4).toString("hex") !== "0061736d"
  ) {
    throw new Error("File does not have a WASM magic header");
  }

  const sections = parseSections(buf);
  const types = parseTypes(buf, sections.get(1));
  const importedTypes = parseImportedFunctionTypes(buf, sections.get(2));
  const definedTypes = parseDefinedFunctionTypes(buf, sections.get(3));
  const bodies = parseBodies(buf, sections.get(10), importedTypes.length);

  const allTypes = [...importedTypes, ...definedTypes];
  const bodyByFunction = new Map(bodies.map(b => [b.fn, b]));

  console.log("INDIRECT CALL FUNCTION INSPECTION");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${buf.length}`);
  console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedTypes.length}`);
  console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedTypes.length}`);
  console.log(`TOTAL FUNCTION BODIES = ${bodies.length}`);
  console.log(`TYPE COUNT = ${types.length}`);
  console.log("");

  if (importedTypes.length !== IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS_EXPECTED) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING: expected ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS_EXPECTED} imports, found ${importedTypes.length}`
    );
    console.log("");
  }

  for (const fn of TARGETS) {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNCTION ${fn}`);

    const typeIndex = allTypes[fn];

    if (typeIndex === undefined) {
      console.log("TYPE INDEX NOT FOUND");
    } else {
      const t = types[typeIndex];

      console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${typeIndex}`);

      if (t) {
        console.log(`PARAMETERS = (${t.params.join(", ")})`);
        console.log(`RESULTS = (${t.results.join(", ")})`);
      } else {
        console.log("TYPE SIGNATURE NOT FOUND");
      }
    }

    const body = bodyByFunction.get(fn);

    if (!body) {
      console.log("NO DEFINED FUNCTION BODY");
      console.log("");
      continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY START = 0x${body.start.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`BODY END   = 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`BODY SIZE  = ${body.size}`);
    console.log("RAW BODY:");
    dumpBytes(buf, body.start, body.end);
    console.log("");
  }

  console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
}

try {
  main();
} catch (error) {
  console.error("FAILED:", error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectIndirectTarget.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);
const TARGET = 2034;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

function uleb(p, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0, shift = 0;
    while (p < limit) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 127) * 2 ** shift;
        if (!(b & 128)) return { value, next: p };
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    }
    throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in ULEB');
}

function sleb(p, limit) {
    let value = 0, shift = 0, b;
    do {
        if (p >= limit) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in SLEB');
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 127) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid SLEB');
    } while (b & 128);
    if (shift < 32 && (b & 64)) value |= (-1 << shift);
    return { value, next: p };
}

function sections() {
    const result = new Map();
    let p = 8;
    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const s = uleb(p);
        const start = s.next;
        const end = start + s.value;
        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error('Section exceeds file');
        if (!result.has(id)) result.set(id, []);
        result.get(id).push({ start, end });
        p = end;
    }
    return result;
}

function readName(p, end) {
    const n = uleb(p, end);
    return { value: n.value, next: n.next + n.value };
}

const sec = sections();
const typeSection = sec.get(1)?.[0];
const functionSection = sec.get(3)?.[0];
const elementSections = sec.get(9) ?? [];

if (!typeSection || !functionSection) {
    throw new Error('Missing type or function section');
}

// Parse function types.
let p = typeSection.start;
const typeCount = uleb(p, typeSection.end);
p = typeCount.next;
const types = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
    if (wasm[p++] !== 0x60) throw new Error(`Unexpected type form ${i}`);

    const pc = uleb(p, typeSection.end);
    p = pc.next;
    const params = [...wasm.subarray(p, p + pc.value)];
    p += pc.value;

    const rc = uleb(p, typeSection.end);
    p = rc.next;
    const results = [...wasm.subarray(p, p + rc.value)];
    p += rc.value;

    types.push({ params, results });
}

// Function section gives type indices for defined functions.
p = functionSection.start;
const funcCount = uleb(p, functionSection.end);
p = funcCount.next;
const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount.value; i++) {
    const t = uleb(p, functionSection.end);
    p = t.next;
    functionTypes.push(t.value);
}

const ordinal = TARGET - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;
const targetTypeIndex = functionTypes[ordinal];

if (targetTypeIndex === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET} is outside defined function range`);
}

const targetType = types[targetTypeIndex];

function sig(t) {
    const names = {
        0x7f: 'i32', 0x7e: 'i64',
        0x7d: 'f32', 0x7c: 'f64',
        0x7b: 'v128', 0x70: 'funcref', 0x6f: 'externref'
    };
    return `(${t.params.map(x => names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(', ')}) -> ` +
        `(${t.results.map(x => names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(', ')})`;
}

// Parse static element segments, including legacy function-index segments.
const matches = [];
const segmentInfo = [];

for (const es of elementSections) {
    let q = es.start;
    const count = uleb(q, es.end);
    q = count.next;

    for (let s = 0; s < count.value; s++) {
        const flags = uleb(q, es.end);
        q = flags.next;

        if (flags.value === 0) {
            // offset expression, normally i32.const <offset>; end
            if (wasm[q++] !== 0x41) throw new Error('Unexpected elem offset expr');
            const offset = sleb(q, es.end);
            q = offset.next;
            if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error('Missing offset end');

            const n = uleb(q, es.end);
            q = n.next;
            const funcs = [];

            for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
                const f = uleb(q, es.end);
                q = f.next;
                funcs.push(f.value);
                if (f.value === TARGET) {
                    matches.push({ segment: s, slot: offset.value + j });
                }
            }

            segmentInfo.push({
                segment: s,
                flags: 0,
                offset: offset.value,
                entries: funcs.length
            });
        } else {
            console.log(`Element segment ${s}: flags=${flags.value}; not decoded by this checker`);
            throw new Error('Non-legacy element segment encountered; stopping safely');
        }
    }
}

console.log('STATIC INDIRECT-TARGET CHECK — READ ONLY');
console.log(`Target function: ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`Target type index: ${targetTypeIndex}`);
console.log(`Target signature: ${sig(targetType)}`);
console.log(`Defined functions: ${funcCount.value}`);
console.log(`Element segments: ${segmentInfo.length}`);

for (const s of segmentInfo) {
    console.log(`Segment ${s.segment}: flags=${s.flags}, offset=${s.offset}, entries=${s.entries}`);
}

console.log(
    matches.length
        ? `TARGET FOUND IN ELEMENT TABLE: ${JSON.stringify(matches)}`
        : 'TARGET NOT FOUND IN STATIC ELEMENT SEGMENTS'
);

const compatible = types
    .map((t, i) => ({ type: t, index: i }))
    .filter(x =>
        JSON.stringify(x.type.params) === JSON.stringify(targetType.params) &&
        JSON.stringify(x.type.results) === JSON.stringify(targetType.results)
    )
    .map(x => x.index);

console.log(`Compatible call_indirect type indices: ${compatible.join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
console.log('No WASM code executed; no network or transactions used.');

import { Address, fromNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBsGx9ArADUrREB34W-ghgsCgBShvfUr4Jvlu-0KGc33Rbt'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi ROOSTER/pTON INITIAL LIQUIDITY CHECK');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');

    const router = DEX.v1.Router.create(ROUTER);
    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Pool address
    // ------------------------------------------------

    const poolAddress =
        await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                token1: PTON_MASTER,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL ADDRESS');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 2. ROOSTER user wallet
    // ------------------------------------------------

    const roosterWalletResult =
        await provider
            .provider(ROOSTER_MASTER)
            .get('get_wallet_address', [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: addressToCell(USER_WALLET),
                },
            ]);

    const roosterWallet =
        roosterWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER ROOSTER WALLET');
    console.log(roosterWallet.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 3. pTON user wallet
    // ------------------------------------------------

    const ptonWalletResult =
        await provider
            .provider(PTON_MASTER)
            .get('get_wallet_address', [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: addressToCell(USER_WALLET),
                },
            ]);

    const ptonWallet =
        ptonWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER pTON WALLET');
    console.log(ptonWallet.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 4. ROOSTER balance
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BALANCE');

    try {
        const data =
            await provider
                .provider(roosterWallet)
                .get('get_wallet_data', []);

        const balance = data.stack.readBigNumber();

        console.log(
            'Raw:',
            balance.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Human:',
            Number(balance) / 1_000_000_000,
            'ROOSTER'
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'Could not read ROOSTER wallet balance.'
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 5. pTON wallet state
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON WALLET STATE');

    try {
        const state =
            await provider
                .provider(ptonWallet)
                .getState();

        console.log(
            'State:',
            state.state.type
        );

        if (state.state.type === 'uninit') {
            console.log(
                'pTON wallet is not initialized yet.'
            );
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'Could not read pTON wallet state.'
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 6. User TON balance
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER TON BALANCE');

    try {
        const state =
            await provider
                .provider(USER_WALLET)
                .getState();

        console.log(
            'Account state:',
            state.state.type
        );

        if (state.balance !== undefined) {
            console.log(
                'TON:',
                fromNano(state.balance),
                'TON'
            );

            console.log(
                'nanoTON:',
                state.balance.toString()
            );
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'Could not read TON balance.'
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 7. Pool state
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL STATE');

    try {
        const poolData =
            await provider
                .provider(poolAddress)
                .get('get_pool_data', []);

        console.log(
            'Pool is executable.'
        );

        console.log(
            'Stack:',
            poolData.stack
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'Pool is currently not executable/deployed.'
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 8. Router state
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER STATE');

    try {
        const routerData =
            await router
                .getRouterData(routerProvider);

        console.log(
            'Router data successfully read.'
        );

        console.log(routerData);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'Could not read Router data.'
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // Final
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' INITIAL LIQUIDITY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');
    console.log('NO TON SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER SENT.');
    console.log('NO pTON CREATED.');
    console.log('');
}

function addressToCell(address: Address) {
    const { beginCell } = require('@ton/core');

    return beginCell()
        .storeAddress(address)
        .endCell();
}

FILE: scripts\inspectInitialRoosterPool.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const filePath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        'initial_rooster_pool_body.boc'
    );

    console.log('===== ROOSTER INITIAL LIQUIDITY DECODER =====');
    console.log('File:', filePath);

    const bocBase64 = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8').trim();

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(bocBase64, 'base64')
    );

    const root = cells[0];

    console.log('\n===== JETTON TRANSFER =====');

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'Jetton amount:',
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount:',
        Number(amount) / 1e9
    );

    const destination = s.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        destination.toString()
    );

    const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'Response destination:',
        responseDestination.toString()
    );

    const customPayload = s.loadMaybeRef();

    console.log(
        'Custom payload:',
        customPayload ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'Forward TON amount:',
        forwardTonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward TON:',
        Number(forwardTonAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining root bits:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining root refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    if (s.remainingBits < 1) {
        throw new Error(
            'No forward payload flag found.'
        );
    }

    const forwardPayloadIsRef = s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'Forward payload:',
        forwardPayloadIsRef ? 'REF' : 'INLINE'
    );

    let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {
        if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {
            throw new Error(
                'Forward payload says REF but no ref exists.'
            );
        }

        forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
    } else {
        forwardPayload = s.asCell();
    }

    if (!forwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'Forward payload not found.'
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== FORWARD PAYLOAD =====');

    console.log(
        'Bits:',
        forwardPayload.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Refs:',
        forwardPayload.refs.length
    );

    const fp = forwardPayload.beginParse();

    if (fp.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const forwardOpcode = fp.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            'Forward opcode:',
            '0x' +
            forwardOpcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log(
            'Forward opcode decimal:',
            forwardOpcode
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'Remaining bits:',
        fp.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining refs:',
        fp.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('\n===== COMPLETE CELL TREE =====');

    function dumpCell(
        cell: Cell,
        level: number,
        index: string
    ) {
        const indent = ' '.repeat(level * 2);

        console.log(
            `${indent}[${index}] bits=${cell.bits.length} refs=${cell.refs.length}`
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            dumpCell(
                cell.refs[i],
                level + 1,
                `${index}.${i}`
            );
        }
    }

    dumpCell(root, 0, '0');

    console.log('\nNO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectInvokeTargets.ts
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const WASM_PATH = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS_EXPECTED = 83;
const TARGET_TYPE_INDEX = 0;

const TARGETS = [
    295,
    484,
    871,
    560,
    1746,
    2530,
    2527,
    1393,
    1747,
];

class Reader {
    data: Buffer;
    pos: number;
    end: number;

    constructor(data: Buffer, start = 0, end = data.length) {
        this.data = data;
        this.pos = start;
        this.end = end;
    }

    byte(): number {
        if (this.pos >= this.end) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF at 0x${this.pos.toString(16)}`);
        }
        return this.data[this.pos++];
    }

    uleb(): number {
        let result = 0;
        let shift = 0;

        while (true) {
            const b = this.byte();
            result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

            if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                return result;
            }

            shift += 7;
            if (shift > 49) {
                throw new Error("ULEB128 is too large");
            }
        }
    }

    sleb(): number {
        let result = 0;
        let shift = 0;
        let b = 0;

        while (true) {
            b = this.byte();
            result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
            shift += 7;

            if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

            if (shift > 35) {
                throw new Error("SLEB128 is too large");
            }
        }

        if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40) !== 0) {
            result |= (~0 << shift);
        }

        return result;
    }

    string(): string {
        const length = this.uleb();
        const start = this.pos;
        this.pos += length;

        if (this.pos > this.end) {
            throw new Error("String extends beyond section");
        }

        return this.data.toString("utf8", start, this.pos);
    }

    skip(count: number): void {
        this.pos += count;
        if (this.pos > this.end) {
            throw new Error("Read extends beyond section");
        }
    }
}

type FuncType = {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
};

type SectionInfo = {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
};

function readSections(data: Buffer): SectionInfo[] {
    const r = new Reader(data);

    if (
        r.byte() !== 0x00 ||
        r.byte() !== 0x61 ||
        r.byte() !== 0x73 ||
        r.byte() !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error("Not a WebAssembly binary");
    }

    const version = [
        r.byte(),
        r.byte(),
        r.byte(),
        r.byte(),
    ];

    if (version.join(",") !== "1,0,0,0") {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected WASM version: ${version.join(",")}`);
    }

    const sections: SectionInfo[] = [];

    while (r.pos < r.end) {
        const id = r.byte();
        const size = r.uleb();
        const start = r.pos;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > r.end) {
            throw new Error(`Section ${id} exceeds file bounds`);
        }

        sections.push({ id, start, end });
        r.pos = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function sectionReader(
    data: Buffer,
    sections: SectionInfo[],
    id: number
): Reader | undefined {
    const section = sections.find((s) => s.id === id);
    if (!section) return undefined;

    return new Reader(data, section.start, section.end);
}

function readTypes(
    data: Buffer,
    sections: SectionInfo[]
): FuncType[] {
    const r = sectionReader(data, sections, 1);
    if (!r) return [];

    const count = r.uleb();
    const types: FuncType[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = r.byte();

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Unsupported function type form 0x${form.toString(16)}`);
        }

        const paramCount = r.uleb();
        const params: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(r.byte());
        }

        const resultCount = r.uleb();
        const results: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(r.byte());
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    return types;
}

function readImportedFunctionTypes(
    data: Buffer,
    sections: SectionInfo[]
): number[] {
    const r = sectionReader(data, sections, 2);
    if (!r) return [];

    const importCount = r.uleb();
    const functionTypes: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
        r.string(); // module
        r.string(); // import name

        const kind = r.byte();

        switch (kind) {
            case 0:
                functionTypes.push(r.uleb());
                break;

            case 1: {
                r.byte(); // element type
                const flags = r.uleb();
                r.uleb(); // minimum
                if (flags & 1) r.uleb(); // maximum
                break;
            }

            case 2: {
                const flags = r.uleb();
                r.uleb(); // minimum
                if (flags & 1) r.uleb(); // maximum
                break;
            }

            case 3:
                r.byte(); // value type
                r.byte(); // mutability
                break;

            case 4:
                r.uleb(); // attribute
                r.uleb(); // type index
                break;

            default:
                throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }
    }

    return functionTypes;
}

function readDefinedFunctionTypes(
    data: Buffer,
    sections: SectionInfo[]
): number[] {
    const r = sectionReader(data, sections, 3);
    if (!r) return [];

    const count = r.uleb();
    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        result.push(r.uleb());
    }

    return result;
}

/**
 * Reads a common constant-expression form used for element offsets.
 * Returns null when the expression is valid but not a simple i32.const.
 */
function readOffsetExpression(r: Reader): number | null {
    const opcode = r.byte();
    let value: number | null = null;

    if (opcode === 0x41) {
        value = r.sleb();
    } else if (opcode === 0x23) {
        r.uleb(); // global.get index
    } else if (opcode === 0x43) {
        r.skip(4); // f32.const
    } else if (opcode === 0x44) {
        r.skip(8); // f64.const
    } else if (opcode === 0xd0) {
        r.byte(); // heap type (signed LEB encoding may be multi-byte)
        // For the offsets used in this file, this is not expected.
    } else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported element offset expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const endOpcode = r.byte();

    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected end of offset expression, got 0x${endOpcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    return value;
}

function readElementKindOrType(r: Reader, expressionForm: boolean): void {
    if (expressionForm) {
        r.byte(); // reference type, e.g. funcref
    } else {
        r.byte(); // elemkind, normally 0
    }
}

function readFunctionIndexVector(r: Reader): number[] {
    const count = r.uleb();
    const funcs: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        funcs.push(r.uleb());
    }

    return funcs;
}

function readExpressionVector(r: Reader): Array<number | null> {
    const count = r.uleb();
    const funcs: Array<number | null> = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        let functionIndex: number | null = null;

        while (true) {
            const opcode = r.byte();

            if (opcode === 0x0b) {
                break;
            }

            if (opcode === 0xd2) {
                functionIndex = r.uleb(); // ref.func
            } else if (opcode === 0xd0) {
                // ref.null has a heap-type immediate.
                // Read its signed LEB value.
                r.sleb();
            } else {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unsupported element expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                );
            }
        }

        funcs.push(functionIndex);
    }

    return funcs;
}

function readElementTables(
    data: Buffer,
    sections: SectionInfo[]
): Array<Array<number | undefined>> {
    const r = sectionReader(data, sections, 9);
    const tables: Array<Array<number | undefined>> = [];

    if (!r) return tables;

    const segmentCount = r.uleb();

    for (let segment = 0; segment < segmentCount; segment++) {
        const flags = r.uleb();

        let active = false;
        let tableIndex = 0;
        let offset: number | null = null;
        let functions: Array<number | null> = [];

        switch (flags) {
            case 0: {
                active = true;
                tableIndex = 0;
                offset = readOffsetExpression(r);
                functions = readFunctionIndexVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 1: {
                readElementKindOrType(r, false);
                functions = readFunctionIndexVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 2: {
                active = true;
                tableIndex = r.uleb();
                offset = readOffsetExpression(r);
                readElementKindOrType(r, false);
                functions = readFunctionIndexVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 3: {
                readElementKindOrType(r, false);
                functions = readFunctionIndexVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 4: {
                active = true;
                tableIndex = 0;
                offset = readOffsetExpression(r);
                functions = readExpressionVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 5: {
                readElementKindOrType(r, true);
                functions = readExpressionVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 6: {
                active = true;
                tableIndex = r.uleb();
                offset = readOffsetExpression(r);
                readElementKindOrType(r, true);
                functions = readExpressionVector(r);
                break;
            }

            case 7: {
                readElementKindOrType(r, true);
                functions = readExpressionVector(r);
                break;
            }

            default:
                throw new Error(`Unsupported element segment flags ${flags}`);
        }

        if (!active) continue;

        if (offset === null) {
            console.log(
                `WARNING: Element segment ${segment} has a non-constant offset; skipped`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (!tables[tableIndex]) tables[tableIndex] = [];

        const table = tables[tableIndex];

        for (let i = 0; i < functions.length; i++) {
            const func = functions[i];

            if (func !== null) {
                table[offset + i] = func;
            }
        }
    }

    return tables;
}

function formatValType(type: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x7f: "i32",
        0x7e: "i64",
        0x7d: "f32",
        0x7c: "f64",
        0x7b: "v128",
        0x70: "funcref",
        0x6f: "externref",
    };

    return names[type] ?? `type_0x${type.toString(16)}`;
}

function formatSignature(type: FuncType): string {
    const params = type.params.map(formatValType).join(", ");
    const results = type.results.map(formatValType).join(", ");

    return `(${params}) -> (${results})`;
}

function main(): void {
    console.log("WASM TABLE / FUNCTION TYPE INSPECTION");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM file not found: ${WASM_PATH}`);
    }

    const data = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);
    const sections = readSections(data);
    const types = readTypes(data, sections);
    const importedFunctionTypes = readImportedFunctionTypes(data, sections);
    const definedFunctionTypes = readDefinedFunctionTypes(data, sections);
    const tables = readElementTables(data, sections);

    const importedFunctionCount = importedFunctionTypes.length;

    console.log(`WASM FILE          = ${WASM_PATH}`);
    console.log(`FILE SIZE          = ${data.length}`);
    console.log(`TYPE COUNT         = ${types.length}`);
    console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedFunctionCount}`);
    console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS  = ${definedFunctionTypes.length}`);
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC RANGE  = ${importedFunctionCount} .. ${
        importedFunctionCount + definedFunctionTypes.length - 1
    }`);

    if (importedFunctionCount !== IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS_EXPECTED) {
        console.log(
            `WARNING: Expected ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS_EXPECTED} imported functions, found ${importedFunctionCount}`
        );
    }

    function getTypeIndex(globalFunction: number): number | undefined {
        if (globalFunction < importedFunctionCount) {
            return importedFunctionTypes[globalFunction];
        }

        const ordinal = globalFunction - importedFunctionCount;

        if (ordinal < 0 || ordinal >= definedFunctionTypes.length) {
            return undefined;
        }

        return definedFunctionTypes[ordinal];
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`TYPE ${TARGET_TYPE_INDEX}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------");

    const targetType = types[TARGET_TYPE_INDEX];

    if (targetType) {
        console.log(`Signature: ${formatSignature(targetType)}`);
    } else {
        console.log("WARNING: Target type index does not exist");
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS MATCHING TYPE 0");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------");

    let matchingFunctionCount = 0;

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedFunctionTypes.length; ordinal++) {
        if (definedFunctionTypes[ordinal] !== TARGET_TYPE_INDEX) continue;

        const globalFunction = importedFunctionCount + ordinal;

        console.log(`GLOBAL ${globalFunction} | ORDINAL ${ordinal}`);
        matchingFunctionCount++;
    }

    console.log(`MATCH COUNT: ${matchingFunctionCount}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGET FUNCTION SIGNATURES");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------");

    for (const globalFunction of TARGETS) {
        const typeIndex = getTypeIndex(globalFunction);

        console.log("");
        console.log(`FUNCTION ${globalFunction}`);

        if (typeIndex === undefined) {
            console.log("  Type index : UNKNOWN");
            console.log("  Signature  : UNKNOWN");
            continue;
        }

        console.log(`  Type index : ${typeIndex}`);

        const type = types[typeIndex];

        if (type) {
            console.log(`  Signature  : ${formatSignature(type)}`);
        } else {
            console.log("  Signature  : TYPE NOT FOUND");
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("ELEMENT TABLE LOOKUP");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------");

    const lookups = [0x0a6e, 0x0cdd, 0x2163, 0x21d2];

    for (const index of lookups) {
        const func = tables[0]?.[index];

        if (func === undefined) {
            console.log(`TABLE[${index}] = UNDEFINED`);
            continue;
        }

        const typeIndex = getTypeIndex(func);
        console.log(
            `TABLE[${index}] = GLOBAL ${func} | TYPE ${
                typeIndex === undefined ? "UNKNOWN" : typeIndex
            }`
        );
    }

    // IMPORTANT:
    // This scan must stay inside main(), because it uses tables and
    // getTypeIndex(), which are local to main().
    console.log("");
    console.log("TABLE 0 ENTRIES WITH TYPE 0");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------");

    let matchingEntries = 0;

    const table0 = tables[0] ?? [];

    for (let index = 0; index < table0.length; index++) {
        const func = table0[index];

        if (func === undefined) continue;

        const typeIndex = getTypeIndex(func);

        if (typeIndex === TARGET_TYPE_INDEX) {
            console.log(`TABLE[${index}] = GLOBAL ${func}`);
            matchingEntries++;
        }
    }

    console.log(`MATCHING TABLE ENTRIES: ${matchingEntries}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (error) {
    console.error("");
    console.error("SCRIPT ERROR");
    console.error("------------------------------------------------");
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const rooster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const pton = Address.parse(
        'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
    );

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER / pTON MASTER INSPECTION');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log('==========================================');

    for (const [name, address] of [
        ['ROOSTER', rooster],
        ['pTON', pton],
    ] as const) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(`${name} Master:`);
        console.log(address.toString());

        const state = await provider.provider(address).getState();

        console.log(`${name} State:`);
        console.log('type:', state.state.type);
        console.log('balance:', state.balance);

        if (state.state.type === 'active') {
            console.log(
                'code bytes:',
                state.state.code ? state.state.code.length : 0
            );

            console.log(
                'data bytes:',
                state.state.data ? state.state.data.length : 0
            );
        } else {
            console.log('code bytes: N/A');
            console.log('data bytes: N/A');
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectKaCritical.ts

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const TARGETS = [
    5539, // Ka
    618,
    106,
    95,
    170,
];

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

let p = 8;

function readULEB(): number {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return value >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readULEBAt(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let i = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[i++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: i,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

let importedFuncs = 0;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const sectionSize = readULEB();

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        const count = readULEB();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            const moduleLen = readULEB();
            p += moduleLen;

            const nameLen = readULEB();
            p += nameLen;

            const kind = b[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                readULEB();
                importedFuncs++;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                p++;
                const flags = readULEB();
                readULEB();

                if (flags & 1) {
                    readULEB();
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = readULEB();
                readULEB();

                if (flags & 1) {
                    readULEB();
                }
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

p = codeStart;

const functionCount = readULEB();

interface Func {
    index: number;
    definedIndex: number;
    offset: number;
    size: number;
    body: Buffer;
}

const funcs: Func[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEBAt(p);

    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const offset = p;

    const body = b.subarray(
        offset,
        offset + sizeInfo.value
    );

    funcs.push({
        index: importedFuncs + i,
        definedIndex: i,
        offset,
        size: sizeInfo.value,
        body,
    });

    p += sizeInfo.value;
}

function readCalls(body: Buffer) {
    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {

        if (body[i] !== 0x10) {
            continue;
        }

        let value = 0;
        let shift = 0;
        let j = i + 1;

        while (j < body.length) {

            const x = body[j++];

            value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

            if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
                break;
            }

            shift += 7;
        }

        result.push(value >>> 0);

        i = j - 1;
    }

    return result;
}

function opcodeName(x: number): string {

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: 'unreachable',
        0x01: 'nop',
        0x02: 'block',
        0x03: 'loop',
        0x04: 'if',
        0x05: 'else',
        0x0b: 'end',
        0x0c: 'br',
        0x0d: 'br_if',
        0x0e: 'br_table',
        0x0f: 'return',
        0x10: 'call',
        0x11: 'call_indirect',
        0x1a: 'drop',
        0x20: 'local.get',
        0x21: 'local.set',
        0x22: 'local.tee',
        0x23: 'global.get',
        0x24: 'global.set',
        0x28: 'i32.load',
        0x29: 'i64.load',
        0x2c: 'i32.load8_s',
        0x2d: 'i32.load8_u',
        0x36: 'i32.store',
        0x37: 'i64.store',
        0x41: 'i32.const',
        0x42: 'i64.const',
        0x45: 'i32.eqz',
        0x46: 'i32.eq',
        0x47: 'i32.ne',
        0x48: 'i32.lt_s',
        0x49: 'i32.lt_u',
        0x4a: 'i32.gt_s',
        0x4b: 'i32.gt_u',
        0x4c: 'i32.le_s',
        0x4d: 'i32.le_u',
        0x4e: 'i32.ge_s',
        0x4f: 'i32.ge_u',
        0x6a: 'i32.add',
        0x6b: 'i32.sub',
        0x6c: 'i32.mul',
        0x6d: 'i32.div_s',
        0x6e: 'i32.div_u',
        0x6f: 'i32.rem_s',
        0x70: 'i32.rem_u',
        0x71: 'i32.and',
        0x72: 'i32.or',
        0x73: 'i32.xor',
        0x74: 'i32.shl',
        0x75: 'i32.shr_s',
        0x76: 'i32.shr_u',
        0x77: 'i32.rotl',
        0x78: 'i32.rotr',
        0x79: 'i64.extend_i32_s',
        0x7c: 'i64.add',
        0x7d: 'i64.sub',
        0x7e: 'i64.mul',
        0xa7: 'i32.wrap_i64',
    };

    return names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    if (target < importedFuncs) {
        console.log(`target=${target}: IMPORT`);
        continue;
    }

    const f = funcs[target - importedFuncs];

    if (!f) {
        console.log(`target=${target}: NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(`FUNCTION ${target}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(`defined index : ${f.definedIndex}`);
    console.log(`offset        : ${f.offset}`);
    console.log(`size          : ${f.size}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log('BYTES:');

    console.log(
        [...f.body]
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('CALLS:');

    console.log(
        readCalls(f.body).join(', ') || 'none'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('IMPORTANT OPCODES:');

    for (let i = 0; i < f.body.length; i++) {

        const x = f.body[i];

        if (
            x === 0x10 ||
            x === 0x11 ||
            x === 0x20 ||
            x === 0x21 ||
            x === 0x22 ||
            x === 0x23 ||
            x === 0x24 ||
            x === 0x28 ||
            x === 0x29 ||
            x === 0x2c ||
            x === 0x2d ||
            x === 0x36 ||
            x === 0x37 ||
            x === 0x41 ||
            x === 0x42 ||
            x === 0x0f
        ) {
            console.log(
                `${i.toString().padStart(4, '0')}  ${opcodeName(x)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('DONE');
console.log('============================================================');

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectKaHelperBodies.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const TARGETS = [
    83,
    95,
    106,
    114,
    170,
    618,
    1075,
    1481,
    1910
];

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

let p = 8;

function readU32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        result |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function skipLimits() {
    const flags = readU32();
    readU32();

    if (flags & 1) {
        readU32();
    }
}

let importedFuncs = 0;
let definedFuncTypes: number[] = [];
let codeSectionOffset = 0;
let codeSectionSize = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const sectionSize = readU32();

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        const count = readU32();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const moduleLen = readU32();
            p += moduleLen;

            const nameLen = readU32();
            p += nameLen;

            const kind = b[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                readU32();
                importedFuncs++;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                p++;
                skipLimits();
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                skipLimits();
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 3) {
        const count = readU32();

        definedFuncTypes = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            definedFuncTypes.push(readU32());
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeSectionOffset = start;
        codeSectionSize = sectionSize;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' Ka HELPER BODY INSPECTOR');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFuncs}`);
console.log(`Defined functions  : ${definedFuncTypes.length}`);
console.log(`Code section       : ${codeSectionOffset}`);
console.log(`Code size          : ${codeSectionSize}`);
console.log('');

let q = codeSectionOffset;

const functionCount = readU32At(q);
q += u32LengthAt(q);

interface Body {
    definedIndex: number;
    functionIndex: number;
    offset: number;
    size: number;
    body: Buffer;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEBAt(q);
    q = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = q;

    const body = b.subarray(
        bodyStart,
        bodyStart + sizeInfo.value
    );

    bodies.push({
        definedIndex: i,
        functionIndex: importedFuncs + i,
        offset: bodyStart,
        size: sizeInfo.value,
        body
    });

    q += sizeInfo.value;
}

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    if (target < importedFuncs) {
        continue;
    }

    const definedIndex = target - importedFuncs;
    const body = bodies[definedIndex];

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`target=${target} NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `=== target=${target}  defined #${definedIndex} ===`
    );

    console.log(`offset : ${body.offset}`);
    console.log(`size   : ${body.size}`);

    const calls: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < body.body.length - 1; i++) {
        if (body.body[i] === 0x10) {

            const result = readULEBFromBuffer(
                body.body,
                i + 1
            );

            calls.push(result.value);

            i += result.length;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `CALLS (${calls.length}): ${
            calls.length ? calls.join(', ') : 'none'
        }`
    );

    const previewLength = Math.min(
        body.body.length,
        160
    );

    console.log(
        'FIRST BYTES:'
    );

    console.log(
        [...body.body.subarray(0, previewLength)]
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ')
    );
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' DONE');
console.log('============================================================');

function readU32At(pos: number): number {
    const r = readULEBAt(pos);
    return r.value;
}

function u32LengthAt(pos: number): number {
    return readULEBAt(pos).length;
}

function readULEBAt(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    length: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let i = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[i++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                length: i - pos,
                next: i
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readULEBFromBuffer(
    buf: Buffer,
    pos: number
): {
    value: number;
    length: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let i = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = buf[i++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                length: i - pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectKaNextLayer.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const TARGETS = [
    12,
    32,
    34,
    65,
    70,
    76,
    95,
    114,
    259,
    327,
    618,
    106,
    170,
    1910,
    1481,
    1075,
    2431,
    2485
];

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

let p = 8;

function readULEB(): {
    value: number;
    length: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let length = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        length++;

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                length
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readULEBAt(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let i = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[i++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: i
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function skipName() {
    const x = readULEB();
    p += x.value;
}

function skipLimits() {
    const flags = readULEB().value;
    readULEB();

    if (flags & 1) {
        readULEB();
    }
}

let importedFuncs = 0;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB();
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {

        const count = readULEB().value;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            skipName();
            skipName();

            const kind = b[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                readULEB();
                importedFuncs++;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                p++;
                skipLimits();
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                skipLimits();
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = sectionStart;
        break;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

p = codeStart;

const functionCount = readULEB().value;

interface FuncBody {
    index: number;
    offset: number;
    size: number;
    bytes: Buffer;
}

const funcs: FuncBody[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEBAt(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const offset = p;

    const bytes = b.subarray(
        offset,
        offset + sizeInfo.value
    );

    funcs.push({
        index: importedFuncs + i,
        offset,
        size: sizeInfo.value,
        bytes
    });

    p += sizeInfo.value;
}

function readCallTargets(bytes: Buffer): number[] {

    const calls: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {

        if (bytes[i] !== 0x10) {
            continue;
        }

        let value = 0;
        let shift = 0;
        let j = i + 1;

        while (j < bytes.length) {

            const x = bytes[j++];

            value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

            if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
                break;
            }

            shift += 7;
        }

        calls.push(value >>> 0);

        i = j - 1;
    }

    return calls;
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' Ka NEXT-LAYER CALL GRAPH');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFuncs}`);
console.log(`Defined functions  : ${functionCount}`);
console.log('');

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    const f = funcs[target - importedFuncs];

    if (!f) {
        console.log(`target=${target} NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    const calls = readCallTargets(f.bytes);

    console.log(
        `target=${target.toString().padStart(4, ' ')}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  defined #${(target - importedFuncs)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  offset=${f.offset} size=${f.size}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  CALLS (${calls.length}): ${
            calls.length ? calls.join(', ') : 'none'
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `  FIRST: ${
            [...f.bytes.subarray(0, Math.min(80, f.bytes.length))]
                .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
                .join(' ')
        }`
    );

    console.log('');
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' DONE');
console.log('============================================================');
