import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

// ============================================================
// STON.FI v2.2 KNOWN POOL INSPECTOR
// READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT
// ============================================================

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROOSTER_WALLET_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const PTON_WALLET_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

// ============================================================
// GENERIC CONTRACT STATE INSPECTOR
// ============================================================

async function inspectContract(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    label: string,
    address: Address
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));
    console.log(` ${label}`);
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));

    console.log('ADDRESS:');
    console.log(address.toString());

    try {
        const contractState =
            await provider.provider(address).getState();

        console.log('');
        console.log('STATE TYPE:', contractState.state.type);

        console.log(
            'BALANCE:',
            contractState.balance.toString()
        );

        if (contractState.state.type === 'active') {
            console.log('CODE: PRESENT');
            console.log('DATA: PRESENT');

            console.log(
                'CODE BYTES:',
                contractState.state.code?.length ?? 0
            );

            console.log(
                'DATA BYTES:',
                contractState.state.data?.length ?? 0
            );
        } else if (contractState.state.type === 'uninit') {
            console.log('CODE: MISSING');
            console.log('DATA: MISSING');
        } else if (contractState.state.type === 'frozen') {
            console.log('CODE: NOT AVAILABLE');
            console.log('DATA: NOT AVAILABLE');

            console.log(
                'STATE HASH:',
                contractState.state.stateHash.toString('hex')
            );
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(`${label} ERROR:`);

        if (error instanceof Error) {
            console.log(error.message);
        } else {
            console.log(error);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// READ STON.FI v2.2 POOL DATA
// ============================================================

async function inspectPoolData(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL DATA');
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));

    try {
        const result = await provider
            .provider(POOL_ADDRESS)
            .get('get_pool_data', []);

        const stack = result.stack;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Decode the common PoolData fields first.
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        const isLocked = stack.readBoolean();

        const routerAddress = stack.readAddress();

        const totalSupply = stack.readBigNumber();

        const reserve0 = stack.readBigNumber();

        const reserve1 = stack.readBigNumber();

        const token0WalletAddress = stack.readAddress();

        const token1WalletAddress = stack.readAddress();

        const lpFee = stack.readNumber();

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // OUTPUT
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('IS LOCKED:');
        console.log(isLocked);

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROUTER FROM POOL:');
        console.log(routerAddress.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOTAL LP SUPPLY:');
        console.log(totalSupply.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('RESERVE 0:');
        console.log(reserve0.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('RESERVE 1:');
        console.log(reserve1.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOKEN 0 WALLET:');
        console.log(token0WalletAddress.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOKEN 1 WALLET:');
        console.log(token1WalletAddress.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('LP FEE:');
        console.log(lpFee);

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // ADDRESS CHECKS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(70));
        console.log(' ADDRESS CHECKS');
        console.log('-'.repeat(70));

        console.log(
            'ROUTER MATCH:',
            routerAddress.equals(ROUTER_ADDRESS)
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        console.log(
            'TOKEN 0 WALLET MATCH:',
            token0WalletAddress.equals(
                ROOSTER_WALLET_ADDRESS
            )
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        console.log(
            'TOKEN 1 WALLET MATCH:',
            token1WalletAddress.equals(
                PTON_WALLET_ADDRESS
            )
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // HUMAN READABLE VALUES
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(70));
        console.log(' HUMAN READABLE RESERVES');
        console.log('-'.repeat(70));

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER RESERVE:',
            Number(reserve0) / 1e9,
            'ROOSTER'
        );

        console.log(
            'PTON RESERVE:',
            Number(reserve1) / 1e9,
            'pTON'
        );

        console.log(
            'LP TOTAL SUPPLY:',
            totalSupply.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'LP FEE RAW:',
            lpFee
        );

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Remaining stack items
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('REMAINING STACK ITEMS:');

        try {
            console.log(stack.remaining);
        } catch {
            console.log('Unable to inspect remaining stack items.');
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('GET_POOL_DATA ERROR:');

        if (error instanceof Error) {
            console.log(error.message);
        } else {
            console.log(error);
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 KNOWN POOL INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_WALLET_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('PTON WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET_ADDRESS.toString());

    // ========================================================
    // ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    await inspectContract(
        provider,
        'ROUTER STATE',
        ROUTER_ADDRESS
    );

    // ========================================================
    // POOL
    // ========================================================

    await inspectContract(
        provider,
        'POOL STATE',
        POOL_ADDRESS
    );

    // ========================================================
    // POOL DATA
    // ========================================================

    await inspectPoolData(provider);

    // ========================================================
    // ROOSTER JETTON WALLET
    // ========================================================

    await inspectContract(
        provider,
        'ROOSTER WALLET STATE',
        ROOSTER_WALLET_ADDRESS
    );

    // ========================================================
    // PTON JETTON WALLET
    // ========================================================

    await inspectContract(
        provider,
        'PTON WALLET STATE',
        PTON_WALLET_ADDRESS
    );

    // ========================================================
    // FINISH
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('');
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
    LiteEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

const LIBRARY_HASHES = [
    'fa0d2b89d20ae82e5f5544eff4d621224573519899c0c98f6a1ced78b5c2ddf3',
    '71c21694f438d1654643be842afe867093c0e0042e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041',
    'b2c1e13ce2bca3c512a47f691c90184ac132f496d6e2c0ae63609860b4fb1495',
    'f2e4bddb5902e0cf5a89e59a386d0097f48ec2a644ad47b37f146563b69e8250',
];

const servers = [
    {
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
    },
    {
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
    },
];

export async function run() {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('   ROUTER LIBRARIES MASTERCHAIN TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const key = Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64');

        console.log('Adding:', server.host);
        console.log('Key bytes:', key.length);

        if (key.length !== 32) {
            console.log('INVALID KEY');
            return;
        }

        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: server.host,
                publicKey: key,
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== GET MASTERCHAIN INFO =====');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Masterchain seqno:', master.last.seqno);
        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            master.last.rootHash.toString('hex')
        );
        console.log(
            'File hash:',
            master.last.fileHash.toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log('       CHECKING 4 ROUTER LIBRARIES');
        console.log('========================================');

        for (let i = 0; i < LIBRARY_HASHES.length; i++) {

            const hashHex = LIBRARY_HASHES[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`===== LIBRARY [${i}] =====`);
            console.log('Hash:', hashHex);

            const hash = Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex');

            try {

                const result = await client.getLibraries([hash]);

                console.log('');
                console.log('RAW RESULT:');
                console.dir(result, {
                    depth: 15,
                });

                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT TYPE:');
                console.log(typeof result);

                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT KEYS:');

                if (
                    result !== null &&
                    typeof result === 'object'
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        Object.keys(result)
                    );
                } else {
                    console.log('Not an object');
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT JSON:');

                try {
                    console.log(
                        JSON.stringify(
                            result,
                            (_key, value) => {
                                if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) {
                                    return {
                                        type: 'Buffer',
                                        data: Array.from(value),
                                    };
                                }

                                return value;
                            },
                            2
                        )
                    );
                } catch {
                    console.log(
                        'JSON serialization failed'
                    );
                }

            } catch (error) {

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `ERROR FOR LIBRARY [${i}]`
                );

                console.error(error);
            }
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== MASTERCHAIN ERROR =====');
        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('          TEST FINISHED');
    console.log('          NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('========================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLibraryServers.ts

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

export async function run() {

    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
        'hex'
    );

    const servers = [
        {
            name: 'SERVER 2',
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            name: 'SERVER 3',
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    console.log('===== DIRECT LIBRARY SERVER TEST =====');
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Library hash:',
        libraryHash.toString('hex')
    );
    console.log('');

    for (const server of servers) {

        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(server.name);
        console.log(server.host);
        console.log('========================================');

        const key = Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64');

        console.log('Key bytes:', key.length);

        if (key.length !== 32) {
            console.log('INVALID KEY');
            continue;
        }

        const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: server.host,
            publicKey: key,
            reconnectTimeout: 5000,
        });

        try {

            console.log('Waiting for engine...');

            let ready = false;

            for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {

                const isReady = engine.isReady();
                const isClosed = engine.isClosed();

                console.log(
                    `Wait ${i + 1}/30 | ready=${isReady} | closed=${isClosed}`
                );

                if (isReady) {
                    ready = true;
                    break;
                }

                if (isClosed) {
                    console.log(
                        'Engine closed before becoming ready.'
                    );
                    break;
                }

                await sleep(500);
            }

            if (!ready) {

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'ENGINE NOT READY - SKIPPING SERVER'
                );

                continue;
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('ENGINE READY');
            console.log('');

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine,
                batchSize: 1,
            });

            console.log(
                'Getting masterchain info...'
            );

            const master =
                await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log(
                'Masterchain seqno:',
                master.last.seqno
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Calling getLibraries()...'
            );

            const result =
                await client.getLibraries([
                    libraryHash
                ]);

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== RAW RESULT =====');

            console.dir(result, {
                depth: 10
            });

            console.log('');

            if (
                !result.result ||
                result.result.length === 0
            ) {

                console.log(
                    '>>> LIBRARY NOT RETURNED'
                );

            } else {

                console.log(
                    '>>> LIBRARY FOUND'
                );

                console.log(
                    'Result count:',
                    result.result.length
                );

                for (
                    let i = 0;
                    i < result.result.length;
                    i++
                ) {

                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        '----- LIBRARY ENTRY',
                        i,
                        '-----'
                    );

                    console.dir(
                        result.result[i],
                        {
                            depth: 10
                        }
                    );
                }
            }

        } catch (error) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== ERROR =====');
            console.error(error);

        } finally {

            console.log('');
            console.log('Closing engine...');
            engine.close();
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('TEST FINISHED');
    console.log('========================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLiquidityBodiesV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parseRaw(
    '0:92e1411ae546892f33b2c8a89ea90390d8ff4cfbb917a643b91e73f706fdb9d2'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const POOL = Address.parseRaw(
    '0:f900b682b84a89ecc8bdaa55ce03b04732b4eaf1ed9f67907fb5e58dd7b319ab'
);

const PROVIDE_LP = 935368415;
const TON_TRANSFER = 32736093;

function addressToString(address: Address | null | undefined): string {
    return address ? address.toString() : 'null';
}

function decodeProvideLp(cell: Cell): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(' PROVIDE_LP FORWARD PAYLOAD');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log('Opcode:', opcode);
        console.log(
            'Opcode hex:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        if (opcode !== PROVIDE_LP) {
            console.log('WARNING: opcode is NOT PROVIDE_LP');
            return;
        }

        const routerWalletAddress = s.loadAddress();
        const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();
        const excessesAddress = s.loadAddress();

        const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Router wallet:');
        console.log(addressToString(routerWalletAddress));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Refund address:');
        console.log(addressToString(refundAddress));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Excesses address:');
        console.log(addressToString(excessesAddress));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Deadline:');
        console.log(deadline.toString());

        const params = s.loadRef().beginParse();

        const minLpOut = params.loadCoins();
        const receiverAddress = params.loadAddress();
        const bothPositive = params.loadBit();

        const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
            params.loadCoins();

        const hasCustomPayload = params.loadBit();

        console.log('');
        console.log('minLpOut:');
        console.log(minLpOut.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('Receiver:');
        console.log(addressToString(receiverAddress));

        console.log('');
        console.log('bothPositive:');
        console.log(bothPositive);

        console.log('');
        console.log('DEX custom payload forward gas:');
        console.log(
            dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Has custom payload:');
        console.log(hasCustomPayload);

        if (hasCustomPayload) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('Custom payload exists: YES');
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('Custom payload exists: NO');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('Provide LP payload BOC:');
        console.log(cell.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('ERROR decoding PROVIDE_LP payload:');
        console.log(error);
    }
}

function decodePtonBody(body: Cell): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' pTON BODY DECODE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
        const tonAmount = s.loadCoins();
        const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();

        const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

        console.log('');
        console.log('TON_TRANSFER opcode:');
        console.log(opcode);

        console.log(
            'Opcode hex:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Expected TON_TRANSFER:');
        console.log(TON_TRANSFER);

        console.log(
            'Expected hex:',
            '0x' + TON_TRANSFER.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Query ID:');
        console.log(queryId.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TON amount:');
        console.log(tonAmount.toString());

        console.log(
            'TON amount display:',
            Number(tonAmount) / 1e9,
            'TON'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Refund address:');
        console.log(addressToString(refundAddress));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Has forward payload:');
        console.log(hasForwardPayload);

        if (hasForwardPayload) {
            const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

            decodeProvideLp(forwardPayload);
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('ERROR: No forward payload found.');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('pTON body BOC:');
        console.log(body.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('ERROR decoding pTON body:');
        console.log(error);
    }
}

function decodeJettonBody(body: Cell): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER JETTON BODY DECODE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
        const amount = s.loadCoins();

        const destination = s.loadAddress();
        const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

        const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

        if (hasCustomPayload) {
            s.loadRef();
        }

        const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

        const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

        console.log('');
        console.log('Jetton opcode:');
        console.log(opcode);

        console.log(
            'Opcode hex:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Query ID:');
        console.log(queryId.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOSTER amount raw:');
        console.log(amount.toString());

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER amount display:',
            Number(amount) / 1e9
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Destination:');
        console.log(addressToString(destination));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Response destination:');
        console.log(addressToString(responseDestination));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Has custom payload:');
        console.log(hasCustomPayload);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Forward TON amount raw:');
        console.log(forwardTonAmount.toString());

        console.log(
            'Forward TON amount:',
            Number(forwardTonAmount) / 1e9,
            'TON'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Has forward payload:');
        console.log(hasForwardPayload);

        if (hasForwardPayload) {
            const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

            decodeProvideLp(forwardPayload);
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('ERROR: No forward payload found.');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOSTER body BOC:');
        console.log(body.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('ERROR decoding ROOSTER body:');
        console.log(error);
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('       STON.FI V2.2 LIQUIDITY BODY INSPECTOR');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const proxyTon =
        DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const poolAddress = POOL;

    console.log('===== KNOWN V2.2 POOL =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    console.log('');

    const pool =
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(poolAddress);

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(poolAddress);

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log('===== CURRENT POOL =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        Number(poolData.reserve0) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        Number(poolData.reserve1) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    const roosterAmount =
        100n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.reserve1 /
        poolData.reserve0;

    console.log('===== PROPOSED DEPOSIT =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON raw:',
        ptonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');

    const lpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        ptonAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    const deadline =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

    console.log('===== LP CALCULATION =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'LP from ROOSTER:',
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP from pTON:',
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Minimum LP (95%):',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('===== BUILD ROOSTER TX =====');
    console.log('');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,
                sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(roosterTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('TON value raw:');
    console.log(roosterTx.value.toString());

    console.log(
        'TON value:',
        Number(roosterTx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Body exists:');
    console.log(roosterTx.body !== undefined);

    console.log('');

    if (roosterTx.body) {
        decodeJettonBody(roosterTx.body);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== BUILD pTON TX =====');
    console.log('');

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                sendAmount: ptonAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(ptonTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('TON value raw:');
    console.log(ptonTx.value.toString());

    console.log(
        'TON value:',
        Number(ptonTx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Body exists:');
    console.log(ptonTx.body !== undefined);

    console.log('');

    if (ptonTx.body) {
        decodePtonBody(ptonTx.body);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('             BUILD + DECODE RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER amount:');
    console.log(
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON amount:');
    console.log(
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9,
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected LP:');
    console.log(expectedLp.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('Minimum LP:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER transaction built: YES');
    console.log('pTON transaction built: YES');

    console.log('');

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('                 END OF READ-ONLY CHECK');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

function inspectBody(name: string, body: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${name}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC base64:');
    console.log(body.toBoc().toString('base64'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Root cell:');
    console.log(body.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Refs:');
    console.log(body.refs.length);

    const slice = body.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log('Remaining bits:');
    console.log(slice.remainingBits);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Remaining refs:');
    console.log(slice.remainingRefs);

    if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        console.log('');
        console.log('First 32-bit opcode:');
        console.log(opcode.toString());

        console.log(
            'Opcode HEX:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    } else {
        console.log('');
        console.log('First 32-bit opcode: NOT AVAILABLE');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi V2.2 LIQUIDITY BODY INSPECTOR');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const proxyTon =
        DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ========================================================
    // REAL POOL
    // ========================================================

    const pool =
        await router.getPool(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER,
                token1: PTON,
            }
        );

    const poolAddress = pool.address;

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    // ========================================================
    // POOL DATA
    // ========================================================

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(poolAddress);

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    const roosterAmount =
        100n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.reserve1 /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        ptonAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    const deadline =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER:');
    console.log(
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON:');
    console.log(
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9,
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected LP:');
    console.log(expectedLp.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Minimum LP:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Deadline:');
    console.log(deadline);

    // ========================================================
    // BUILD ROOSTER BODY
    // ========================================================

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,
                sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    // ========================================================
    // BUILD pTON BODY
    // ========================================================

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                sendAmount: ptonAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    // ========================================================
    // INSPECT
    // ========================================================

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
    throw new Error('ROOSTER transaction body is missing');
}

if (!ptonTx.body) {
    throw new Error('pTON transaction body is missing');
}

inspectBody(
    'ROOSTER TRANSACTION BODY',
    roosterTx.body
);

inspectBody(
    'pTON TRANSACTION BODY',
    ptonTx.body
);

    // ========================================================
    // DESTINATIONS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DESTINATIONS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER TX destination:');
    console.log(roosterTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX destination:');
    console.log(ptonTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLiquidityTraceV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient, Cell } from '@ton/ton';

const USER_WALLET =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250nC3';

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const MAX_TX = 20;

const client = new TonClient({
    endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
});

function shortAddress(value: string): string {
    if (value.length <= 28) return value;
    return `${value.slice(0, 12)}...${value.slice(-10)}`;
}

function formatNano(value: bigint | string | number): string {
    try {
        const n = BigInt(value);
        return `${n.toString()} nanotons (${Number(n) / 1e9} TON)`;
    } catch {
        return String(value);
    }
}

function getOpcode(body: Cell | null | undefined): string {
    if (!body) return 'NO BODY';

    try {
        const slice = body.beginParse();

        if (slice.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'NO 32-BIT OPCODE';
        }

        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        return `0x${opcode
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0')}`;
    } catch {
        return 'OPCODE READ ERROR';
    }
}

function opcodeName(opcode: string): string {
    switch (opcode.toLowerCase()) {

        case '0x37c096df':
            return 'provide_lp';

        case '0x0f8a7ea5':
            return 'provide_lp / pool-side message';

        case '0x00000015':
            return 'mint';

        default:
            return 'unknown';
    }
}

function addressFromInfo(info: any): string {
    try {
        if (info?.type === 'internal' && info.dest) {
            return info.dest.toString();
        }

        if (info?.dest) {
            return info.dest.toString();
        }

        if (info?.src) {
            return info.src.toString();
        }
    } catch {
        return 'unknown';
    }

    return 'unknown';
}

function sourceFromInfo(info: any): string {
    try {
        if (info?.type === 'internal' && info.src) {
            return info.src.toString();
        }

        if (info?.src) {
            return info.src.toString();
        }
    } catch {
        return 'unknown';
    }

    return 'unknown';
}

function messageValue(message: any): bigint {
    try {
        if (
            message?.info?.type === 'internal' &&
            message.info.value?.coins !== undefined
        ) {
            return BigInt(message.info.value.coins);
        }
    } catch {
        // ignore
    }

    return 0n;
}

function printMessage(
    message: any,
    direction: string,
    index: number
) {
    if (!message) return;

    const info = message.info;

    const source =
        sourceFromInfo(info);

    const destination =
        addressFromInfo(info);

    const value =
        messageValue(message);

    const opcode =
        getOpcode(message.body);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`    ${direction} MESSAGE ${index}`);
    console.log(`      Source      : ${shortAddress(source)}`);
    console.log(`      Destination : ${shortAddress(destination)}`);
    console.log(`      Value       : ${formatNano(value)}`);
    console.log(`      Opcode      : ${opcode}`);
    console.log(`      Meaning     : ${opcodeName(opcode)}`);
}

async function inspectAddress(
    label: string,
    addressText: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(`Address: ${addressText}`);

    let address: Address;

    try {
        address = Address.parse(addressText);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'ADDRESS ERROR:',
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
        return [];
    }

    try {

        const transactions =
            await client.getTransactions(
                address,
                {
                    limit: MAX_TX,
                }
            );

        console.log(
            `Transactions returned: ${transactions.length}`
        );

        if (transactions.length === 0) {
            console.log('No transactions found.');
            return [];
        }

        transactions.forEach(
            (tx: any, index: number) => {

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `---------------- Transaction ${index + 1} ----------------`
                );

                console.log(
                    `LT          : ${tx.lt}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Hash        : ${Buffer.from(tx.hash()).toString('base64')}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Account     : ${shortAddress(address.toString())}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `In message  : ${tx.inMessage ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Out messages: ${tx.outMessages?.size ?? 0}`
                );

                if (tx.inMessage) {
                    printMessage(
                        tx.inMessage,
                        'IN',
                        1
                    );
                }

                if (tx.outMessages) {

                    let i = 1;

                    for (
                        const message
                        of tx.outMessages.values()
                    ) {

                        printMessage(
                            message,
                            'OUT',
                            i
                        );

                        i++;
                    }
                }
            }
        );

        return transactions;

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TRANSACTION READ ERROR:',
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );

        return [];
    }
}

function normalize(value: string): string {
    return value
        .replace(/^EQ/, '')
        .replace(/^UQ/, '')
        .toLowerCase();
}

function sameAddress(
    a: string,
    b: string
): boolean {
    return normalize(a) === normalize(b);
}

function analyzeRoute(
    allTransactions: any[]
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('             ROUTE ANALYSIS');
    console.log('================================================');

    const expected = [
        USER_WALLET,
        ROOSTER_WALLET,
        ROUTER,
        PTON_ROUTER_WALLET,
        POOL,
    ];

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected V2.2 route:');

    expected.forEach(
        (address, index) => {
            console.log(
                `${index + 1}. ${shortAddress(address)}`
            );
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Observed matching messages:');

    let matches = 0;

    for (const item of allTransactions) {

        const tx = item.tx;
        const owner = item.owner;

        if (!tx) continue;

        if (tx.inMessage) {

            const info =
                tx.inMessage.info;

            const src =
                sourceFromInfo(info);

            const dst =
                addressFromInfo(info);

            if (
                expected.some(
                    x => sameAddress(x, src)
                ) ||
                expected.some(
                    x => sameAddress(x, dst)
                )
            ) {

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `ACCOUNT: ${shortAddress(owner)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `IN: ${shortAddress(src)} -> ${shortAddress(dst)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `OPCODE: ${getOpcode(tx.inMessage.body)}`
                );

                matches++;
            }
        }

        if (tx.outMessages) {

            for (
                const message
                of tx.outMessages.values()
            ) {

                const info =
                    message.info;

                const src =
                    sourceFromInfo(info);

                const dst =
                    addressFromInfo(info);

                if (
                    expected.some(
                        x => sameAddress(x, src)
                    ) ||
                    expected.some(
                        x => sameAddress(x, dst)
                    )
                ) {

                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        `ACCOUNT: ${shortAddress(owner)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `OUT: ${shortAddress(src)} -> ${shortAddress(dst)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `OPCODE: ${getOpcode(message.body)}`
                    );

                    matches++;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');

    if (matches === 0) {
        console.log(
            'No direct route messages were found in the fetched transactions.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `Route-related messages found: ${matches}`
        );
    }
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '        ROOSTER / STON.FI V2.2 LIQUIDITY TRACE'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TONCENTER TESTNET INDEXER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'This diagnostic reads existing blockchain transactions only.'
    );

    const accounts = [
        {
            label: '1. USER WALLET',
            address: USER_WALLET,
        },
        {
            label: '2. ROOSTER JETTON WALLET',
            address: ROOSTER_WALLET,
        },
        {
            label: '3. ROUTER pTON WALLET',
            address: PTON_ROUTER_WALLET,
        },
        {
            label: '4. STON.FI ROUTER V2.2',
            address: ROUTER,
        },
        {
            label: '5. POOL',
            address: POOL,
        },
    ];

    const allTransactions: {
        owner: string;
        tx: any;
    }[] = [];

    for (const account of accounts) {

        const transactions =
            await inspectAddress(
                account.label,
                account.address
            );

        for (const tx of transactions) {
            allTransactions.push({
                owner: account.address,
                tx,
            });
        }
    }

    analyzeRoute(allTransactions);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('                     TRACE FINISHED');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('IMPORTANT:');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TONCONNECT REQUEST');
    console.log('NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('');
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER_WALLET =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const PTon_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

const DELAY_MS = 1200;
const MAX_RETRIES = 4;

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function getJson(
    url: string,
    label: string
): Promise<any> {

    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= MAX_RETRIES; attempt++) {

        try {

            const response = await fetch(url);

            if (response.ok) {
                return await response.json();
            }

            if (response.status === 429) {

                const wait =
                    DELAY_MS * attempt * 2;

                console.log(
                    `[${label}] HTTP 429 - waiting ${wait} ms...`
                );

                await sleep(wait);
                continue;
            }

            throw new Error(
                `[${label}] HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
            );

        } catch (error: any) {

            if (attempt === MAX_RETRIES) {
                throw error;
            }

            const wait =
                DELAY_MS * attempt;

            console.log(
                `[${label}] request error - retry ${attempt}/${MAX_RETRIES} after ${wait} ms`
            );

            await sleep(wait);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(
        `[${label}] request failed after retries`
    );
}

async function getTransactions(
    address: string,
    label: string
): Promise<any[]> {

    const url =
        `${TONCENTER}/transactions` +
        `?account=${encodeURIComponent(address)}` +
        `&limit=20` +
        `&sort=desc`;

    const data =
        await getJson(url, label);

    await sleep(DELAY_MS);

    return data.transactions ?? [];
}

function printTransaction(
    tx: any,
    index: number,
    prefix: string
) {

    console.log(`${prefix} #${index + 1}`);

    console.log(
        'hash:',
        tx.hash ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'lt:',
        tx.lt ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'utime:',
        tx.utime
            ? new Date(
                  Number(tx.utime) * 1000
              ).toISOString()
            : 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'total_fees:',
        tx.total_fees ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'success:',
        tx.success ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'end_status:',
        tx.end_status ?? 'N/A'
    );

    if (tx.in_msg) {
        console.log(
            'in_msg:',
            JSON.stringify(tx.in_msg)
        );
    }

    if (
        tx.out_msgs &&
        Array.isArray(tx.out_msgs)
    ) {
        console.log(
            'out_msgs:',
            JSON.stringify(tx.out_msgs)
        );
    }

    if (tx.description) {
        console.log(
            'description:',
            JSON.stringify(tx.description)
        );
    }

    console.log('');
}

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000001n;

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const EXPECTED_PTON_POOL_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

function hexOpcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        const slice = body.beginParse();

        if (slice.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'NO_32BIT_OPCODE';
        }

        return '0x' + slice.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'UNREADABLE';
    }
}

function messageInfo(message: any, index: number | string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`MESSAGE ${index}`);
    console.log('----------------------------------------');

    if (!message) {
        console.log('MESSAGE: NULL');
        return;
    }

    console.log('TYPE:', message.info.type);

    if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
        console.log('FROM:', message.info.src.toString());
        console.log('TO:', message.info.dest.toString());
        console.log(
            'VALUE:',
            message.info.value.coins.toString(),
            'nanoTON'
        );
    }

    console.log('OPCODE:', hexOpcode(message.body));

    try {
        console.log(
            'BODY BOC:',
            message.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('BODY BOC: UNAVAILABLE');
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 LIQUIDITY TRANSACTION INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('NETWORK: TESTNET');
    console.log('WALLET:', USER.toString());
    console.log('TARGET LT:', TARGET_LT.toString());
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com',
    });

    const last = await client.getLastBlock();

    const account = await client.getAccount(
        last.last.seqno,
        USER
    );

    if (!account.account.last) {
        throw new Error('Wallet has no transaction history.');
    }

    const latestLt = BigInt(account.account.last.lt);

    const latestHash = Buffer.from(
        account.account.last.hash,
        'base64'
    );

    const transactions = await client.getAccountTransactions(
        USER,
        latestLt,
        latestHash
    );

    const item = transactions.find(
        (x: any) => BigInt(x.tx.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!item) {
        throw new Error(
            `Target transaction LT ${TARGET_LT} was not found in recent transactions.`
        );
    }

    const tx: any = item.tx;

    console.log('TRANSACTION FOUND: PASS');
    console.log('');
    console.log('LT:', tx.lt.toString());
    console.log('HASH:', tx.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('TIME:', tx.now);
    console.log('BLOCK SEQNO:', item.block.seqno);
    console.log(
        'TOTAL FEES:',
        tx.totalFees.coins.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );
    console.log('OUT MESSAGES:', tx.outMessagesCount);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('INCOMING MESSAGE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    messageInfo(tx.inMessage, 'IN');

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('OUTGOING MESSAGES');
    console.log('============================================================');

   const outMessages: any[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < tx.outMessagesCount; i++) {
    const message = tx.outMessages.get(i);
    outMessages.push(message);
}

    console.log('');
    console.log('Decoded outgoing messages:', outMessages.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < outMessages.length; i++) {
        messageInfo(outMessages[i], i + 1);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('EXPECTED DESTINATION CHECKS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    for (let i = 0; i < outMessages.length; i++) {
        const message = outMessages[i];

        if (message.info.type !== 'internal') {
            continue;
        }

        const destination = message.info.dest.toString();

        console.log('');
        console.log(`MESSAGE ${i + 1} DESTINATION:`);
        console.log(destination);

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER WALLET MATCH:',
            destination === EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        console.log(
            'pTON POOL WALLET MATCH:',
            destination === EXPECTED_PTON_POOL_WALLET
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ ONLY.');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('');

    void provider;
}

import { Address, beginCell, fromNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBsGx9ArADUrREB34W-ghgsCgBShvfUr4Jvlu-0KGc33Rbt'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

// فقط برای ساخت پارامتر تراکنش.
// این مقدار هرگز ارسال نمی‌شود.
const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100n * 1_000_000_000n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 1n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi V1 READ-ONLY LIQUIDITY INSPECTION');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON V1 Master:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('User wallet:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());

    const router = DEX.v1.Router.create(ROUTER);
    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 1. Calculate pool address
    // ------------------------------------------

    const poolAddress =
        await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                token1: PTON_MASTER,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('POOL');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('Pool address:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 2. Get ROOSTER Router wallet
    // ------------------------------------------

    const roosterRouterWalletResult =
        await provider
            .provider(ROOSTER_MASTER)
            .get('get_wallet_address', [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: addressToCell(ROUTER),
                },
            ]);

    const roosterRouterWalletAddress =
        roosterRouterWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Router wallet:');
    console.log(roosterRouterWalletAddress.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 3. Get pTON Router wallet
    // ------------------------------------------

    const ptonRouterWalletResult =
        await provider
            .provider(PTON_MASTER)
            .get('get_wallet_address', [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: addressToCell(ROUTER),
                },
            ]);

    const ptonRouterWalletAddress =
        ptonRouterWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Router wallet:');
    console.log(ptonRouterWalletAddress.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 4. Get user's ROOSTER wallet
    // ------------------------------------------

    const roosterUserWalletResult =
        await provider
            .provider(ROOSTER_MASTER)
            .get('get_wallet_address', [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: addressToCell(USER_WALLET),
                },
            ]);

    const roosterUserWalletAddress =
        roosterUserWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('User ROOSTER wallet:');
    console.log(roosterUserWalletAddress.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 5. Build liquidity transaction parameters
    // ------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('BUILDING LIQUIDITY TX PARAMETERS');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Test amount:');
    console.log('100 ROOSTER');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Minimum LP out:');
    console.log(MIN_LP_OUT.toString());

    const txParams =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER_WALLET,
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
                sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString(),
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT.toString(),
            }
        );

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 6. Print transaction parameters
    // ------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('TRANSACTION PARAMETERS');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Destination (to):');
    console.log(txParams.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TON value:');
    console.log(txParams.value.toString(), 'nanoTON');

    console.log(
        'TON value:',
        fromNano(txParams.value),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER amount:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString(), 'raw');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount:',
        Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1_000_000_000,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Body exists:');
    console.log(txParams.body ? 'YES' : 'NO');

    if (txParams.body) {
        const bodyBoc = txParams.body.toBoc();

        console.log('');
        console.log('Transaction body BOC:');
        console.log(bodyBoc.toString('base64'));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Transaction body size:');
        console.log(bodyBoc.length, 'bytes');
    } else {
        console.log('');
        console.log('Transaction body BOC: NONE');
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 7. Read current pool state
    // ------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('CURRENT POOL STATE');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    try {
        const poolData =
            await provider
                .provider(poolAddress)
                .get('get_pool_data', []);

        console.log('Pool get_pool_data succeeded.');
        console.log(poolData.stack);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'Pool get_pool_data could not be executed.'
        );

        console.log(
            'This normally means the calculated pool is not deployed yet.'
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------
    // 8. Safety information
    // ------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO LIQUIDITY WAS ADDED.');

    console.log('');
}

// get_wallet_address expects a Cell containing
// the serialized address.
function addressToCell(address: Address) {
    return beginCell()
        .storeAddress(address)
        .endCell();
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

export async function run(provider: any) {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('   LIVE ROUTER CODE + DATA INSPECTION');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const result = await provider.provider(ROUTER).getState();

    console.log('===== ACCOUNT STATE =====');
    console.log('state type:', result.state.type);
    console.log('');

    if (result.state.type !== 'active') {
        console.log('Router is not active.');
        return;
    }

    // ================================
    // CODE
    // ================================

    const code = Cell.fromBoc(result.state.code)[0];

    console.log('===== LIVE CODE =====');
    console.log('bits:', code.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', code.refs.length);
    console.log('exotic:', code.isExotic);
    console.log('type:', code.type);
    console.log('hash:', code.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('CODE BOC:');
    console.log(
        code.toBoc({ idx: false }).toString('base64')
    );

    // ================================
    // DATA
    // ================================

    const data = Cell.fromBoc(result.state.data)[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== LIVE DATA =====');
    console.log('bits:', data.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', data.refs.length);
    console.log('exotic:', data.isExotic);
    console.log('type:', data.type);
    console.log('hash:', data.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('DATA BOC:');
    console.log(
        data.toBoc({ idx: false }).toString('base64')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DATA REFERENCES =====');

    data.refs.forEach((r: Cell, i: number) => {

        console.log('');
        console.log(`ref[${i}]`);
        console.log('bits:', r.bits.length);
        console.log('refs:', r.refs.length);
        console.log('exotic:', r.isExotic);
        console.log('type:', r.type);
        console.log('hash:', r.hash().toString('hex'));
        console.log(
            'boc:',
            r.toBoc({ idx: false }).toString('base64')
        );
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' LIVE INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('========================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLpAccountAfterCallbackV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions';

function opName(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';

    switch (op >>> 0) {
        case 0x06ecd527:
            return 'LP_ACCOUNT_CALLBACK';

        case 0x50c6a654:
            return 'ADD_LIQUIDITY';

        case 0x178d4519:
            return 'JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER';

        case 0x37c096df:
            return 'ROUTER_PROVIDE_LP_V22';

        case 0x0f8a7ea5:
            return 'PROVIDE_LP';

        default:
            return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
}

function printSeparator() {
    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
}

function printShortTx(tx: any) {
    console.log('\nLT:');
    console.log(tx.lt);

    console.log('HASH:');
    console.log(tx.hash);

    console.log('ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(tx.account);

    console.log('NOW:');
    console.log(tx.now);

    console.log('END STATUS:');
    console.log(tx.end_status);

    console.log('TOTAL FEES:');
    console.log(tx.total_fees);
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 LP ACCOUNT TRANSACTION SEARCH');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nLP ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(LP_ACCOUNT.toString());

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    console.log('\nSOURCE:');
    console.log(TONCENTER);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Fetch recent transactions from TON Center Testnet V3
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const url =
        TONCENTER +
        '?account=' +
        encodeURIComponent(LP_ACCOUNT.toString()) +
        '&limit=20' +
        '&offset=0' +
        '&sort=desc';

    console.log('\nFetching transactions...');

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `TONCENTER HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const data = await response.json();

    const transactions = data.transactions ?? [];

    console.log('\nTRANSACTIONS RETURNED:');
    console.log(transactions.length);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Print timeline
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    printSeparator();
    console.log(' LP ACCOUNT TRANSACTION TIMELINE');
    printSeparator();

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(70));

        printShortTx(tx);

        if (lt === TARGET_LT) {
            console.log('\n*** TARGET TRANSACTION ***');
        }

        if (lt > TARGET_LT) {
            console.log('\n*** NEWER THAN TARGET ***');
        }

        if (lt < TARGET_LT) {
            console.log('\n*** OLDER THAN TARGET ***');
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Find target
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const target = transactions.find(
        (tx: any) => BigInt(tx.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    printSeparator();
    console.log(' TARGET SEARCH');
    printSeparator();

    if (!target) {

        console.log('\nTARGET FOUND: NO');

        console.log(
            '\nThe returned 20 transactions did not contain LT',
            TARGET_LT.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            '\nThis does NOT mean the transaction is missing.'
        );

        console.log(
            'It may simply be outside the first 20 indexed results.'
        );

    } else {

        console.log('\nTARGET FOUND: YES');

        printShortTx(target);

        console.log('\nTARGET JSON:');

        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(target, null, 2)
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Newer transactions
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const newer = transactions.filter(
        (tx: any) => BigInt(tx.lt) > TARGET_LT
    );

    printSeparator();
    console.log(' TRANSACTIONS AFTER TARGET');
    printSeparator();

    console.log(
        '\nCOUNT:',
        newer.length
    );

    for (const tx of newer) {

        console.log('\nLT:', tx.lt);
        console.log('HASH:', tx.hash);
        console.log('NOW:', tx.now);
        console.log('END STATUS:', tx.end_status);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Older transactions
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const older = transactions.filter(
        (tx: any) => BigInt(tx.lt) < TARGET_LT
    );

    printSeparator();
    console.log(' TRANSACTIONS BEFORE TARGET');
    printSeparator();

    console.log(
        '\nCOUNT:',
        older.length
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Final
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    printSeparator();
    console.log(' SEARCH COMPLETE');
    printSeparator();

    console.log('\nREAD ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
}
