import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

function formatToken(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction.length > 0
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toString();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 LP ACCOUNT POST-ROOSTER INSPECTION');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('======================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(LP_ACCOUNT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('PTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    // ==================================================
    // 1. Create read-only ContractProvider
    // ==================================================

    const lpProvider =
        provider.provider(LP_ACCOUNT);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== LP ACCOUNT GETTER =====');

    // ==================================================
    // 2. Call get_lp_account_data()
    // ==================================================

    const result =
        await lpProvider.get(
            'get_lp_account_data',
            []
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Getter executed successfully.'
    );

    // ==================================================
    // 3. Decode getter result
    //
    // Expected:
    // user_address
    // pool_address
    // amount0
    // amount1
    // ==================================================

    const stack = result.stack;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Stack remaining before decode:',
        stack.remaining
    );

    const userAddress =
        stack.readAddress();

    const poolAddress =
        stack.readAddress();

    const amount0 =
        stack.readBigNumber();

    const amount1 =
        stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER ADDRESS:');
    console.log(userAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL ADDRESS:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('AMOUNT0 RAW:');
    console.log(amount0.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('AMOUNT1 RAW:');
    console.log(amount1.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('AMOUNT0 DISPLAY:');
    console.log(
        formatToken(amount0),
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('AMOUNT1 DISPLAY:');
    console.log(
        formatToken(amount1),
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Stack remaining after decode:',
        stack.remaining
    );

    // ==================================================
    // 4. Guards
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== GUARDS =====');

    const userOk =
        userAddress.equals(USER);

    const poolOk =
        poolAddress.equals(POOL);

    const roosterStored =
        amount0 === 100_000_000_000n;

    const ptonStored =
        amount1 === 0n;

    console.log(
        'USER MATCH:',
        userOk ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'POOL MATCH:',
        poolOk ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER STORED = 100:',
        roosterStored ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'PTON STORED = 0:',
        ptonStored ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    // ==================================================
    // 5. Final interpretation
    // ==================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== INTERPRETATION =====');

    if (
        userOk &&
        poolOk &&
        roosterStored &&
        ptonStored
    ) {
        console.log(
            'PASS: 100 ROOSTER is stored in the LP Account.'
        );

        console.log(
            'PASS: No pTON is currently stored.'
        );

        console.log(
            'PASS: LP Account is waiting for the second leg.'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'NEXT STEP: inspect pool state and prepare pTON leg.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'WARNING: LP Account state does not match expected staged state.'
        );

        console.log(
            'DO NOT SEND THE pTON LEG.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================');
    console.log(' END OF READ-ONLY INSPECTION');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('======================================================');
    console.log('');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectLpAccountFlowV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const TARGET_CALLBACK_LT = '99809465000012';

const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x06ecd527;
const OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) {
        return 'UNKNOWN';
    }

    return (
        '0x' +
        op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );
}

function getOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function formatInteger(value: bigint): string {
    return value
        .toString()
        .replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

function formatToken9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    if (fraction.length === 0) {
        return whole.toLocaleString();
    }

    return `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`;
}

function messageInfo(
    body: Cell,
    prefix: string
) {
    const opcode = getOpcode(body);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`${prefix} OPCODE:`);
    console.log(opcodeHex(opcode));

    console.log('');
    console.log(`${prefix} BODY:`);
    console.log(
        `${body.bits.length} bits / ${body.refs.length} refs`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`${prefix} BOC:`);
    console.log(
        body.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    return opcode;
}

function tryDecodeJettonTransfer(
    body: Cell
) {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        if (opcode !== OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
            return;
        }

        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

        const amount = s.loadCoins();

        const destination = s.loadAddress();

        const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

        const customPayloadBit = s.loadBit();

        if (customPayloadBit) {
            s.loadRef();
        }

        const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

        let forwardPayloadDescription = '';

        if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
            const forwardPayloadIsRef = s.loadBit();

            if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {
                if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
                    const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

                    forwardPayloadDescription =
                        `ref: ${forwardPayload.bits.length} bits / ${forwardPayload.refs.length} refs`;
                }
            } else {
                forwardPayloadDescription =
                    'inline';
            }
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('---------------- JETTON TRANSFER DECODE ----------------');

        console.log('');
        console.log('QUERY ID:');
        console.log(queryId.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('AMOUNT RAW:');
        console.log(formatInteger(amount));

        console.log('');
        console.log('AMOUNT TOKEN UNITS:');
        console.log(formatToken9(amount));

        console.log('');
        console.log('DESTINATION:');
        console.log(
            destination
                ? destination.toString()
                : 'NULL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('RESPONSE DESTINATION:');
        console.log(
            responseDestination
                ? responseDestination.toString()
                : 'NULL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('FORWARD TON AMOUNT:');
        console.log(
            formatInteger(forwardTonAmount)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD:');
        console.log(
            forwardPayloadDescription || 'NONE'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('--------------------------------------------------------');
    } catch {
        console.log('');
        console.log('JETTON TRANSFER DECODE: FAILED');
    }
}

function tryDecodeCallback(
    body: Cell
) {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        if (opcode !== OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
            return;
        }

        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

        const amount0 = s.loadCoins();

        const amount1 = s.loadCoins();

        const userAddress = s.loadAddress();

        const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();

        const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();

        const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

        if (hasCustomPayload) {
            s.loadRef();
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('================ CALLBACK DECODE =================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('QUERY ID:');
        console.log(queryId.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOT AM0:');
        console.log(
            `${formatInteger(amount0)} raw`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOT AM0 TOKEN:');
        console.log(
            formatToken9(amount0)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOT AM1:');
        console.log(
            `${formatInteger(amount1)} raw`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TOT AM1 TOKEN:');
        console.log(
            formatToken9(amount1)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('USER:');
        console.log(
            userAddress
                ? userAddress.toString()
                : 'NULL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('MIN LP OUT:');
        console.log(
            formatInteger(minLpOut)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('FWD AMOUNT:');
        console.log(
            formatInteger(fwdAmount)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '================================================='
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('');
        console.log('CALLBACK DECODE: FAILED');
    }
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 LP ACCOUNT FLOW INSPECTOR');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(LP_ACCOUNT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET CALLBACK LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_CALLBACK_LT);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching LP Account transactions...');

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            LP_ACCOUNT,
            {
                limit: 100,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRANSACTIONS FETCHED:');
    console.log(transactions.length);

    let txIndex = 0;

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        txIndex++;

        const lt = tx.lt.toString();

        const hash =
            tx.hash().toString('base64');

        console.log('');
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );
        console.log(
            ` LP ACCOUNT TX #${txIndex}`
        );
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(lt);

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(hash);

        console.log('');
        console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
        console.log(tx.now.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('MATCH TARGET CALLBACK LT:');
        console.log(
            lt === TARGET_CALLBACK_LT
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );


        /*
         * ========================================================
         * INCOMING MESSAGE
         * ========================================================
         */

        if (tx.inMessage) {

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '---------------- INCOMING MESSAGE ----------------'
            );

            const inMessage =
                tx.inMessage;

            if (
                inMessage.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(
                    inMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(
                    inMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    inMessage.info.value.coins.toString()
                );
            }

            const opcode =
                messageInfo(
                    inMessage.body,
                    'INCOMING'
                );

            console.log('');
            console.log('KNOWN OPCODE:');

            if (
                opcode ===
                OP_JETTON_TRANSFER
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'JETTON TRANSFER 0x0f8a7ea5'
                );

                tryDecodeJettonTransfer(
                    inMessage.body
                );
            } else if (
                opcode ===
                OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'ADD LIQUIDITY 0x50c6a654'
                );
            } else if (
                opcode ===
                OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'CALLBACK ADD LIQUIDITY 0x06ecd527'
                );

                tryDecodeCallback(
                    inMessage.body
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    'OTHER / UNKNOWN'
                );
            }
        }


        /*
         * ========================================================
         * OUTGOING MESSAGES
         * ========================================================
         */

        let outIndex = 0;

        for (
            const outMessage
            of tx.outMessages.values()
        ) {

            outIndex++;

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `---------------- OUTGOING MESSAGE #${outIndex} ----------------`
            );

            if (
                outMessage.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');

                console.log(
                    outMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');

                console.log(
                    outMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('VALUE:');

                console.log(
                    outMessage.info.value.coins.toString()
                );
            }

            const opcode =
                messageInfo(
                    outMessage.body,
                    'OUTGOING'
                );

            console.log('');
            console.log('KNOWN OPCODE:');

            if (
                opcode ===
                OP_JETTON_TRANSFER
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'JETTON TRANSFER 0x0f8a7ea5'
                );

                tryDecodeJettonTransfer(
                    outMessage.body
                );
            } else if (
                opcode ===
                OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'ADD LIQUIDITY 0x50c6a654'
                );
            } else if (
                opcode ===
                OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'CALLBACK ADD LIQUIDITY 0x06ecd527'
                );

                tryDecodeCallback(
                    outMessage.body
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    'OTHER / UNKNOWN'
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' LP ACCOUNT FLOW INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TESTNET_ENDPOINT = 'https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

function printObjectKeys(label: string, obj: any) {
    console.log(`${label} KEYS:`);

    if (obj && typeof obj === 'object') {
        console.log(Object.keys(obj));
    } else {
        console.log('NOT AN OBJECT');
    }
}

function inspectMessage(label: string, message: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`========== ${label} ==========`);

    if (!message) {
        console.log('MESSAGE: null');
        return;
    }

    printObjectKeys('MESSAGE', message);

    console.log(
        'MESSAGE TYPE:',
        message?.constructor?.name
    );

    if (message.info) {
        console.log('MESSAGE.INFO KEYS:');
        console.log(Object.keys(message.info));

        console.log(
            'MESSAGE.INFO:',
            message.info
        );
    }

    if (message.body) {
        console.log(
            'BODY TYPE:',
            message.body?.constructor?.name
        );

        try {
            console.log(
                'BODY BOC:',
                message.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'BODY BOC ERROR:',
                e
            );
        }

        try {
            console.log(
                'BODY HASH:',
                message.body
                    .hash()
                    .toString('hex')
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'BODY HASH ERROR:',
                e
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');

    );

    );

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: TESTNET_ENDPOINT,
    });

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        'MASTERCHAIN SEQNO:',
        latest.last.seqno
    );

    const account = await client.getAccount(
        latest.last.seqno,
        LP_ACCOUNT
    );

    console.log(
        'LP ACCOUNT STATE:',
        account.account.state.type
    );

 const transactions =
    await client.getAccountTransactions(
        LP_ACCOUNT,
        BigInt('99809465000012'),
        Buffer.from(
            'dlkr3JO/BoGKETJ6iJtsauxZMSPiGQd8zoNHnLvgTmU=',
            'base64'
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TRANSACTIONS RETURNED:',
        transactions.length
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
    const tx: any = transactions[i].tx;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '========== RAW TRANSACTION OBJECT =========='
    );

    console.log(
        'RAW TRANSACTION KEYS:',
        Object.keys(tx ?? {})
    );

    console.log(
        'RAW TRANSACTION:',
        tx
    );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '================================================'
        );
        console.log(
            `TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`
        );
        console.log(
            '================================================'
        );

        console.log(
            'LT:',
            tx.lt?.toString()
        );

        console.log(
    'HASH:',
    typeof tx.hash === 'function'
        ? tx.hash().toString('base64')
        : tx.hash
            ? Buffer.from(tx.hash).toString('base64')
            : 'N/A'
);

        console.log(
            'PREV LT:',
            tx.prevTransactionLt?.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'OUT MESSAGES:',
            tx.outMessagesCount
        );

        inspectMessage(
            'INBOUND MESSAGE',
            tx.inMessage
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '========== OUTBOUND MESSAGES =========='
        );

        const outMessages: any =
            tx.outMessages;

        console.log(
            'OUT_MESSAGES TYPE:',
            outMessages?.constructor?.name
        );

        console.log(
            'OUT_MESSAGES INTERNAL MAP TYPE:',
            outMessages?._map?.constructor?.name
        );

       if (outMessages?._map) {
    const internalMap: Map<any, any> =
        outMessages._map;

    console.log(
        'INTERNAL MAP SIZE:',
        internalMap.size
    );

    for (
        const [key, message]
        of internalMap.entries()
    ) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'OUTBOUND DICTIONARY KEY:',
            key
        );

        inspectMessage(
            'REAL OUTBOUND MESSAGE',
            message
        );
    }
}
     
else {
            console.log(
                'NO INTERNAL _map FOUND'
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY: YES'
    );
    console.log(
        ' TRANSACTION SENT: NO'
    );
    console.log(
        ' pTON LEG SENT: NO'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TARGET = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const CODE_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAIwAIQgLJWi7SKrUW93+dSJjcRXjnLxiiRI6PaDIzSwtL9QG8eU64SPQ=';

const DATA_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEASwAAkYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvAD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B/teWN17MZq1F0h26AAJ1XV7e';

function printHex(buffer: Buffer) {
    console.log(buffer.toString('hex'));

    console.log();
    console.log('BYTES:');

    for (let i = 0; i < buffer.length; i += 16) {
        const row: string[] = [];

        for (
            let j = i;
            j < Math.min(i + 16, buffer.length);
            j++
        ) {
            row.push(
                buffer[j].toString(16).padStart(2, '0')
            );
        }

        console.log(
            i.toString().padStart(4, '0'),
            row.join(' ')
        );
    }
}

async function main() {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP DESTINATION CODE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log();
    console.log('TARGET:');
    console.log(TARGET.toString());

    // ============================================================
    // CODE
    // ============================================================

    console.log();
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('CODE BOC');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('BOC:');
    console.log(CODE_BOC);

    const code = Cell.fromBase64(CODE_BOC);

    console.log();
    console.log('root bits:', code.bits.length);
    console.log('root refs:', code.refs.length);
    console.log('root exotic:', code.isExotic);

    console.log();
    console.log(
        'NOTE: Code cell is exotic, so beginParse() is intentionally NOT used.'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Inspect raw cell bits through the BitString object.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('RAW CODE BITSTRING');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('bits:', code.bits.length);

    const bitString: any = code.bits;

    console.log();
    console.log('BitString object keys:');
    console.log(
        Object.getOwnPropertyNames(
            Object.getPrototypeOf(bitString)
        )
    );

    console.log();
    console.log('BitString object:');
    console.log(bitString);

    // ============================================================
    // DATA
    // ============================================================

    console.log();
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DATA CELL');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const data = Cell.fromBase64(DATA_BOC);

    console.log('bits:', data.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', data.refs.length);
    console.log('exotic:', data.isExotic);

    const s = data.beginParse();

    const owner = s.loadAddress();
    const pool = s.loadAddress();

    console.log();
    console.log('FIELD 1 OWNER:');
    console.log(owner?.toString());

    console.log();
    console.log('FIELD 2 POOL:');
    console.log(pool?.toString());

    console.log();
    console.log('FIELD 3:');

    console.log('remaining bits:', s.remainingBits);

    const field3 = s.loadUintBig(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('decimal:', field3.toString());
    console.log(
        'hex:',
        '0x' + field3.toString(16)
    );

    console.log();
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('FIELD 3 BITS');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log(
        field3.toString(2).padStart(48, '0')
    );

    console.log();
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('No transaction was sent.');
}

    await main();

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { TupleReader } from '@ton/core';

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT = 'https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const LP_JETTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0:8D9D78729EAE46ED167C82585748D83A7976F2D64CEA0FEA7C1CDC6EE8F37948'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

function fmt(value: bigint, decimals: number): string {
    const negative = value < 0n;
    const v = negative ? -value : value;

    const s = v.toString().padStart(decimals + 1, '0');
    const integer = s.slice(0, -decimals);
    const fraction = s.slice(-decimals);

    return `${negative ? '-' : ''}${integer}.${fraction}`;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP JETTON MASTER INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();
    const seqno = latest.last.seqno;

    console.log(`MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${seqno}`);
    console.log('');

    console.log('LP JETTON WALLET:');
    console.log(LP_JETTON_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('EXPECTED USER:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('EXPECTED POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());
    console.log('');

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. get_wallet_data()
    // ----------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' 1. LP JETTON WALLET -> get_wallet_data()');
    console.log('==============================================================');

   const walletResult = await client.runMethod(
    seqno,
    LP_JETTON_WALLET,
    'get_wallet_data',
    []
);

const walletStack = new TupleReader(walletResult.result);

const walletBalance = walletStack.readBigNumber();
const walletOwner = walletStack.readAddress();
const jettonMaster = walletStack.readAddress();
const walletCode = walletStack.readCell();

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP TOKEN BALANCE:');
    console.log(walletBalance.toString());

    console.log('LP TOKEN BALANCE DISPLAY:');
    console.log(fmt(walletBalance, 9));

    console.log('');
    console.log('OWNER:');
    console.log(walletOwner.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('JETTON MASTER:');
    console.log(jettonMaster.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('OWNER MATCH:');
    console.log(
        walletOwner.equals(USER_WALLET)
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('WALLET CODE BOC:');
    console.log(
        walletCode.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. get_jetton_data()
    // ----------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' 2. JETTON MASTER -> get_jetton_data()');
    console.log('==============================================================');

    const masterResult = await client.runMethod(
    seqno,
    jettonMaster,
    'get_jetton_data',
    []
);

const masterStack = new TupleReader(masterResult.result);

const totalSupply = masterStack.readBigNumber();
const mintable = masterStack.readBigNumber();
const adminAddress = masterStack.readAddress();
const contentCell = masterStack.readCell();
const jettonWalletCode = masterStack.readCell();

    console.log('');
    console.log('JETTON MASTER:');
    console.log(jettonMaster.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TOTAL SUPPLY:');
    console.log(totalSupply.toString());

    console.log('TOTAL SUPPLY DISPLAY:');
    console.log(fmt(totalSupply, 9));

    console.log('');
    console.log('MINTABLE RAW:');
    console.log(mintable.toString());

    console.log('MINTABLE:');
    console.log(
        mintable !== 0n
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ADMIN:');
    console.log(adminAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('CONTENT CELL BOC:');
    console.log(
        contentCell.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('JETTON WALLET CODE BOC:');
    console.log(
        jettonWalletCode.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Cross-check
    // ----------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' 3. CROSS CHECK');
    console.log('==============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP WALLET:');
    console.log(LP_JETTON_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('MASTER:');
    console.log(jettonMaster.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('OWNER EXPECTED:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('OWNER ACTUAL:');
    console.log(walletOwner.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP BALANCE:');
    console.log(fmt(walletBalance, 9));

    console.log('');
    console.log('MASTER TOTAL SUPPLY:');
    console.log(fmt(totalSupply, 9));

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log(' TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address, TonClient, beginCell } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const OWNER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const RPC = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function fmt9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${frac}`;
}

function printAddress(label: string, address: Address | null) {
    console.log(`${label}:`);
    console.log(address ? address.toString() : 'null');
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: RPC,
    });

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP MASTER + LP ACCOUNT INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nPOOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('\nOWNER:');
    console.log(OWNER.toString());

    // ============================================================
    // 1. get_jetton_data
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('1. POOL get_jetton_data()');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_jetton_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('\nRAW STACK:');
        console.dir(result.stack, { depth: null });

        const totalSupply = result.stack.readBigNumber();
        const mintable = result.stack.readBigNumber();
        const admin = result.stack.readAddress();
        const content = result.stack.readCell();
        const walletCode = result.stack.readCell();

        console.log('\nDECODED JETTON DATA');

        console.log('\n1. TOTAL SUPPLY RAW:');
        console.log(totalSupply.toString());

        console.log('\n2. TOTAL SUPPLY / 1e9:');
        console.log(fmt9(totalSupply));

        console.log('\n3. MINTABLE:');
        console.log(mintable.toString());

        printAddress('\n4. ADMIN', admin);

        console.log('\n5. CONTENT BOC:');
        console.log(content.toBoc().toString('hex'));

        console.log('\n6. LP WALLET CODE BOC:');
        console.log(walletCode.toBoc().toString('hex'));

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nget_jetton_data ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 2. get_pool_data
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('2. POOL get_pool_data()');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_pool_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('\nRAW STACK:');
        console.dir(result.stack, { depth: null });

        /*
         * We intentionally inspect the stack first.
         * The exact field layout will be confirmed from the live result.
         */

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nget_pool_data ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 3. get_wallet_address(owner)
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('3. POOL get_wallet_address(owner)');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_wallet_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(OWNER)
                        .endCell(),
                },
            ]
        );

        console.log('\nRAW STACK:');
        console.dir(result.stack, { depth: null });

        const calculated = result.stack.readAddress();

        printAddress('\nCALCULATED LP WALLET', calculated);
        printAddress('\nKNOWN LP WALLET', LP_WALLET);

        console.log(
            '\nLP WALLET MATCH:',
            calculated.equals(LP_WALLET)
        );

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nget_wallet_address ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 4. get_lp_account_address(owner)
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('4. POOL get_lp_account_address(owner)');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    let lpAccount: Address | null = null;

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_lp_account_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(OWNER)
                        .endCell(),
                },
            ]
        );

        console.log('\nRAW STACK:');
        console.dir(result.stack, { depth: null });

        lpAccount = result.stack.readAddress();

        printAddress('\nLP ACCOUNT ADDRESS', lpAccount);

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nget_lp_account_address ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 5. LP Account getter
    // ============================================================

    if (lpAccount) {

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
        console.log('5. LP ACCOUNT DATA');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        try {
            const result = await client.runMethod(
                lpAccount,
                'get_lp_account_data',
                []
            );

            console.log('\nRAW STACK:');
            console.dir(result.stack, { depth: null });

        } catch (e) {
            console.log('\nLP ACCOUNT GETTER ERROR:');
            console.log(e);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 6. LP Wallet getter
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('6. LP WALLET get_wallet_data()');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            LP_WALLET,
            'get_wallet_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('\nRAW STACK:');
        console.dir(result.stack, { depth: null });

        const balance = result.stack.readBigNumber();
        const owner = result.stack.readAddress();
        const master = result.stack.readAddress();
        const code = result.stack.readCell();

        console.log('\nDECODED LP WALLET');

        console.log('\n1. BALANCE RAW:');
        console.log(balance.toString());

        console.log('\n2. BALANCE / 1e9:');
        console.log(fmt9(balance));

        printAddress('\n3. OWNER', owner);
        printAddress('\n4. MASTER / POOL', master);

        console.log('\n5. CODE BOC:');
        console.log(code.toBoc().toString('hex'));

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nLP WALLET DATA ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLpMintV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TESTNET_ENDPOINT = 'https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = BigInt('99809465000012');

const TARGET_HASH = Buffer.from(
    '7f91056d5cef6db3b01afa3304191b66ec64b9839d15cc660ba9c85d00efa3cf',
    'hex'
);

const INTERNAL_TRANSFER_OP = 0x178d4519;

function printAddress(label: string, address: Address) {
    console.log(`${label}: ${address.toString()}`);
}

function inspectMessage(message: any) {
    if (!message) {
        console.log('MESSAGE: null');
        return;
    }

    console.log('MESSAGE TYPE:', message.constructor?.name);

    if (message.info) {
        console.log('INFO:', message.info);
    }

    if (!message.body) {
        console.log('NO BODY');
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'BODY BOC:',
        message.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    try {
        const slice = message.body.beginParse();

        const op = slice.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            'OPCODE:',
            '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        if (op !== INTERNAL_TRANSFER_OP) {
            return;
        }

        const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);
        const amount = slice.loadCoins();
        const from = slice.loadAddress();
        const responseAddress = slice.loadAddress();
        const forwardTonAmount = slice.loadCoins();

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '========== JETTON INTERNAL TRANSFER =========='
        );

        console.log(
            'query_id:',
            queryId.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'LP TOKEN AMOUNT:',
            amount.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'LP TOKEN AMOUNT DISPLAY:',
            Number(amount) / 1_000_000_000
        );

        console.log('');

        printAddress('from:', from);

        console.log('');

        printAddress(
            'response_address:',
            responseAddress
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'forward_ton_amount:',
            forwardTonAmount.toString()
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'REMAINING BITS:',
            slice.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'REMAINING REFS:',
            slice.remainingRefs
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'BODY PARSE ERROR:',
            error
        );
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 LP MINT INSPECTOR'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: TESTNET_ENDPOINT,
    });

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        'MASTERCHAIN SEQNO:',
        latest.last.seqno
    );

    console.log('');

    printAddress('POOL', POOL);

    console.log(
        'TARGET LT:',
        TARGET_LT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'TARGET HASH:',
        TARGET_HASH.toString('hex')
    );

    console.log('');

    const transactions =
        await client.getAccountTransactions(
            POOL,
            TARGET_LT,
            TARGET_HASH
        );

    console.log(
        'TRANSACTIONS RETURNED:',
        transactions.length
    );

    console.log('');

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx: any = transactions[i].tx;

        if (!tx) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            '=============================================================='
        );

        console.log(
            `TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`
        );

        console.log(
            '=============================================================='
        );

        console.log(
            'LT:',
            tx.lt?.toString()
        );

        const txHash =
            typeof tx.hash === 'function'
                ? tx.hash().toString('hex')
                : tx.hash
                    ? Buffer.from(tx.hash).toString('hex')
                    : 'N/A';

        console.log(
            'HASH:',
            txHash
        );

        console.log(
            'OUT MESSAGES:',
            tx.outMessagesCount
        );

        const outMessages: any =
            tx.outMessages;

        console.log(
            'OUT_MESSAGES TYPE:',
            outMessages?.constructor?.name
        );

        console.log(
            'INTERNAL MAP TYPE:',
            outMessages?._map?.constructor?.name
        );

        if (!outMessages?._map) {
            console.log(
                'NO INTERNAL _map FOUND'
            );
            continue;
        }

        const internalMap: Map<any, any> =
            outMessages._map;

        console.log(
            'INTERNAL MAP SIZE:',
            internalMap.size
        );

        for (
            const [key, message]
            of internalMap.entries()
        ) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '--------------------------------------------------------------'
            );

            console.log(
                'OUTBOUND DICTIONARY KEY:',
                key
            );

            if (!message) {
                continue;
            }

            if (message.info) {
                console.log(
                    'DESTINATION:',
                    message.info.dest?.toString?.()
                );

                console.log(
                    'VALUE:',
                    message.info.value
                );
            }

            if (message.body) {
                console.log(
                    'BODY BOC:',
                    message.body
                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')
                );

                try {
                    const probe =
                        message.body.beginParse();

                    const op =
                        probe.loadUint(32);

                    console.log(
                        'OPCODE:',
                        '0x' +
                        op
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(8, '0')
                    );

                    if (
                        op === INTERNAL_TRANSFER_OP
                    ) {
                        console.log('');
                        console.log(
                            '*** FOUND LP INTERNAL TRANSFER ***'
                        );

                        inspectMessage(message);
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    console.log(
                        'OPCODE PARSE ERROR:',
                        error
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY: YES'
    );
    console.log(
        ' TRANSACTION SENT: NO'
    );
    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );
    console.log('');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLpShortV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient, beginCell } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const OWNER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const RPC = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function safeError(e: any): string {
    return e?.response?.data?.result ||
           e?.message ||
           String(e);
}

function fmt9(n: bigint): string {
    const whole = n / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (n % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${frac}`;
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: RPC,
    });

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP SHORT INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    // ============================================================
    // 1
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n[1] get_jetton_data');

    try {
        const r = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_jetton_data',
            []
        );

        const totalSupply = r.stack.readBigNumber();
        const mintable = r.stack.readBigNumber();
        const admin = r.stack.readAddress();

        console.log('TOTAL_SUPPLY_RAW =', totalSupply.toString());
        console.log('TOTAL_SUPPLY_1e9 =', fmt9(totalSupply));
        console.log('MINTABLE =', mintable.toString());
        console.log('ADMIN =', admin.toString());

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR =', safeError(e));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 2
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n[2] get_pool_data');

    try {
        const r = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_pool_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('STACK_ITEMS =', r.stack.remaining);

        while (r.stack.remaining > 0) {
            try {
                console.log('ITEM =', r.stack.readBigNumber().toString());
            } catch {
                try {
                    console.log('ITEM =', r.stack.readAddress()?.toString());
                } catch {
                    console.log('ITEM = [non-numeric/non-address]');
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR =', safeError(e));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 3
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n[3] get_wallet_address');

    try {
        const r = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_wallet_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(OWNER)
                        .endCell(),
                },
            ]
        );

        const calculated = r.stack.readAddress();

        console.log('CALCULATED =', calculated.toString());
        console.log('KNOWN      =', LP_WALLET.toString());
        console.log(
            'MATCH      =',
            calculated.equals(LP_WALLET)
        );

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR =', safeError(e));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 4
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n[4] get_lp_account_address');

    try {
        const r = await client.runMethod(
            POOL,
            'get_lp_account_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(OWNER)
                        .endCell(),
                },
            ]
        );

        const lpAccount = r.stack.readAddress();

        console.log('LP_ACCOUNT =', lpAccount.toString());

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR =', safeError(e));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 5
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n[5] LP wallet get_wallet_data');

    try {
        const r = await client.runMethod(
            LP_WALLET,
            'get_wallet_data',
            []
        );

        const balance = r.stack.readBigNumber();
        const owner = r.stack.readAddress();
        const master = r.stack.readAddress();

        console.log('BALANCE_RAW =', balance.toString());
        console.log('BALANCE_1e9 =', fmt9(balance));
        console.log('OWNER       =', owner.toString());
        console.log('MASTER      =', master.toString());

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR =', safeError(e));
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectLpWalletGetterV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET =
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

async function rpc(method: string, params: any) {
    const response = await fetch(ENDPOINT, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            jsonrpc: '2.0',
            id: 1,
            method,
            params,
        }),
    });

    return await response.json();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET GETTER');
    console.log(' get_wallet_data()');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nLP Wallet:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET);

    console.log('\nCalling get_wallet_data...');

    const result = await rpc(
        'runGetMethod',
        {
            address: LP_WALLET,
            method: 'get_wallet_data',
            stack: [],
        }
    );

    console.log('\nRAW GETTER RESPONSE:');
    console.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));

    if (!result.ok || !result.result) {
        console.log('\nGETTER FAILED');
        return;
    }

    const stack = result.result.stack ?? [];

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('GETTER STACK');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log(
        JSON.stringify(stack, null, 2)
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('EXPECTED LP WALLET DATA');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log(`
Expected get_wallet_data() result:

[0] balance
[1] owner_address
[2] master_address
[3] wallet_code
`);

    if (stack.length >= 1) {
        console.log(
            '\n[0] LP BALANCE RAW:'
        );
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(stack[0], null, 2)
        );
    }

    if (stack.length >= 2) {
        console.log(
            '\n[1] OWNER RAW:'
        );
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(stack[1], null, 2)
        );
    }

    if (stack.length >= 3) {
        console.log(
            '\n[2] MASTER / POOL RAW:'
        );
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(stack[2], null, 2)
        );
    }

    if (stack.length >= 4) {
        console.log(
            '\n[3] WALLET CODE RAW:'
        );
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(stack[3], null, 2)
        );
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('GETTER COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET_RAW =
    '0:8D9D78729EAE46ED167C82585748D83A7976F2D64CEA0FEA7C1CDC6EE8F37948';

const POOL_RAW =
    '0:F900B682B84A89ECC8BDAA55CE03B04732B4EAF1ED9F67907FB5E58DD7B319AB';

const EXPECTED_AMOUNT = '146643762880';

const API =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions';

function friendly(raw: string) {
    try {
        return Address.parse(raw).toString({
            urlSafe: true,
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        });
    } catch {
        return raw;
    }
}

function printSeparator() {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

function printMessage(msg: any, index: number) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`MESSAGE #${index}`);
    console.log('-'.repeat(72));

    console.log('hash        =', msg.hash);
    console.log('source      =', msg.source);
    console.log('destination =', msg.destination);
    console.log('value       =', msg.value);
    console.log('created_lt  =', msg.created_lt);
    console.log('opcode      =', msg.opcode);
    console.log('decoded     =', msg.decoded_opcode);
    console.log('bounced     =', msg.bounced);

    if (msg.message_content?.decoded) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('DECODED BODY:');

        console.dir(
            msg.message_content.decoded,
            {
                depth: null,
                colors: false,
            }
        );
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    printSeparator();

    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET RECEIVE INSPECTOR'
    );

    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    printSeparator();

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP WALLET RAW:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET_RAW);

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP WALLET FRIENDLY:');
    console.log(friendly(LP_WALLET_RAW));

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL RAW:');
    console.log(POOL_RAW);

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED LP TRANSFER:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_AMOUNT);

    const url =
        API +
        '?account=' +
        encodeURIComponent(LP_WALLET_RAW) +
        '&limit=20';

    console.log('');
    console.log('GET:');
    console.log(url);

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const raw = await response.json();

    console.log('');
    console.log('RESPONSE TYPE:');
    console.log(
        Array.isArray(raw)
            ? 'ARRAY'
            : typeof raw
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('RESPONSE KEYS:');

    if (
        raw &&
        typeof raw === 'object' &&
        !Array.isArray(raw)
    ) {
        console.log(
            Object.keys(raw)
        );
    }

    // TON Center v3 returns:
    // {
    //   transactions: [...]
    // }

    const transactions: any[] =
        Array.isArray(raw)
            ? raw
            : Array.isArray(raw.transactions)
                ? raw.transactions
                : [];

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TRANSACTIONS FOUND:',
        transactions.length
    );

    if (transactions.length === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'NO TRANSACTIONS FOUND FOR THIS LP WALLET.'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'RAW RESPONSE:'
        );

        console.dir(
            raw,
            {
                depth: 4,
                colors: false,
            }
        );

        printSeparator();
        console.log(
            ' END OF READ-ONLY LP WALLET INSPECTION'
        );
        printSeparator();

        return;
    }

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < transactions.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const tx = transactions[i];

        printSeparator();

        console.log(
            `TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`
        );

        console.log(
            'account         =',
            tx.account
        );

        console.log(
            'hash            =',
            tx.hash
        );

        console.log(
            'lt              =',
            tx.lt
        );

        console.log(
            'prev_trans_lt   =',
            tx.prev_trans_lt
        );

        console.log(
            'now             =',
            tx.now
        );

        console.log(
            'total_fees      =',
            tx.total_fees
        );

        console.log(
            'aborted         =',
            tx.description?.aborted
        );

        console.log(
            'compute_success =',
            tx.description?.compute_ph?.success
        );

        console.log(
            'compute_exit    =',
            tx.description?.compute_ph?.exit_code
        );

        console.log(
            'action_success  =',
            tx.description?.action?.success
        );

        console.log(
            'action_result   =',
            tx.description?.action?.result_code
        );

        console.log(
            'finality        =',
            tx.finality
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'ACCOUNT STATE AFTER:'
        );

        if (tx.account_state_after) {
            console.log(
                'balance         =',
                tx.account_state_after.balance
            );

            console.log(
                'account_status  =',
                tx.account_state_after.account_status
            );

            console.log(
                'code_hash       =',
                tx.account_state_after.code_hash
            );

            console.log(
                'data_hash       =',
                tx.account_state_after.data_hash
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                'NO ACCOUNT STATE AFTER'
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'INCOMING MESSAGE:'
        );

        if (tx.in_msg) {
            printMessage(
                tx.in_msg,
                1
            );

            const decoded =
                tx.in_msg
                    ?.message_content
                    ?.decoded;

            const amount =
                decoded?.amount?.value;

            if (
                tx.in_msg.source === POOL_RAW &&
                tx.in_msg.opcode === '0x178d4519'
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '*** MATCH: POOL -> LP WALLET ***'
                );

                console.log(
                    'transfer amount =',
                    amount ??
                    'NOT DECODED'
                );

                console.log(
                    'expected amount =',
                    EXPECTED_AMOUNT
                );

                console.log(
                    'amount match    =',
                    amount === EXPECTED_AMOUNT
                        ? 'YES'
                        : 'NO'
                );
            }
        } else {
            console.log(
                'NO INCOMING MESSAGE'
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'OUTGOING MESSAGES:'
        );

        if (
            Array.isArray(tx.out_msgs)
        ) {
            console.log(
                'count =',
                tx.out_msgs.length
            );

            tx.out_msgs.forEach(
                (msg: any, j: number) => {
                    printMessage(
                        msg,
                        j + 1
                    );
                }
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                'NO OUTGOING MESSAGES'
            );
        }
    }

    printSeparator();

    console.log(
        ' END OF READ-ONLY LP WALLET INSPECTION'
    );

    printSeparator();
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET =
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

async function rpc(
    method: string,
    params: any
) {
    const response = await fetch(ENDPOINT, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            jsonrpc: '2.0',
            id: 1,
            method,
            params,
        }),
    });

    return await response.json();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET STATE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nLP Wallet:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET);

    console.log('\nREQUESTING ACCOUNT STATE...');

    const result = await rpc(
        'getAddressInformation',
        {
            address: LP_WALLET,
        }
    );

    console.log('\nRAW RPC RESPONSE:');
    console.log(
        JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)
    );

    if (
        !result.result ||
        result.result.state !== 'active'
    ) {
        console.log('\nAccount is not active or result is unavailable.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('ACCOUNT SUMMARY');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nState:');
    console.log(result.result.state);

    console.log('\nBalance (nanoTON):');
    console.log(result.result.balance);

    console.log('\nBalance (TON):');
    console.log(
        (
            Number(result.result.balance) / 1e9
        ).toFixed(9)
    );

    console.log('\nCode BOC:');
    console.log(
        result.result.code ?? 'NOT RETURNED'
    );

    console.log('\nData BOC:');
    console.log(
        result.result.data ?? 'NOT RETURNED'
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('STATE INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectLpWalletTxV22.ts
