import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET TRANSACTION TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('\nLP Wallet:', LP_WALLET.toString());

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(LP_WALLET, {
        limit: 20,
        archival: true,
    });

    console.log('\nFetched:', txs.length);

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('LT:', lt);
        console.log('hash:', tx.hash().toString('hex'));
        console.log('utime:', tx.now);

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            const info: any = tx.inMessage.info;

            console.log('\nIN');
            console.log('type:', info.type);

            if (info.src) {
                try {
                    console.log('src:', info.src.toString());
                } catch {}
            }

            if (tx.inMessage.body) {
                console.log(
                    'body:',
                    tx.inMessage.body
                        .toBoc({ idx: false })
                        .toString('base64')
                );
            }
        }

        if (tx.outMessages) {
            let i = 0;

            for (const [key, msg] of tx.outMessages) {
                const info: any = msg.info;

                console.log(`\nOUT #${i++}`);
                console.log('key:', key);
                console.log('type:', info.type);

                if (info.dest) {
                    try {
                        console.log(
                            'dest:',
                            info.dest.toString()
                        );
                    } catch {}
                }

                if (msg.body) {
                    console.log(
                        'body:',
                        msg.body
                            .toBoc({ idx: false })
                            .toString('base64')
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('TARGET POOL TX LT:', TARGET_LT.toString());
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectLpWalletV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET =
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd';

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

const OPCODE_LP_TRANSFER = 0x178d4519;

function opcodeHex(value: number): string {
    return '0x' + value.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function nanoToTon(value: string | number | bigint): string {
    return (Number(value) / 1e9).toFixed(9);
}

function decodeBody(bodyBase64: string) {
    try {
        const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(bodyBase64, 'base64')
        )[0];

        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        let queryId: bigint | null = null;

        if (slice.remainingBits >= 64) {
            queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);
        }

        return {
            opcode,
            queryId,
            remainingBits: slice.remainingBits,
            remainingRefs: slice.remainingRefs,
        };
    } catch (e) {
        return {
            error: String(e),
        };
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET MESSAGE TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nLP Wallet:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET);

    console.log('\nTarget Pool LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    console.log('\nExpected opcode:');
    console.log(opcodeHex(OPCODE_LP_TRANSFER));

    console.log('\nFETCHING LP WALLET TRANSACTIONS...');

    const response = await fetch(TONCENTER, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            address: LP_WALLET,
            limit: 30,
        }),
    });

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        console.log('\nTONCENTER ERROR:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(json, null, 2));
        return;
    }

    const transactions = json.result ?? [];

    console.log(
        `Fetched ${transactions.length} recent LP Wallet transactions.`
    );

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of transactions) {
        const lt = BigInt(
            tx.transaction_id?.lt ?? 0
        );

        /*
         * We are interested in the LP Wallet transaction
         * immediately after Pool LT 99809465000012.
         *
         * The exact LT may differ because account LT is local,
         * so inspect a small surrounding window.
         */
        if (
            lt < TARGET_LT - 20n ||
            lt > TARGET_LT + 20n
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        found++;

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
        console.log(`TRANSACTION LT: ${lt}`);
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        console.log('\nHASH:');
        console.log(
            tx.transaction_id?.hash ?? ''
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // INCOMING
        // --------------------------------------------------

        const inMsg = tx.in_msg;

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('INCOMING MESSAGE');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        if (!inMsg) {
            console.log('No incoming message.');
        } else {
            console.log('Source:');
            console.log(inMsg.source ?? 'null');

            console.log('Destination:');
            console.log(inMsg.destination ?? 'null');

            console.log('Value (nanoTON):');
            console.log(inMsg.value ?? '0');

            console.log('Value (TON):');
            console.log(
                nanoToTon(inMsg.value ?? '0')
            );

            console.log('Message type:');
            console.log(inMsg.msg_type ?? '');

            const body =
                inMsg.message_content?.body;

            if (!body) {
                console.log('\nNo body BOC.');
            } else {
                console.log('\nBody BOC HEX:');
                console.log(body);

                const decoded =
                    decodeBody(body);

                console.log('\nDecoded BODY HEADER:');

                if ('error' in decoded) {
                    console.log(
                        'Decode error:',
                        decoded.error
                    );
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        'Opcode:',
                        opcodeHex(decoded.opcode)
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'Query ID:',
                        decoded.queryId?.toString() ??
                            'not available'
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'Remaining bits:',
                        decoded.remainingBits
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'Remaining refs:',
                        decoded.remainingRefs
                    );

                    if (
                        decoded.opcode ===
                        OPCODE_LP_TRANSFER
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            '\n*** MATCH: 0x178d4519 ***'
                        );

                        console.log(
                            'This transaction contains the expected LP Wallet message.'
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // OUTGOING
        // --------------------------------------------------

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('OUTGOING MESSAGES');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        const outMsgs =
            tx.out_msgs ?? [];

        if (outMsgs.length === 0) {
            console.log('No outgoing messages.');
        }

        let index = 0;

        for (const msg of outMsgs) {
            index++;

            console.log(
                `\nOUTGOING MESSAGE #${index}`
            );

            console.log('Source:');
            console.log(msg.source ?? '');

            console.log('Destination:');
            console.log(msg.destination ?? '');

            console.log('Value (nanoTON):');
            console.log(msg.value ?? '0');

            console.log('Value (TON):');
            console.log(
                nanoToTon(msg.value ?? '0')
            );

            console.log('Message type:');
            console.log(msg.msg_type ?? '');

            const body =
                msg.message_content?.body;

            if (!body) {
                console.log('No body BOC.');
                continue;
            }

            console.log('\nBody BOC HEX:');
            console.log(body);

            const decoded =
                decodeBody(body);

            if ('error' in decoded) {
                console.log(
                    '\nDecode error:',
                    decoded.error
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    '\nDecoded opcode:',
                    opcodeHex(decoded.opcode)
                );

                console.log(
                    'Query ID:',
                    decoded.queryId?.toString() ??
                        'not available'
                );

                console.log(
                    'Remaining bits:',
                    decoded.remainingBits
                );

                console.log(
                    'Remaining refs:',
                    decoded.remainingRefs
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));

    if (found === 0) {
        console.log(
            'NO LP WALLET TRANSACTION FOUND IN TARGET LT WINDOW.'
        );
        console.log(
            'The LP Wallet has a different LT sequence.'
        );
        console.log(
            'We will widen the search window in the next step.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `FOUND ${found} LP WALLET TRANSACTION(S).`
        );
    }

    console.log('TRACE COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectMasterchainLibraries.ts

import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

export async function run() {
    const server = {
        host: 'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842',
        key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM='
    };

    console.log('===== MASTERCHAIN LIBRARY ENVIRONMENT =====');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: server.host,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
        reconnectTimeout: 3000
    });

    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        if (engine.isReady()) break;
        await sleep(500);
    }

    console.log('Engine ready:', engine.isReady());

    if (!engine.isReady()) {
        console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
        return;
    }

    const client = new LiteClient({ engine });

    const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('Masterchain seqno:', info.last.seqno);
    console.log('Masterchain workchain:', info.last.workchain);
    console.log('Masterchain shard:', info.last.shard.toString());

    console.log('\nRaw masterchain info:');
    console.log(info);
}

FILE: scripts\inspectMasterchainLibrary.ts

import { LiteClient, LiteRoundRobinEngine, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const LIBRARY_HASH =
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

const engines = [
    new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
            'base64'
        ),
    }),

    new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
            'base64'
        ),
    }),
];

const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);
const client = new LiteClient({ engine });

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' MASTERCHAIN LIBRARY INSPECTION');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Target Library Hash:');
    console.log(LIBRARY_HASH);
    console.log('');

    console.log('Getting masterchain info...');

    const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('Masterchain seqno:', master.last.seqno);
    console.log('Root hash:', Buffer.from(master.last.rootHash).toString('hex'));
    console.log('File hash:', Buffer.from(master.last.fileHash).toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    console.log('Querying getLibraries()...');

    const result = await client.getLibraries([
        Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH, 'hex'),
    ]);

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' RAW RESULT');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log(result);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Result kind:', result.kind);
    console.log('Library count:', result.result.length);

    if (result.result.length === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('LIBRARY NOT RETURNED');
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'The LiteServer did not return a public library entry'
        );
        console.log('for this hash.');
    } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < result.result.length; i++) {
            const item = result.result[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`LIBRARY ENTRY #${i + 1}`);
            console.log('Hash:', Buffer.from(item.hash).toString('hex'));
            console.log('Data bytes:', item.data.length);

            const data = Buffer.from(item.data);

            console.log('Data hex:', data.toString('hex'));

            try {
                const cell = Cell.fromBoc(data)[0];

                console.log('BOC decoded successfully');
                console.log('Cell bits:', cell.bits.length);
                console.log('Cell refs:', cell.refs.length);
                console.log(
                    'Cell hash:',
                    cell.hash().toString('hex')
                );
            } catch (e) {
                console.log('Could not decode library data as BOC');
                console.log(String(e));
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' TEST FINISHED');
    console.log('========================================');

    await engine.close();
}

main().catch(async (e) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('ERROR:');
    console.error(e);

    try {
        await engine.close();
    } catch {}

    process.exit(1);
});

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    // STON.fi Testnet CPI Router v2.1.0
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    // Rooster Coin Master
    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    // STON.fi Testnet pTON v2.1
    const ptonAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
    );

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi TESTNET - POOL BY MINTERS');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('\nRouter:');
    console.log(routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('\nROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(roosterAddress.toString());

    console.log('\npTON Master:');
    console.log(ptonAddress.toString());

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress)
    );

    console.log('\nChecking getPoolAddressByJettonMinters...');

    try {
        const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters({
            token0: roosterAddress,
            token1: ptonAddress,
        });

        console.log('\n========================================');
        console.log(' POOL ADDRESS FOUND');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(poolAddress.toString());

        const poolState = await provider.provider(poolAddress).getState();

        console.log('\nPool State:');
        console.log(poolState.state);

        if (poolState.state.type === 'active') {
            console.log('\nPool is ACTIVE.');

            try {
                const pool = provider.open(
                    DEX.v2_1.Pool.CPI.create(poolAddress)
                );

                const poolData = await pool.getPoolData();

                console.log('\nPool Data:');
                console.log(poolData);
            } catch (error) {
                console.log('\nCould not read Pool Data:');
                console.log(error);
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('\n========================================');
        console.log(' FAILED');
        console.log('========================================');

        console.log(error);
    }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPoolCallbackFlowV22.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const TARGET_CALLBACK_LT = '99809465000012';

const OP_CALLBACK = 0x06ecd527;
const OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;
const OP_JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER = 0x178d4519;
const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const OP_EXCESSES = 0xd53276db;

function getOpcode(body: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();
        if (s.remainingBits < 32) return null;
        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function hexOpcode(op: number | null): string {
    return op === null
        ? 'UNKNOWN'
        : '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function fmt(value: bigint): string {
    return value.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

function fmt9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction
        ? `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toLocaleString();
}

function decodeCallback(body: Cell) {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.loadUint(32) !== OP_CALLBACK) return;

        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
        const amount0 = s.loadCoins();
        const amount1 = s.loadCoins();
        const user = s.loadAddress();
        const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();
        const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();
        const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

        if (hasCustomPayload) s.loadRef();

        const additional = s.loadRef();

        console.log('');
        console.log('CALLBACK FIELDS:');
        console.log('query_id:', queryId.toString());
        console.log('tot_am0 raw:', fmt(amount0));
        console.log('tot_am0 token:', fmt9(amount0));
        console.log('tot_am1 raw:', fmt(amount1));
        console.log('tot_am1 token:', fmt9(amount1));
        console.log('user:', user?.toString() ?? 'NULL');
        console.log('min_lp_out:', fmt(minLpOut));
        console.log('fwd_amount:', fmt(fwdAmount));
        console.log('custom_payload:', hasCustomPayload ? 'YES' : 'NO');
        console.log('additional bits:', additional.bits.length);
        console.log('additional refs:', additional.refs.length);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('CALLBACK DECODE FAILED:', String(e));
    }
}

function printMessage(label: string, message: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`--- ${label} ---`);

    if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
        console.log('source:', message.info.src.toString());
        console.log('destination:', message.info.dest.toString());
        console.log('TON value:', message.info.value.coins.toString());
    } else {
        console.log('message type:', message.info.type);
    }

    const body: Cell = message.body;
    const opcode = getOpcode(body);

    console.log('opcode:', hexOpcode(opcode));
    console.log('body:', `${body.bits.length} bits / ${body.refs.length} refs`);
    console.log('BOC:', body.toBoc().toString('base64'));

    if (opcode === OP_CALLBACK) {
        decodeCallback(body);
    } else if (opcode === OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
        console.log('message type: STON.fi ADD_LIQUIDITY');
    } else if (opcode === OP_JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER) {
        console.log('message type: Jetton internal_transfer');
    } else if (opcode === OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
        console.log('message type: Jetton transfer');
    } else if (opcode === OP_EXCESSES) {
        console.log('message type: Excesses');
    } else {
        console.log('message type: other / unknown');
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 POOL CALLBACK FLOW INSPECTOR');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('POOL:', POOL.toString());
    console.log('LP ACCOUNT:', LP_ACCOUNT.toString());
    console.log('TARGET CALLBACK LT:', TARGET_CALLBACK_LT);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(POOL, {
        limit: 30,
    });

    console.log('TRANSACTIONS FETCHED:', transactions.length);

    let callbackCount = 0;
    let txIndex = 0;

    for (const tx of transactions) {
        txIndex++;

        const lt = tx.lt.toString();
        const hash = tx.hash().toString('base64');

        const inMessage = tx.inMessage;
        const inOpcode = inMessage ? getOpcode(inMessage.body) : null;

        const relevant =
            inOpcode === OP_CALLBACK ||
            inOpcode === OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY ||
            lt.startsWith('99809465');

        if (!relevant) continue;

        console.log('');
        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(`POOL TX #${txIndex}`);
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log('LT:', lt);
        console.log('HASH:', hash);
        console.log('TIMESTAMP:', tx.now.toString());
        console.log('INCOMING OPCODE:', hexOpcode(inOpcode));

        if (inMessage) {
            printMessage('POOL INCOMING MESSAGE', inMessage);

            if (
                inOpcode === OP_CALLBACK &&
                inMessage.info.type === 'internal' &&
                inMessage.info.src.equals(LP_ACCOUNT)
            ) {
                callbackCount++;
                console.log('');
                console.log('*** CALLBACK SOURCE MATCHES LP ACCOUNT ***');
            }
        }

        let outIndex = 0;
        for (const outMessage of tx.outMessages.values()) {
            outIndex++;
            printMessage(`POOL OUTGOING MESSAGE #${outIndex}`, outMessage);
        }

        const description: any = tx.description;
        console.log('');
        console.log('TRANSACTION STATUS:');
        console.log('description type:', description.type);

        if (description.type === 'generic') {
            console.log('aborted:', description.aborted);
            console.log('destroyed:', description.destroyed);

            if (description.computePhase?.type === 'vm') {
                console.log(
                    'compute exit code:',
                    description.computePhase.exitCode
                );
                console.log(
                    'compute success:',
                    description.computePhase.success
                );
            }

            if (description.actionPhase) {
                console.log(
                    'action result code:',
                    description.actionPhase.resultCode
                );
                console.log(
                    'action success:',
                    description.actionPhase.success
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('POOL CALLBACK FLOW SUMMARY');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('Matching callback messages found:', callbackCount);
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPoolCallbackStructureV22.ts

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101020100a700017906ecd52700000000000000006b60e887f16c4303d034801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86e811e4f8a2080100c9801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18f003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1bc049d92b3f';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 CALLBACK BOC STRUCTURE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const bytes = Buffer.from(BOC, 'hex');

    console.log('\nHEX LENGTH:', BOC.length);
    console.log('BYTE LENGTH:', bytes.length);
    console.log('MAGIC:', bytes.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex'));

    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(bytes);

    console.log('\nROOT COUNT:', roots.length);

    function inspectCell(cell: Cell, name: string, depth: number) {
        const indent = ' '.repeat(depth * 2);

        console.log(`\n${indent}${name}`);
        console.log(`${indent}bits = ${cell.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`${indent}refs = ${cell.refs.length}`);

        console.log(
            `${indent}BOC = ${cell.toBoc().toString('base64')}`
        );

        console.log(
            `${indent}HEX DATA = ${cell.bits.toString()}`
        );

        cell.refs.forEach((ref, i) => {
            inspectCell(
                ref,
                `${name}.ref[${i}]`,
                depth + 1
            );
        });
    }

    inspectCell(roots[0], 'ROOT', 0);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPoolCellStructureV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000009n;

function hexBits(cell: any): string {
    try {
        const bytes = cell.bits.toBuffer();
        return bytes.toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return '<UNAVAILABLE>';
    }
}

function dumpCell(cell: any, name: string, depth = 0) {
    const pad = ' '.repeat(depth * 2);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`${pad}===== ${name} =====`);
    console.log(`${pad}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`${pad}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`${pad}hex:  ${hexBits(cell)}`);

    try {
        console.log(
            `${pad}boc:  ${cell.toBoc().toString('base64')}`
        );
    } catch {
        console.log(`${pad}boc: <ERROR>`);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        dumpCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${name}.ref[${i}]`,
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

function dumpSliceBits(cell: any, name: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`===== ${name} BIT STREAM =====`);

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    let index = 0;

    while (s.remainingBits >= 8) {
        try {
            const value = s.loadUint(8);

            console.log(
                `${index.toString().padStart(3, '0')}: ` +
                `0x${value.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')} ` +
                `(${value})`
            );

            index++;
        } catch {
            break;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `Remaining bits after byte dump: ${s.remainingBits}`
    );
}

function dumpTopLevelFields(cell: any, name: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`===== ${name} TOP LEVEL =====`);

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const op = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            `opcode: 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
        );
    }

    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
        const qid = s.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log(
            `query_id: ${qid.toString()}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `remaining bits: ${s.remainingBits}`
    );

    console.log(
        `remaining refs: ${s.remainingRefs}`
    );
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 POOL CELL STRUCTURE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log(`POOL: ${POOL.toString()}`);
    console.log(`TARGET LT: ${TARGET_LT.toString()}`);

    const client = provider.api() as TonClient;

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    const tx = txs.find(
        t => t.lt === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log(`LT: ${tx.lt.toString()}`);
    console.log(
        `HASH: ${tx.hash().toString('base64')}`
    );

    if (tx.now) {
        console.log(
            `TIME: ${new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()}`
        );
    }

    if (tx.inMessage) {
        const body = tx.inMessage.body;

        dumpTopLevelFields(
            body,
            'INPUT'
        );

        dumpCell(
            body,
            'INPUT CELL'
        );

        dumpSliceBits(
            body,
            'INPUT'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('OUTPUT MESSAGES');
    console.log('================================================');

    for (let i = 0; i < tx.outMessagesCount; i++) {
        const msg = tx.outMessages.get(i);

        if (!msg) continue;

        const body = msg.body;

        dumpTopLevelFields(
            body,
            `OUTPUT #${i + 1}`
        );

        dumpCell(
            body,
            `OUTPUT #${i + 1} CELL`
        );

        dumpSliceBits(
            body,
            `OUTPUT #${i + 1}`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log(' Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('================================================');
}

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx';

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== INSPECT V2.2 POOL-CREATION ROUTERS =====');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:', ROOSTER_MASTER);
    console.log('');

    const api = new StonApiClient({
        baseURL: 'https://api.ston.fi',
    });

    try {
        const routers = await api.getRouters({
            dexV2: true,
        });

        console.log('TOTAL ROUTERS:', routers.length);

        const candidates = routers.filter((router) =>
            router.majorVersion === 2 &&
            router.minorVersion === 2 &&
            router.routerType === 'ConstantProduct' &&
            router.poolCreationEnabled === true
        );

        console.log('POOL-CREATION V2.2 CPI CANDIDATES:', candidates.length);
        console.log('');

        candidates.forEach((router, index) => {
            console.log('==================================================');

            console.log('==================================================');

            console.log('Address:', router.address);
            console.log(
                'Version:',
                `${router.majorVersion}.${router.minorVersion}`,
            );
            console.log('Router Type:', router.routerType);
            console.log('Pool Creation Enabled:', router.poolCreationEnabled);
            console.log('pTON Version:', router.ptonVersion);
            console.log('pTON Master:', router.ptonMasterAddress);
            console.log('pTON Wallet:', router.ptonWalletAddress);

            console.log('');
            console.log('ALL API FIELDS:');

            for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(router)) {
                console.log(`  ${key}:`, value);
            }

            console.log('');
        });

        console.log('==================================================');
        console.log('SUMMARY');
        console.log('==================================================');

        candidates.forEach((router, index) => {
            console.log(
                `#${index + 1}`,
                router.address,
                '|',
                `${router.majorVersion}.${router.minorVersion}`,
                '|',
                router.routerType,
                '|',
                'poolCreation:',
                router.poolCreationEnabled,
                '|',
                'pTON:',
                router.ptonMasterAddress,
                '|',
                'pTON wallet:',
                router.ptonWalletAddress,
            );
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== INSPECTION COMPLETE =====');
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'API ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e),
        );
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const POOL =
    'EQC16KrrZUbN4HQaWrDOr2GfB9WDb8m6u1T3uw2qQn_LpFQ0';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI POOL INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const pool = Address.parse(POOL);

    const latest =
        await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        `MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${latest.last.seqno}`,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`POOL: ${pool.toString()}`);
    console.log(`RAW : ${pool.toRawString()}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log('================ ACCOUNT STATE =================');

    const account =
        await client.getAccount(
            latest.last.seqno,
            pool,
        );

    console.log(
        `ACCOUNT STATE : ${account.account.state.type}`,
    );

    if (account.account.state.type === 'active') {
        console.log('STATUS        : ACTIVE');

        console.log(
            `BALANCE       : ${account.account.balance.coins}`,
        );

        console.log(
            `CODE          : ${
                account.account.state.code
                    ? 'PRESENT'
                    : 'ABSENT'
            }`,
        );

        console.log(
            `DATA          : ${
                account.account.state.data
                    ? 'PRESENT'
                    : 'ABSENT'
            }`,
        );
    } else {
        console.log('Pool account is not active.');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================ GET_POOL_DATA =================');

    try {
        const result =
            await client.runMethod(
                latest.last.seqno,
                pool,
                'get_pool_data',
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `EXIT CODE: ${result.exitCode}`,
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('RESULT RAW:');
        console.log(result.resultRaw);

        if (result.exitCode === 0) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'GET_POOL_DATA: SUCCESS',
            );
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'GET_POOL_DATA: FAILED',
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'GET_POOL_DATA CALL ERROR:',
        );

        console.log(
            e instanceof Error
                ? e.message
                : String(e),
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINISHED - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPoolGettersV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    '0:f900b682b84a89ecc8bdaa55ce03b04732b4eaf1ed9f67907fb5e58dd7b319ab';

function friendly(address: Address) {
    return address.toString({
        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    });
}

async function tryGetter(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    address: Address,
    name: string,
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(`GETTER: ${name}`);
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    try {
        const result = await provider.provider(address).get(name, []);

        console.log('SUCCESS');

        console.dir(result.stack, {
            depth: null,
            colors: false,
        });

        return result.stack;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('FAILED');

        console.dir(e, {
            depth: null,
            colors: false,
        });

        return null;
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL GETTER INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    const address = Address.parse(POOL);

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL RAW:');
    console.log(address.toRawString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL FRIENDLY:');
    console.log(friendly(address));

    /*
     * First: standard Jetton Master getter.
     *
     * The Pool is also the LP Jetton Master.
     */
    await tryGetter(
        provider,
        address,
        'get_jetton_data',
    );

    /*
     * Possible Pool state getters.
     *
     * We intentionally try several names.
     * Failed getters are harmless and READ ONLY.
     */
    await tryGetter(
        provider,
        address,
        'get_pool_data',
    );

    await tryGetter(
        provider,
        address,
        'get_pool_state',
    );

    await tryGetter(
        provider,
        address,
        'get_pool_params',
    );

    await tryGetter(
        provider,
        address,
        'get_pool_address',
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' END OF READ-ONLY POOL GETTER INSPECTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPoolMessagesV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const LTS = [
    99809465000011n,
    99809465000012n,
];

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL MESSAGE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('\nPool:', POOL.toString());

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(POOL, {
        limit: 50,
        archival: true,
    });

    console.log(`\nFetched transactions: ${txs.length}`);

    for (const lt of LTS) {
        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
        console.log(`LT ${lt}`);
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        const tx = txs.find(
            (x: any) => BigInt(x.lt.toString()) === lt
        );

        if (!tx) {
            console.log('Transaction not found in returned range.');
            continue;
        }

        console.log('hash:', tx.hash().toString('hex'));
        console.log('lt:', tx.lt.toString());
        console.log('utime:', tx.now);

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // IN MESSAGE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('\nIN MESSAGE');
        console.log('----------');

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            const info: any = tx.inMessage.info;

            console.log('type:', info.type);

            if (info.src) {
                try {
                    console.log('src:', info.src.toString());
                } catch {}
            }

            if (tx.inMessage.body) {
                const boc = tx.inMessage.body
                    .toBoc({ idx: false })
                    .toString('base64');

                console.log('body BOC:');
                console.log(boc);
            } else {
                console.log('No body');
            }
        } else {
            console.log('No inMessage');
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // OUT MESSAGES
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('\nOUT MESSAGES');
        console.log('------------');

        if (!tx.outMessages) {
            console.log('No outMessages');
            continue;
        }

        let index = 0;

        for (const [key, msg] of tx.outMessages) {
            console.log(`\nOUT #${index++}`);
            console.log('key:', key);

            const info: any = msg.info;

            console.log('type:', info.type);

            if (info.dest) {
                try {
                    console.log('dest:', info.dest.toString());
                } catch {}
            }

            if (msg.body) {
                const boc = msg.body
                    .toBoc({ idx: false })
                    .toString('base64');

                console.log('body BOC:');
                console.log(boc);
            } else {
                console.log('No body');
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPoolMethodV22.ts

import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== SDK V2.2 POOL METHOD =====');

    const Router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI;

    let proto: any = Router.prototype;
    let level = 0;

    while (proto) {
        const names = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(proto);

        console.log(`\n--- PROTOTYPE LEVEL ${level} ---`);

        for (const name of names) {
            if (name === 'constructor') continue;

            const value = proto[name];

            if (typeof value === 'function') {
                console.log(`METHOD: ${name}`);
                console.log(value.toString());
            }
        }

        proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(proto);
        level++;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPoolMintPathV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const SDK = path.resolve(
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules\\@ston-fi\\sdk'
);

const TARGET_DIRS = [
    path.join(SDK, 'dist', 'contracts', 'dex', 'v2_1', 'pool'),
    path.join(SDK, 'dist', 'contracts', 'dex', 'v2_2', 'pool'),
    path.join(SDK, 'dist', 'contracts', 'dex', 'v2_1'),
    path.join(SDK, 'dist', 'contracts', 'dex', 'v2_2'),
];

const TARGETS = [
    'mint',
    'MINT',
    'burn',
    'BURN',
    'JettonMinter',
    'jetton_minter',
    'createMint',
    'mintLp',
    'mintLP',
    'LP',
    'liquidity'
];

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];

    const result: string[] = [];

    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
        const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            result.push(...walk(full));
        } else {
            result.push(full);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function isText(file: string): boolean {
    return [
        '.js',
        '.cjs',
        '.mjs',
        '.map',
        '.d.ts'
    ].includes(path.extname(file).toLowerCase());
}

function context(source: string, pos: number): string {
    const start = Math.max(0, pos - 350);
    const end = Math.min(source.length, pos + 700);

    return source
        .slice(start, end)
        .replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

async function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL / LP MINT PATH SEARCH');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LOCAL SDK ONLY');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');

    let files: string[] = [];

    for (const dir of TARGET_DIRS) {
        console.log(`SEARCH ROOT: ${dir}`);
        files.push(...walk(dir));
    }

    files = [...new Set(files)].filter(isText);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`FILES SCANNED: ${files.length}`);
    console.log('');

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
        console.log(`TARGET: ${target}`);
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

        let count = 0;

        for (const file of files) {
            let source: string;

            try {
                source = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
            } catch {
                continue;
            }

            let pos = 0;

            while (true) {
                const found = source.indexOf(target, pos);

                if (found === -1) break;

                count++;

                console.log('');
                console.log(`FILE: ${path.relative(SDK, file)}`);
                console.log(`OFFSET: ${found}`);
                console.log(`CONTEXT: ${context(source, found)}`);

                pos = found + Math.max(target.length, 1);

                if (count >= 40) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('... maximum 40 matches shown ...');
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(`MATCHES: ${count}`);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' SEARCH COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main();
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPoolOutgoingV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const LT_FROM = 99809465000008n;
const LT_TO   = 99809465000012n;

const ADD_LIQUIDITY_OPCODE = 0x50c6a654;

function nanoToTon(value: bigint): string {
    return (Number(value) / 1e9).toFixed(9);
}

function getOpcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();
        if (s.remainingBits < 32) return 'NO_OPCODE';
        return '0x' + s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_ERROR';
    }
}

function getBodyHex(body: any): string {
    try {
        return body.toBoc().toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return 'BOC_ERROR';
    }
}

function getAddress(msg: any): string {
    try {
        return msg?.info?.src?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE';
    } catch {
        return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
}

function getDestination(msg: any): string {
    try {
        return msg?.info?.dest?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE';
    } catch {
        return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
}

function getValue(msg: any): bigint {
    try {
        return BigInt(msg?.info?.value?.coins ?? 0);
    } catch {
        return 0n;
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL MESSAGE / BODY TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Pool Wallet:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log(`LT range: ${LT_FROM} -> ${LT_TO}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`ADD_LIQUIDITY opcode: 0x${ADD_LIQUIDITY_OPCODE.toString(16)}`);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('FETCHING POOL TRANSACTIONS...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
            archival: true,
        },
    );

    console.log(`Fetched ${transactions.length} recent Pool transactions.`);

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt < LT_FROM || lt > LT_TO) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log(`TRANSACTION LT: ${lt}`);
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // INCOMING MESSAGE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const inMsg: any = tx.inMessage;

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('INCOMING MESSAGE');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        if (!inMsg) {
            console.log('No incoming message.');
        } else {

            console.log('Source:');
            console.log(getAddress(inMsg));

            console.log('Destination:');
            console.log(getDestination(inMsg));

            const value = getValue(inMsg);

            console.log('Value (nanoTON):');
            console.log(value.toString());

            console.log('Value (TON):');
            console.log(nanoToTon(value));

            console.log('Opcode:');
            console.log(getOpcode(inMsg.body));

            console.log('Body BOC HEX:');
            console.log(getBodyHex(inMsg.body));
        }

              // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // OUTGOING MESSAGES
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('OUTGOING MESSAGES');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        const outMessages: any = tx.outMessages;

        if (!outMessages || outMessages.size === 0) {
            console.log('No outgoing messages.');
        } else {

            let outIndex = 0;

            for (const [key, msg] of outMessages) {

                outIndex++;

                console.log('');
                console.log(`OUTGOING MESSAGE #${outIndex}`);
                console.log(`Dictionary key: ${key}`);

                console.log('Source:');
                console.log(getAddress(msg));

                console.log('Destination:');
                console.log(getDestination(msg));

                const value = getValue(msg);

                console.log('Value (nanoTON):');
                console.log(value.toString());

                console.log('Value (TON):');
                console.log(nanoToTon(value));

                console.log('Opcode:');
                console.log(getOpcode(msg.body));

                console.log('Body BOC HEX:');
                console.log(getBodyHex(msg.body));
            }
        }
       
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('TRACE COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPoolState7219004800V22.ts

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_RAW = '7219004800';

const URL =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/accountStates';

function findNumber(
    value: any,
    path: string,
    target: string,
    results: string[]
) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
        return;
    }

    if (
        typeof value === 'string' ||
        typeof value === 'number'
    ) {
        if (String(value) === target) {
            results.push(path);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        value.forEach((v, i) =>
            findNumber(
                v,
                `${path}[${i}]`,
                target,
                results
            )
        );
        return;
    }

    if (typeof value === 'object') {
        for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
            findNumber(
                child,
                `${path}.${key}`,
                target,
                results
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL ACCOUNT STATE');
    console.log(' FIND 7.219004800 TON');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('\nPOOL:');
    console.log(POOL);

    console.log('\nTARGET RAW:');
    console.log(TARGET_RAW);

    console.log('\nFETCHING ACCOUNT STATE...');

    const url =
        `${URL}?address=${encodeURIComponent(POOL)}&include_boc=true`;

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        console.log('\nHTTP ERROR:', response.status);
        console.log(await response.text());
        return;
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    console.log(
        '\nACCOUNT OBJECTS:',
        json.accounts?.length ?? 0
    );

    const account = json.accounts?.[0];

    if (!account) {
        console.log('\nPOOL ACCOUNT NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('POOL ACCOUNT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('Address:', account.address);
    console.log('Balance RAW:', account.balance);
    console.log(
        'Balance TON:',
        Number(account.balance) / 1e9
    );
    console.log('Status:', account.status);
    console.log('Last LT:', account.last_transaction_lt);
    console.log('Last TX:', account.last_transaction_hash);
    console.log('Code hash:', account.code_hash);
    console.log('Data hash:', account.data_hash);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('SEARCH TARGET IN ACCOUNT JSON');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    const matches: string[] = [];

    findNumber(
        account,
        'account',
        TARGET_RAW,
        matches
    );

    console.log(
        'TARGET:',
        TARGET_RAW
    );

    console.log(
        'MATCH COUNT:',
        matches.length
    );

    if (matches.length) {
        console.log('\nMATCHES:');

        for (const path of matches) {
            console.log(path);
        }
    } else {
        console.log(
            '\n7219004800 NOT FOUND IN ACCOUNT STATE.'
        );
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('DATA BOC');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    if (account.data_boc) {
        console.log(
            'DATA BOC LENGTH:',
            account.data_boc.length
        );

        console.log(
            'DATA BOC:',
            account.data_boc
        );
    } else {
        console.log('No data_boc returned.');
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');

}

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const pton = pTON.v1.create(PTON_MASTER);

    // ContractProvider for the pTON master
    const ptonProvider = provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    const ptonWallet = await pton.getWalletAddress(
        ptonProvider,
        USER_WALLET
    );

    console.log('\n===== pTON GAS DIAGNOSTIC =====');

    console.log('pTON version:', pton.version);
    console.log('pTON master:', PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('User wallet:', USER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('User pTON wallet:', ptonWallet.toString());

    console.log('\n===== pTON WALLET STATE =====');

    try {
        const walletProvider = provider.provider(ptonWallet);
        const state = await walletProvider.getState();

        console.log('State:', state.state);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('State: unable to read');
    }

    console.log('\n===== SDK GAS CONSTANTS =====');

    console.log(
        'Deploy wallet gas:',
        pton.gasConstants.deployWallet.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Deploy wallet gas:',
        Number(pton.gasConstants.deployWallet) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('\n===== DEPLOY TX PARAMS =====');

    const deployTx = await pton.getDeployWalletTxParams(
        ptonProvider,
        {
            ownerAddress: USER_WALLET,
        }
    );

    console.log('Destination:', deployTx.to.toString());

    console.log(
        'Value nanoTON:',
        deployTx.value.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Value TON:',
        Number(deployTx.value) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    if (deployTx.body) {
        console.log(
            'Body BOC:',
            deployTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== TON TRANSFER TX PARAMS =====');

    const tonTransferTx = await pton.getTonTransferTxParams(
        ptonProvider,
        {
            tonAmount: toNano('0.1'),
            destinationAddress: USER_WALLET,
            refundAddress: USER_WALLET,
        }
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        tonTransferTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Value nanoTON:',
        tonTransferTx.value.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Value TON:',
        Number(tonTransferTx.value) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    if (tonTransferTx.body) {
        console.log(
            'Body BOC:',
            tonTransferTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== END: NO TRANSACTION SENT =====\n');
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ACCOUNT =
    '0:9220C181A6CFEACD11B7B8F62138DF1BB9CC82B6ED2661D2F5FAEE204B3EFB20';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000004';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI pTON ORIGIN TX LT 99809465000004');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const url =
        `https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions` +
        `?account=${ACCOUNT}` +
        `&limit=50` +
        `&include_msg_body=true` +
        `&include_actions=true`;

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const data: any = await response.json();
    const txs = data.transactions ?? [];

    const tx = txs.find(
        (t: any) => String(t.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        console.log(`ACCOUNT: ${ACCOUNT}`);
        console.log(`TARGET LT: ${TARGET_LT}`);
        return;
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log(`\nTX LT   : ${tx.lt}`);
    console.log(`HASH    : ${tx.hash}`);
    console.log(`ACCOUNT : ${ACCOUNT}`);

    console.log('\nSTATUS:');
    console.log(`aborted        : ${tx.aborted}`);
    console.log(`compute.success : ${tx.compute_phase?.success}`);
    console.log(`compute.exit    : ${tx.compute_phase?.exit_code}`);
    console.log(`action.success  : ${tx.action_phase?.success}`);
    console.log(`action.result   : ${tx.action_phase?.result_code}`);

    console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(70));
    console.log('IN MESSAGE');
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));

    if (tx.in_msg) {
        const m = tx.in_msg;

        console.log(`source       : ${m.source}`);
        console.log(`destination  : ${m.destination}`);
        console.log(`value        : ${m.value}`);
        console.log(`created_lt   : ${m.created_lt}`);
        console.log(`opcode       : ${m.opcode}`);
        console.log(`decoded      : ${m.decoded_opcode}`);

        console.log('\nIN MESSAGE BODY:');
        console.log(m.msg_data?.body ?? m.body ?? 'NO BODY');

        console.log('\nFULL IN MESSAGE:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(m, null, 2));
    }

    console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(70));
    console.log('OUT MESSAGES');
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));

    for (const [i, m] of (tx.out_msgs ?? []).entries()) {
        console.log(`\nOUT_MSG #${i}`);

        console.log(`source       : ${m.source}`);
        console.log(`destination  : ${m.destination}`);
        console.log(`value        : ${m.value}`);
        console.log(`created_lt   : ${m.created_lt}`);
        console.log(`opcode       : ${m.opcode}`);
        console.log(`decoded      : ${m.decoded_opcode}`);

        if (String(m.created_lt) === '99809465000005') {
            console.log('\n*** THIS IS THE TARGET MESSAGE ***');

            console.log('\nBODY:');
            console.log(m.msg_data?.body ?? m.body ?? 'NO BODY');

            console.log('\nFULL TARGET MESSAGE:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(m, null, 2));
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPtonPoolTargetV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const PTON_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const TARGET_TIME = 1790587381;

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function hex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';

    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function ton(v: bigint): string {
    return (
        Number(v) / 1e9
    ).toFixed(9);
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' pTON POOL TARGET-TIME INSPECTION');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log('==========================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Pool:');
    console.log(PTON_POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET TIMESTAMP:');
    console.log(TARGET_TIME);
    console.log(
        new Date(TARGET_TIME * 1000).toISOString()
    );

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        PTON_POOL,
        {
            limit: 30,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('FETCHED:', txs.length);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '===== TRANSACTIONS AROUND TARGET ====='
    );

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const delta =
            Math.abs(tx.now - TARGET_TIME);

        // Show transactions within 24 hours
        if (delta > 86400) {
            continue;
        }

        const inMsg = tx.inMessage;

        console.log('');
        console.log('------------------------------------------');

        console.log(
            'LT:',
            tx.lt.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'HASH:',
            tx.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log(
            'TIME:',
            tx.now
        );

        console.log(
            'UTC:',
            new Date(
                tx.now * 1000
            ).toISOString()
        );

        if (inMsg) {
            const op =
                getOpcode(inMsg.body);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'INPUT OPCODE:',
                hex(op)
            );

            if (
                inMsg.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'SOURCE:',
                    inMsg.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'DESTINATION:',
                    inMsg.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'TON VALUE:',
                    ton(
                        inMsg.info.value.coins
                    )
                );
            }

            console.log(
                'BODY:',
                inMsg.body.bits.length,
                'bits /',
                inMsg.body.refs.length,
                'refs'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('INPUT BOC:');

            console.log(
                inMsg.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('OUTGOING:');

        let n = 0;

        for (
            const outMsg
            of tx.outMessages.values()
        ) {
            const op =
                getOpcode(outMsg.body);

            console.log(
                `OUT #${n}:`
            );

            if (
                outMsg.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'DEST:',
                    outMsg.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'TON:',
                    ton(
                        outMsg.info.value.coins
                    )
                );
            }

            console.log(
                'OPCODE:',
                hex(op)
            );

            console.log(
                'BODY:',
                outMsg.body.bits.length,
                'bits /',
                outMsg.body.refs.length,
                'refs'
            );

            n++;
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPtonSource.ts
====================================================================================================
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';
import { join } from 'path';

export async function run() {
    console.log('\n===== INSTALLED STON.FI pTON V1 =====\n');

    const base = join(
        process.cwd(),
        'node_modules',
        '@ston-fi',
        'sdk',
        'dist',
        'contracts',
        'pTON',
        'v1'
    );

    const files = [
        'PtonV1.js',
        'PtonV1.d.ts',
    ];

    for (const file of files) {
        const path = join(base, file);

        console.log(`\n===== ${file} =====`);

        try {
            const text = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');

            const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

            for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
                const line = lines[i];

                if (
                    line.includes('getDeployWalletTxParams') ||
                    line.includes('getTonTransferTxParams') ||
                    line.includes('deployWallet') ||
                    line.includes('tonTransfer') ||
                    line.includes('gasConstants')
                ) {
                    const start = Math.max(0, i - 5);
                    const end = Math.min(lines.length, i + 25);

                    console.log(
                        lines.slice(start, end).join('\n')
                    );
                    console.log('\n----------------------------------------\n');
                }
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.log('Could not read:', path);
            console.log(error);
        }
    }

    console.log('\n===== END: READ-ONLY SOURCE INSPECTION =====\n');
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const SOURCE =
    '0:92E1411AE546892F33B2C8A89EA90390D8FF4CFBB917A643B91E73F706FDB9D1';

const LT = '99809465000006';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 pTON SOURCE LT 00006 INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const url =
        `https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions` +
        `?account=${SOURCE}` +
        `&lt=${LT}` +
        `&limit=1` +
        `&include_msg_body=true` +
        `&include_actions=true`;

    console.log('\nSOURCE:');
    console.log(SOURCE);

    console.log('\nTRANSACTION LT:');
    console.log(LT);

    console.log('\nQUERY:');
    console.log(url);

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const data: any = await response.json();
    const tx = (data.transactions ?? [])[0];

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('\nNO TRANSACTION FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('TRANSACTION FOUND');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nLT:');
    console.log(tx.lt);

    console.log('\nHASH:');
    console.log(tx.hash);

    console.log('\nPREV_LT:');
    console.log(tx.prev_trans_lt);

    console.log('\nIN_MSG:');
    console.log(JSON.stringify(tx.in_msg ?? null, null, 2));

    console.log('\nOUT_MSGS:');
    console.log(JSON.stringify(tx.out_msgs ?? null, null, 2));

    console.log('\nACTIONS:');
    console.log(JSON.stringify(tx.actions ?? null, null, 2));

    console.log('\nRAW TRANSACTION:');
    console.log(JSON.stringify(tx, null, 2));

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('IMPORTANT CHECKS');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    for (const [i, msg] of (tx.out_msgs ?? []).entries()) {
        console.log(`\nOUT_MSG #${i}`);

        console.log('created_lt:', msg.created_lt);
        console.log('source:', msg.source);
        console.log('destination:', msg.destination);
        console.log('value:', msg.value);
        console.log('opcode:', msg.opcode);
        console.log('message_content:', msg.message_content);
        console.log('raw_body:', msg.raw_body);

        if (String(msg.created_lt) === '99809465000007') {
            console.log('\n*** THIS IS THE MESSAGE TO POOL LT 00008 ***');
        }
    }
}
