import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const SOURCE =
    '0:92E1411AE546892F33B2C8A89EA90390D8FF4CFBB917A643B91E73F706FDB9D1';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000007';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 pTON SOURCE TRANSACTION SEARCH');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nSOURCE:');
    console.log(SOURCE);

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT);

    const url =
        `https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions` +
        `?account=${SOURCE}` +
        `&limit=50` +
        `&include_msg_body=true` +
        `&include_actions=true`;

    console.log('\nQUERY:');
    console.log(url);

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const data: any = await response.json();

    const txs = data.transactions ?? data;

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS RETURNED: ${txs.length}`);
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const lt = String(tx.lt ?? '');

        console.log('\n----------------------------------------');
        console.log(`LT       : ${lt}`);
        console.log(`HASH     : ${tx.hash ?? ''}`);
        console.log(`UTIME    : ${tx.utime ?? ''}`);
        console.log(`ABORTED  : ${tx.aborted ?? ''}`);

        if (lt === TARGET_LT) {
            console.log('\n*** TARGET LT FOUND ***');

            console.log('\nIN_MSG:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(tx.in_msg ?? null, null, 2));

            console.log('\nOUT_MSGS:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(tx.out_msgs ?? null, null, 2));

            console.log('\nACTIONS:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(tx.actions ?? null, null, 2));

            console.log('\nRAW TARGET TRANSACTION:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(tx, null, 2));
        }
    }

    const target = txs.find(
        (tx: any) => String(tx.lt ?? '') === TARGET_LT
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));

    if (!target) {
        console.log('TARGET LT NOT FOUND IN RETURNED TRANSACTIONS');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));

        console.log('\nReturned LTs:');
        for (const tx of txs) {
            console.log(String(tx.lt ?? ''));
        }

        console.log('\nThis means we should NOT assume LT 99809465000007');
        console.log('belongs to this source until the account history confirms it.');
    } else {
        console.log('TARGET LT FOUND SUCCESSFULLY');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));
    }
}

FILE: scripts\inspectPtonTransferOriginV22.ts

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ACCOUNT =
    '0:F19F930B6C5C95EB8F7E381BE242B329E4AA968C61CD57014CE864DE68B97C37';

const TARGET_CREATED_LT = '99809465000003';
const TARGET_OPCODE = '0x01f3835d';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI pTON TRANSFER ORIGIN SEARCH');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const url =
        `https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions` +
        `?account=${ACCOUNT}` +
        `&limit=50` +
        `&include_msg_body=true` +
        `&include_actions=true`;

    console.log('\nACCOUNT:');
    console.log(ACCOUNT);

    console.log('\nTARGET CREATED_LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_CREATED_LT);

    console.log('\nTARGET OPCODE:');
    console.log(TARGET_OPCODE);

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const data: any = await response.json();
    const txs = data.transactions ?? [];

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS RETURNED: ${txs.length}`);
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        for (const [i, msg] of (tx.out_msgs ?? []).entries()) {
            const createdLtMatch =
                String(msg.created_lt) === TARGET_CREATED_LT;

            const opcodeMatch =
                String(msg.opcode ?? '').toLowerCase() ===
                TARGET_OPCODE.toLowerCase();

            if (!createdLtMatch && !opcodeMatch) {
                continue;
            }

            found = true;

            console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
            console.log('*** MATCH FOUND ***');
            console.log('='.repeat(70));

            console.log(`\nTX LT       : ${tx.lt}`);
            console.log(`TX HASH     : ${tx.hash}`);
            console.log(`TRACE ID    : ${tx.trace_id}`);
            console.log(`OUT_MSG #   : ${i}`);

            console.log('\nMESSAGE:');
            console.log(`source      : ${msg.source}`);
            console.log(`destination : ${msg.destination}`);
            console.log(`created_lt  : ${msg.created_lt}`);
            console.log(`created_at  : ${msg.created_at}`);
            console.log(`value       : ${msg.value}`);
            console.log(`opcode      : ${msg.opcode}`);
            console.log(`decoded     : ${msg.decoded_opcode}`);

            console.log('\nFULL MESSAGE:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(msg, null, 2));

            console.log('\nFULL SOURCE TRANSACTION:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(tx, null, 2));
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));

    if (!found) {
        console.log('NO MATCH FOUND');
        console.log(
            'The target message was not found in the returned history.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log('ORIGIN SEARCH COMPLETE');
    }

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

import { Address, fromNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

const PTON_V1_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

// فقط برای ساخت پارامترها
// هیچ تراکنشی ارسال نمی‌شود.
const TEST_TON_AMOUNT = 100_000_000n; // 0.1 TON

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi pTON V1 READ-ONLY INSPECTION');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('User wallet:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON V1 address:');
    console.log(PTON_V1_ADDRESS.toString());

    const pton = pTON.v1.create(PTON_V1_ADDRESS);

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON V1 version:');
    console.log(pton.version);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Test TON amount:');
    console.log(TEST_TON_AMOUNT.toString(), 'nanoTON');

    console.log(
        'Test TON amount:',
        fromNano(TEST_TON_AMOUNT),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('BUILDING pTON TRANSFER PARAMETERS');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    const ptonProvider = provider.provider(
        PTON_V1_ADDRESS
    );

    const txParams = await pton.getTonTransferTxParams(
        ptonProvider,
        {
            tonAmount: TEST_TON_AMOUNT,
            destinationAddress: USER_WALLET,
            refundAddress: USER_WALLET,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('pTON TRANSACTION PARAMETERS');
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Destination (to):');
    console.log(txParams.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TON value:');
    console.log(txParams.value.toString(), 'nanoTON');

    console.log(
        'TON value:',
        fromNano(txParams.value),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Body exists:');
    console.log(txParams.body ? 'YES' : 'NO');

    if (txParams.body) {
        const bodyBoc = txParams.body.toBoc();

        console.log('');
        console.log('Body BOC:');
        console.log(bodyBoc.toString('base64'));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Body size:');
        console.log(bodyBoc.length, 'bytes');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO pTON WAS CREATED.');
    console.log('');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPtonV22Raw.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from "@ton/core";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";

const BOC_PATH = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "pton_v22_generated.boc"
);

export async function run() {
  console.log("");
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 pTON RAW BOC INSPECTOR");
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log("READ ONLY");
  console.log("NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");
  console.log("NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED.");
  console.log("");

  console.log(`BOC: ${BOC_PATH}`);

  if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_PATH)) {
    throw new Error(
      `BOC not found: ${BOC_PATH}`
    );
  }

  const boc =
    fs.readFileSync(BOC_PATH);

  console.log(`Raw bytes: ${boc.length}`);

  if (boc.length < 4) {
    throw new Error("BOC too short.");
  }

  const magic =
    boc.subarray(0, 4).toString("hex");

  console.log(`BOC magic: 0x${magic}`);

  if (magic !== "b5ee9c72") {
    throw new Error(
      `Invalid BOC magic: 0x${magic}`
    );
  }

  console.log("PASS | BOC magic");

  const cells =
    Cell.fromBoc(boc);

  console.log(
    `Root cells: ${cells.length}`
  );

  if (cells.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(
      "BOC contains no cells."
    );
  }

  const root =
    cells[0];

  console.log(
    `Root bits: ${root.bits.length}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Root refs: ${root.refs.length}`
  );

  const s =
    root.beginParse();

  console.log("");
  console.log("===== ROOT RAW STRUCTURE =====");

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // First 32 bits
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const first32 =
    s.loadUint(32);

  console.log(
    `first uint32: ${first32}`
  );

  console.log(
    `first uint32 hex: 0x${first32.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Next 64 bits
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const queryId =
    s.loadUintBig(64);

  console.log(
    `query_id: ${queryId}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Try TON amount
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const tonAmount =
    s.loadCoins();

  console.log(
    `TON amount: ${tonAmount}`
  );

  console.log(
    `TON amount: ${tonAmount} nanoTON`
  );

  console.log(
    `TON amount display: ${Number(tonAmount) / 1e9} TON`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Address
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const destination =
    s.loadAddress();

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "destination:"
  );

  console.log(
    destination
      ? destination.toString({
          bounceable: true,
          urlSafe: true,
        })
      : "null"
  );

  console.log(
    destination
      ? `RAW: ${destination.toRawString()}`
      : ""
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Remaining structure
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "===== AFTER INITIAL FIELDS ====="
  );

  console.log(
    `remaining bits: ${s.remainingBits}`
  );

  console.log(
    `remaining refs: ${s.remainingRefs}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Print root cell refs
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "===== ROOT REFERENCES ====="
  );

  console.log(
    `root refs: ${root.refs.length}`
  );

  for (
    let i = 0;
    i < root.refs.length;
    i++
  ) {
    const ref =
      root.refs[i];

    console.log(
      `REF ${i}: bits=${ref.bits.length}, refs=${ref.refs.length}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  console.log(
    " INSPECTION COMPLETE"
  );

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  console.log(
    "No transaction was sent."
  );

  console.log(
    "No wallet signature was requested."
  );
}

            }
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('SEARCH COMPLETE');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectPtonWalletPagedV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const PAGE_SIZE = 30;

function short(s: string, n = 70) {
    return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n) + '...' : s;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 pTON WALLET TRANSACTIONS - PAGED');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Wallet:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    const client = provider.api() as TonClient;

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(
        PTON_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 100,
        }
    );

    console.log(`Total fetched: ${transactions.length}`);
    console.log(`Page size: ${PAGE_SIZE}`);
    console.log('');

    for (let pageStart = 0; pageStart < transactions.length; pageStart += PAGE_SIZE) {
        const pageEnd = Math.min(
            pageStart + PAGE_SIZE,
            transactions.length
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('------------------------------------------------');
        console.log(
            ` PAGE ${Math.floor(pageStart / PAGE_SIZE) + 1} `
            + `(transactions ${pageStart + 1}-${pageEnd})`
        );
        console.log('------------------------------------------------');

        for (let i = pageStart; i < pageEnd; i++) {
            const tx = transactions[i];

            const hash = tx.hash().toString('base64');
            const lt = tx.lt.toString();
            const utime = tx.now
                ? new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
                : 'unknown';

            const inMsg = tx.inMessage;
            const inBody = inMsg?.body;

            let opcode = 'NONE';

            if (inBody && inBody.bits.length > 0) {
                try {
                    const s = inBody.beginParse();

                    if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
                        opcode =
                            '0x' +
                            s.loadUint(32)
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(8, '0');
                    }
                } catch {
                    opcode = 'DECODE_ERROR';
                }
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log(`#${i + 1}`);
            console.log(`LT:      ${lt}`);
            console.log(`TIME:    ${utime}`);
            console.log(`HASH:    ${hash}`);
            console.log(`OPCODE:  ${opcode}`);

            if (opcode === '0x01f3835d') {
                console.log('TYPE:    pton_ton_transfer');
            } else if (opcode === '0x0f8a7ea5') {
                console.log('TYPE:    jetton_transfer');
            } else if (opcode === '0x7362d09c') {
                console.log('TYPE:    jetton_notify');
            } else if (opcode === '0x37c096df') {
                console.log('TYPE:    stonfi_provide_lp_v2');
            } else if (opcode === '0x06ecd527') {
                console.log('TYPE:    stonfi_cb_add_liquidity_v2');
            }

            if (inMsg) {
                console.log(
                    `IN VALUE: ${inMsg.info.type === 'internal'
                        ? inMsg.info.value.coins.toString()
                        : 'external'}`
                );
            }

            console.log(`BODY:    ${short(inBody?.toBoc().toString('base64') ?? 'EMPTY', 100)}`);
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log(
            `END PAGE ${Math.floor(pageStart / PAGE_SIZE) + 1}`
        );
        console.log('================================================');

        if (pageEnd < transactions.length) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('برای صفحه بعدی، فقط عدد صفحه را اجرا کنید.');
            console.log('');
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log(' Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('================================================');
}

import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

function showSection(
    filename: string,
    pattern: string,
    before: number,
    after: number
) {
    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(`FILE: ${filename}`);
    console.log(`PATTERN: ${pattern}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    if (!fs.existsSync(filename)) {
        console.log('FILE NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    const text = fs.readFileSync(filename, 'utf8');
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

    const matches: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        if (
            lines[i]
                .toLowerCase()
                .includes(pattern.toLowerCase())
        ) {
            matches.push(i);
        }
    }

    console.log(`Matches: ${matches.length}`);

    for (const lineNo of matches) {
        const start = Math.max(0, lineNo - before);
        const end = Math.min(
            lines.length - 1,
            lineNo + after
        );

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `--- lines ${start + 1}-${end + 1} ---`
        );

        for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
            console.log(
                `${String(i + 1).padStart(4, ' ')} | ${lines[i]}`
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 PUSHCONT DECODER INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' LOCAL NODE_MODULES ONLY');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    const root =
        path.resolve(
            'node_modules',
            '@tact-lang',
            'opcode'
        );

    console.log(`Package root: ${root}`);

    const files = [
        path.join(
            root,
            'src',
            'decompiler',
            'operand-loader.ts'
        ),
        path.join(
            root,
            'dist',
            'decompiler',
            'operand-loader.js'
        ),
        path.join(
            root,
            'src',
            'decompiler',
            'disasm.ts'
        ),
        path.join(
            root,
            'dist',
            'decompiler',
            'disasm.js'
        ),
    ];

    console.log();
    console.log('Checking files...');

    for (const file of files) {
        console.log(
            `${fs.existsSync(file) ? '[FOUND]' : '[MISSING]'} ${file}`
        );
    }

    for (const file of files) {
        if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
            continue;
        }

        showSection(
            file,
            'PUSHCONT',
            8,
            30
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('========================================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectRawPoolMessagesV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET = 7219004800n;

const TARGET_HEX = TARGET
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(16, '0');

const TARGET_BYTES = TARGET_HEX.match(/.{2}/g)!.join(' ');

const TARGET_LTS = [
    '99809465000008',
    '99809465000010',
    '99809465000012',
];

const KNOWN_OPS: Record<number, string> = {
    0x37c096df: 'PROVIDE_LP',
    0x50c6a654: 'ADD_LIQUIDITY',
    0x178d4519: 'LP_TRANSFER/CALLBACK',
    0x01f3835d: 'TON_TRANSFER',
    0x0f8a7ea5: 'JETTON_TRANSFER',
};

function hex8(n: number): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function cellFromBody(body: any): Cell | null {
    if (!body) return null;

    try {
        if (
            body['@type'] === 'tvm.cell' &&
            typeof body.bytes === 'string'
        ) {
            return Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(body.bytes, 'base64')
            )[0];
        }

        if (typeof body === 'string') {
            return Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(body, 'base64')
            )[0];
        }
    } catch {
        return null;
    }

    return null;
}

function printBits(
    cell: Cell,
    label: string
) {
    const bits = cell.bits;

    console.log('');
    console.log(`--- ${label} ---`);
    console.log(
        `bits=${bits.length} refs=${cell.refs.length}`
    );

    /*
     * Print first 32 bits if available.
     */
    if (bits.length >= 32) {
        const s = cell.beginParse();
        const op = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            `opcode=0x${hex8(op)} ${
                KNOWN_OPS[op] ?? 'UNKNOWN'
            }`
        );
    }

    /*
     * Print the first 256 bits in compact chunks.
     */
    const maxBits = Math.min(
        bits.length,
        256
    );

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    let output = '';

    for (
        let offset = 0;
        offset + 8 <= maxBits;
        offset += 8
    ) {
        const b = s.loadUint(8);

        output += b
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, '0');

        if (
            offset % 32 === 24
        ) {
            output += ' ';
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `first ${maxBits} bits:`
    );

    console.log(output);
}

function scanTarget(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string
) {
    /*
     * Search exact TARGET at every bit offset
     * for widths that matter.
     */
    const widths = [
        32,
        64,
        96,
        128,
        160,
        256,
    ];

    for (const width of widths) {
        if (cell.bits.length < width) {
            continue;
        }

        for (
            let offset = 0;
            offset + width <= cell.bits.length;
            offset++
        ) {
            const s = cell.beginParse();

            if (offset > 0) {
                s.skip(offset);
            }

            let value: bigint;

            try {
                value =
                    s.loadUintBig(width);
            } catch {
                continue;
            }

            if (value === TARGET) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!'
                );
                console.log(
                    'TARGET FOUND'
                );
                console.log(
                    '!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!'
                );

                console.log(
                    `path   : ${path}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `offset : ${offset} bits`
                );

                console.log(
                    `width  : ${width}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `decimal: ${value}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `hex    : 0x${value
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(
                            Math.ceil(width / 4),
                            '0'
                        )}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    /*
     * Recurse.
     */
    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        scanTarget(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`
        );
    }
}

function printRefTree(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    depth: number
) {
    const prefix =
        '  '.repeat(depth);

    console.log(
        `${prefix}${path}: bits=${cell.bits.length}, refs=${cell.refs.length}`
    );

    if (cell.bits.length >= 32) {
        const op =
            cell.beginParse()
                .preloadUint(32);

        console.log(
            `${prefix}  opcode=0x${hex8(op)} ${
                KNOWN_OPS[op] ?? ''
            }`
        );
    }

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        printRefTree(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`,
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

function inspectMessage(
    message: any,
    label: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(label);

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `source : ${message.source ?? ''}`
    );

    console.log(
        `destination : ${message.destination ?? ''}`
    );

    console.log(
        `value : ${message.value ?? ''}`
    );

    const body =
        cellFromBody(message.body);

    if (!body) {
        console.log(
            'BODY: unable to decode'
        );
        return;
    }

    printBits(
        body,
        `${label} BODY`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'REF TREE'
    );

    printRefTree(
        body,
        'root',
        0
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TARGET SCAN'
    );

    scanTarget(
        body,
        'root'
    );
}

async function getTxByLt(
    lt: string
) {
    const url =
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions' +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(
            POOL.toString()
        )}` +
        '&limit=100';

    const response =
        await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    const json =
        await response.json();

    if (
        !json.ok ||
        !Array.isArray(json.result)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'Invalid Toncenter response'
        );
    }

    return json.result.find(
        (tx: any) =>
            tx.transaction_id?.lt === lt
    );
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 RAW POOL MESSAGE INSPECTOR'
    );

    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Pool: ${POOL.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target: ${TARGET}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target hex: 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target bytes: ${TARGET_BYTES}`
    );

    for (
        const lt of TARGET_LTS
    ) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '############################################################'
        );

        console.log(
            `POOL TRANSACTION LT ${lt}`
        );

        console.log(
            '############################################################'
        );

        const tx =
            await getTxByLt(lt);

        if (!tx) {
            console.log(
                `Transaction ${lt} not found`
            );

            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `hash: ${
                tx.transaction_id?.hash ?? ''
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `utime: ${tx.utime ?? ''}`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'RAW TRANSACTION KEYS:'
        );

        console.log(
            Object.keys(tx).join(', ')
        );

        /*
         * IN MESSAGE
         */
        if (tx.in_msg) {
            inspectMessage(
                tx.in_msg,
                `LT ${lt} - IN MESSAGE`
            );
        }

        /*
         * OUT MESSAGES
         */
        if (
            tx.out_msgs &&
            typeof tx.out_msgs === 'object'
        ) {
            const keys =
                Object.keys(
                    tx.out_msgs
                );

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `OUT MESSAGE CONTAINER KEYS: ${keys.join(', ')}`
            );

            for (
                const key of keys
            ) {
                const msg =
                    tx.out_msgs[key];

                if (!msg) continue;

                inspectMessage(
                    msg,
                    `LT ${lt} - OUT MESSAGE ${key}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';

    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

        lpAccountAddress

    );

    console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('===== GETTER =====');
    console.log('');

    if (deployed) {
        try {
            const lpAccount = DEX.v2_2.LpAccount.create(
                lpAccountAddress
            );

                provider.provider(lpAccountAddress)

            console.log('Pool address:', data.poolAddress.toString());

            console.log('Amount1:', data.amount1.toString());
        } catch (error) {
            console.log('Getter failed:');
            console.log(error);
        }
    } else {
        console.log('LP ACCOUNT IS NOT DEPLOYED.');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== END READ-ONLY CHECK =====');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 REAL POOL INSPECTION');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('EXPECTED ROUTER:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('EXPECTED ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('EXPECTED pTON WALLET:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 1. OPEN POOL PROVIDER
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('1. OPEN POOL PROVIDER');
    console.log('================================================');

    const poolProvider = provider.provider(POOL);

    console.log('Pool provider created: YES');
    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 2. get_pool_data
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('2. get_pool_data');
    console.log('================================================');

    try {
        const result = await poolProvider.get(
            'get_pool_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('get_pool_data: SUCCESS');
        console.log('');

        console.log('RAW STACK:');
        console.log(result.stack);
        console.log('');

        console.log('Attempting to decode PoolData...');
        console.log('');

        try {
            const isLocked = result.stack.readBoolean();

            console.log('is_locked:');
            console.log(isLocked);

            const routerAddress = result.stack.readAddress();

            console.log('router_address:');
            console.log(routerAddress?.toString());

            const totalSupply = result.stack.readBigNumber();

            console.log('total_supply:');
            console.log(totalSupply.toString());

            const reserve0 = result.stack.readBigNumber();

            console.log('reserve0:');
            console.log(reserve0.toString());

            const reserve1 = result.stack.readBigNumber();

            console.log('reserve1:');
            console.log(reserve1.toString());

            const token0 = result.stack.readAddress();

            console.log('token0_wallet_address:');
            console.log(token0?.toString());

            const token1 = result.stack.readAddress();

            console.log('token1_wallet_address:');
            console.log(token1?.toString());

            const lpFee = result.stack.readBigNumber();

            console.log('lp_fee:');
            console.log(lpFee.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('================================================');
            console.log('3. VALIDATION');
            console.log('================================================');

            console.log(
                'Router matches expected:',
                routerAddress?.equals(EXPECTED_ROUTER)
            );

            console.log(
                'Token0 matches ROOSTER:',
                token0?.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET)
            );

            console.log(
                'Token1 matches pTON:',
                token1?.equals(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET)
            );

            console.log('');

            console.log('Pool locked:', isLocked);
            console.log('LP total supply:', totalSupply.toString());
            console.log('Reserve0:', reserve0.toString());
            console.log('Reserve1:', reserve1.toString());
            console.log('LP fee:', lpFee.toString());

        } catch (decodeError) {
            console.log('POOL DATA DECODE ERROR:');
            console.log(decodeError);
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('get_pool_data ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 4. get_lp_account_address
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('4. get_lp_account_address');
    console.log('================================================');

    try {
        const result = await poolProvider.get(
            'get_lp_account_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(USER)
                        .endCell()
                }
            ]
        );

        console.log('get_lp_account_address: SUCCESS');

        try {
            console.log(
                'LP account:',
                result.stack.readAddress()?.toString()
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Unable to decode LP account:');
            console.log(e);
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('get_lp_account_address ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 5. FINAL
    // ------------------------------------------------

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION FINISHED');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
}

import { Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { LiteClient } from "ton-lite-client";
import { LiteSingleEngine } from "ton-lite-client/dist/engines/single";

const LIBRARY_HASHES = [
    "050ed2107f730a4493cfcddbc67bdb83854f1905b3486236530d5a50721d1817",
    "631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c6342e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041",
    "7c1f3aa01265453058887378a358cf37314856350400804c169b648c3fdfbe4c",
    "1b63154f10acd98345a65890997094e9856aa2be4f1e37060865519fa42032fd",
];

const SERVER = {
    host: "tcp://168.119.95.207:27787",
    key: "Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=",
};

async function waitUntilReady(
    engine: LiteSingleEngine,
    timeoutMs = 15000
) {
    const start = Date.now();

    while (Date.now() - start < timeoutMs) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            return true;
        }

        await new Promise((resolve) =>
            setTimeout(resolve, 250)
        );
    }

    return false;
}

function inspectCell(cell: Cell) {
    console.log("  bits:", cell.bits.length);
    console.log("  refs:", cell.refs.length);
    console.log("  exotic:", cell.isExotic);

    console.log(
        "  hash:",
        cell.hash().toString("hex")
    );

    console.log(
        "  bits(hex):",
        cell.bits.toString()
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = cell.refs[i];

        console.log(`  ref[${i}]:`);
        console.log("    bits:", ref.bits.length);
        console.log("    refs:", ref.refs.length);
        console.log("    exotic:", ref.isExotic);
        console.log(
            "    hash:",
            ref.hash().toString("hex")
        );
        console.log(
            "    bits(hex):",
            ref.bits.toString()
        );
    }
}

async function inspectLibrary(
    client: LiteClient,
    index: number,
    hash: string
) {
    console.log(`\n===== LIBRARY [${index}] =====`);
    console.log("Requested hash:");
    console.log(hash);

    const requestedHash = Buffer.from(hash, "hex");

    try {
        const result = await client.getLibraries([
            requestedHash,
        ]);

        console.log(
            "Result kind:",
            result.kind
        );

        console.log(
            "Result count:",
            result.result.length
        );

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            console.log("STATUS: NOT FOUND");
            return;
        }

        for (const entry of result.result) {
            const returnedHash =
                entry.hash.toString("hex");

            console.log("STATUS: FOUND");
            console.log(
                "Returned hash:",
                returnedHash
            );

            console.log(
                "Hash matches:",
                returnedHash === hash
            );

            console.log(
                "Data bytes:",
                entry.data.length
            );

            try {
                const cells = Cell.fromBoc(entry.data);

                console.log(
                    "Decoded root cells:",
                    cells.length
                );

                if (cells.length === 0) {
                    console.log(
                        "WARNING: No root cell."
                    );
                    continue;
                }

                const root = cells[0];

                console.log(
                    "\nROOT CELL:"
                );

                inspectCell(root);

                const calculatedHash =
                    root.hash().toString("hex");

                console.log(
                    "\nCalculated root hash:"
                );

                console.log(
                    calculatedHash
                );

                console.log(
                    "Requested hash matches root:",
                    calculatedHash === hash
                );
            } catch (e: any) {
                console.log(
                    "BOC decode ERROR:",
                    e?.message ?? e
                );
            }
        }
    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log(
            "getLibraries ERROR:",
            e?.message ?? e
        );
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log(" RESOLVED ROUTER LIBRARY CODE INSPECTION");
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");
    console.log("Server:", SERVER.host);

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: SERVER.host,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            SERVER.key,
            "base64"
        ),
        client: "tcp",
        reconnectTimeout: 3000,
    });

    try {
        console.log(
            "Waiting for LiteServer connection..."
        );

        const ready = await waitUntilReady(
            engine
        );

        console.log(
            "Engine ready:",
            ready
        );

        console.log(
            "Engine closed:",
            engine.isClosed()
        );

        if (!ready) {
            console.log(
                "CONNECTION NOT READY"
            );
            return;
        }

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < LIBRARY_HASHES.length;
            i++
        ) {
            await inspectLibrary(
                client,
                i,
                LIBRARY_HASHES[i]
            );
        }

        console.log("\n========================================");
        console.log("INSPECTION FINISHED");
        console.log("NO TRANSACTION");
        console.log("========================================");
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectResolvedLibraryV22.ts

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TESTNET_CONFIG_URL =
    'https://ton-blockchain.github.io/testnet-global.config.json';

function intToIP(ip: number): string {
    const n = ip >>> 0;

    return [
        (n >>> 24) & 255,
        (n >>> 16) & 255,
        (n >>> 8) & 255,
        n & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

async function getTestnetConfig(): Promise<any> {
    const response = await fetch(TESTNET_CONFIG_URL);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Cannot download Testnet config: HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    return await response.json();
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function waitForEngine(
    engine: LiteSingleEngine,
    timeoutMs: number
): Promise<void> {
    const started = Date.now();

    while (Date.now() - started < timeoutMs) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            return;
        }

        if (engine.isClosed()) {
            await sleep(250);
            continue;
        }

        await sleep(250);
    }

    throw new Error(
        `LiteSingleEngine did not become ready within ${timeoutMs} ms`
    );
}

function dumpCell(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    depth: number,
    maxDepth: number
): void {
    const indent = '  '.repeat(depth);

    const hash = cell.hash().toString('hex');

    console.log(`${indent}${path}`);
    console.log(`${indent}  hash    : ${hash}`);
    console.log(`${indent}  bits    : ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`${indent}  refs    : ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`${indent}  exotic  : ${cell.isExotic}`);

    if (depth >= maxDepth) {
        if (cell.refs.length > 0) {
            console.log(
                `${indent}  ... max depth reached`
            );
        }

        return;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        dumpCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`,
            depth + 1,
            maxDepth
        );
    }
}

function bitsToHex(cell: Cell): string {
    const byteLength = Math.floor(cell.bits.length / 8);

    if (byteLength === 0) {
        return '';
    }

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    try {
        return slice.loadBuffer(byteLength).toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return '(unable to read bits as buffer)';
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ON-CHAIN LIBRARY INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' GET_LIBRARIES + CELL TREE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log('');
    console.log(`Library hash: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);

    const config = await getTestnetConfig();

    if (!config.liteservers || config.liteservers.length === 0) {
        throw new Error('No Testnet LiteServers found');
    }

    console.log(
        `Testnet LiteServers available: ${config.liteservers.length}`
    );

    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
        'hex'
    );

    let lastError: unknown = null;

    for (let i = 0; i < config.liteservers.length; i++) {
        const server = config.liteservers[i];

        const ip = intToIP(server.ip);
        const port = server.port;
        const publicKey = Buffer.from(
            server.id.key,
            'base64'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `Trying LiteServer ${i + 1}/${config.liteservers.length}: ${ip}:${port}`
        );

        let engine: LiteSingleEngine | null = null;

        try {
            engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${ip}:${port}`,
                publicKey,
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            });

            console.log(
                `Initial state: closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
            );

            await waitForEngine(engine, 15000);

            console.log(
                `Engine ready: closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
            );

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine,
            });

            const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo({
                timeout: 10000,
            });

            console.log(
                `Masterchain seqno: ${master.last.seqno}`
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');

            const result = await client.getLibraries(
                [libraryHash],
                {
                    timeout: 15000,
                }
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('getLibraries() returned');
            console.log(`Entries: ${result.result.length}`);

            if (result.result.length === 0) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT: LIBRARY NOT RETURNED');

                engine.close();
                return;
            }

            for (const entry of result.result) {
                const hashHex =
                    Buffer.from(entry.hash).toString('hex');

                const data =
                    Buffer.from(entry.data);

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '------------------------------------------------------------'
                );

                console.log(`Entry hash: ${hashHex}`);
                console.log(
                    `Requested : ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Library data bytes: ${data.length}`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'Library data hex (first 64 bytes):'
                );

                console.log(
                    data.subarray(0, 64).toString('hex')
                );

                let cells: Cell[];

                try {
                    /*
                     * IMPORTANT:
                     * Parse the exact bytes returned by
                     * getLibraries().
                     *
                     * No manually copied BOC is used.
                     */
                    cells = Cell.fromBoc(data);
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        'Cell.fromBoc() FAILED on the live Library data.'
                    );
                    console.log(String(e));

                    engine.close();
                    return;
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `BOC root cells: ${cells.length}`
                );

                if (cells.length === 0) {
                    console.log(
                        'No root cells returned.'
                    );

                    engine.close();
                    return;
                }

                const root = cells[0];

                const actualHash =
                    root.hash().toString('hex');

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );
                console.log(' ROOT CELL');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );

                console.log(
                    `Root bits   : ${root.bits.length}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Root refs   : ${root.refs.length}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Root exotic : ${root.isExotic}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Root hash   : ${actualHash}`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `Expected hash: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()}`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `HASH MATCH: ${
                        actualHash.toLowerCase() ===
                        LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()
                    }`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'Root bits hex:'
                );

                console.log(
                    bitsToHex(root)
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );
                console.log(' CELL TREE');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );

                /*
                 * Depth 12 is enough to see the Library structure
                 * without producing an uncontrolled amount of output.
                 */
                dumpCell(
                    root,
                    'root',
                    0,
                    12
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );
                console.log(' FIRST-LEVEL REFERENCES');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );

                for (
                    let refIndex = 0;
                    refIndex < root.refs.length;
                    refIndex++
                ) {
                    const ref =
                        root.refs[refIndex];

                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        `Reference ${refIndex}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  hash   : ${ref.hash().toString('hex')}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  bits   : ${ref.bits.length}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  refs   : ${ref.refs.length}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  exotic : ${ref.isExotic}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  bits hex: ${bitsToHex(ref)}`
                    );
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );
                console.log(' RESULT');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );

                if (
                    actualHash.toLowerCase() ===
                    LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        'ON-CHAIN LIBRARY HASH MATCH: TRUE'
                    );
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        'ON-CHAIN LIBRARY HASH MATCH: FALSE'
                    );
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'RESULT: ON-CHAIN LIBRARY INSPECTED'
                );

                console.log(
                    'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
                );

                engine.close();
                return;
            }

            engine.close();
            return;
        } catch (e) {
            lastError = e;

            console.log(
                `LiteServer failed: ${String(e)}`
            );

            if (engine) {
                try {
                    engine.close();
                } catch {
                    // ignore
                }
            }
        }
    }

    throw new Error(
        `All Testnet LiteServers failed. Last error: ${String(lastError)}`
    );
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
    LiteEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const LIBRARY_HASHES = [
    '050ed2107f730a4493cfcddbc67bdb83854f1905b3486236530d5a50721d1817',
    '631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c6342e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041',
    '7c1f3aa01265453058887378a358cf37314856350400804c169b648c3fdfbe4c',
    '1b63154f10acd98345a65890997094e9856aa2be4f1e37060865519fa42032fd',
];

const servers = [
    {
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
    },
    {
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
    },
];

export async function run() {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('  RESOLVED ROUTER LIBRARY INSPECTION');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const key = Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64');

        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: server.host,
                publicKey: key,
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        for (let i = 0; i < LIBRARY_HASHES.length; i++) {

            const hashHex = LIBRARY_HASHES[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`===== LIBRARY [${i}] =====`);
            console.log('Requested hash:', hashHex);

            const hash =
                Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex');

            const result =
                await client.getLibraries([hash]);

            if (
                !result ||
                !Array.isArray(result.result) ||
                result.result.length === 0
            ) {

                console.log('NOT FOUND');
                continue;
            }

            const entry = result.result[0];

            console.log('FOUND');
            console.log(
                'Returned hash:',
                entry.hash.toString('hex')
            );

            console.log(
                'Data bytes:',
                entry.data.length
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== LIBRARY BOC =====');

            console.log(
                entry.data.toString('base64')
            );

            try {

                const cells =
                    Cell.fromBoc(entry.data);

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'Decoded root cells:',
                    cells.length
                );

                for (let j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {

                    const cell = cells[j];

                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        `----- ROOT CELL [${j}] -----`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'bits:',
                        cell.bits.length
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'refs:',
                        cell.refs.length
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'exotic:',
                        cell.isExotic
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'type:',
                        cell.type
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'cell hash:',
                        cell.hash().toString('hex')
                    );

                    console.log(
                        'bits:',
                        cell.bits.toString()
                    );
                }

            } catch (error) {

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'BOC decode failed:'
                );

                console.error(error);
            }
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== ERROR =====');
        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('             TEST FINISHED');
    console.log('             NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core'; import * as fs from 'fs'; import * as path from 'path'; import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint'; export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) { const filePath = path.resolve( process.cwd(), 'rooster_pool_body.boc' ); const boc = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8').trim(); const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc); console.log('===== ROOSTER POOL BODY INSPECTOR ====='); console.log('BOC length:', boc.length); console.log('Root bits:', cell.bits.length); console.log('Root refs:', cell.refs.length); const s = cell.beginParse(); console.log('\n===== JETTON TRANSFER ====='); const opcode = s.loadUint(32); console.log( 'Opcode:', '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0') ); const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64); console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString()); const amount = s.loadCoins(); console.log('Jetton amount:', amount.toString()); console.log( 'Jetton amount ROOSTER:', Number(amount) / 1e9 ); const destination = s.loadAddress(); console.log('Destination:', destination.toString()); const responseDestination = s.loadAddress(); console.log( 'Response destination:', responseDestination.toString() ); console.log('\nRemaining bits:', s.remainingBits); console.log('Remaining refs:', s.remainingRefs); if (s.remainingRefs > 0) { const customPayload = s.loadMaybeRef(); if (customPayload) { console.log('\n===== FORWARD PAYLOAD ====='); console.log( 'Payload bits:', customPayload.bits.length ); console.log( 'Payload refs:', customPayload.refs.length ); const p = customPayload.beginParse(); const payloadOpcode = p.loadUint(32); console.log( 'Payload opcode:', '0x' + payloadOpcode .toString(16) .padStart(8, '0') ); console.log( 'Payload remaining bits:', p.remainingBits ); console.log( 'Payload remaining refs:', p.remainingRefs ); console.log('\nPayload cell tree:'); console.log(customPayload.toString()); } } console.log('\n===== COMPLETE CELL TREE ====='); console.log(cell.toString()); console.log('\n===== END ====='); }

FILE: scripts\inspectRouter.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    console.log('===== ROUTER CODE HASH =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());

    try {
        const state = await provider.provider(routerAddress).getState();

        if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
            console.log('Router is not active');
            return;
        }

        if (!state.state.code || !state.state.data) {
            console.log('Router code/data is missing');
            return;
        }

        const code = Cell.fromBoc(state.state.code)[0];
        const data = Cell.fromBoc(state.state.data)[0];

        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
        console.log('Code hash:', code.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase());
        console.log('Data hash:', data.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase());
        console.log('Code bits:', code.bits.length);
        console.log('Code refs:', code.refs.length);
        console.log('Data bits:', data.bits.length);
        console.log('Data refs:', data.refs.length);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('===== INSPECTION FAILED =====');
        console.log(error);
    }
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
    LiteEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { Address } from '@ton/core';

export async function run() {

    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const servers = [
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    console.log('===== ROUTER ACCOUNT INSPECTION =====');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(router.toString());
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const key = Buffer.from(
            server.key,
            'base64'
        );

        console.log(
            'Adding:',
            server.host
        );

        console.log(
            'Key bytes:',
            key.length
        );

        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: server.host,
                publicKey: key,
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '===== MASTERCHAIN ====='
        );

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            master.last.rootHash.toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            '===== GET ROUTER ACCOUNT STATE ====='
        );

        const result =
            await client.getAccountState(
                router,
                master.last
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '===== ACCOUNT RESULT ====='
        );

        console.log(
            'State:',
            result.state === null
                ? 'UNINIT'
                : 'ACTIVE'
        );

        console.log(
            'Balance:',
            result.balance.coins.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Balance TON:',
            Number(result.balance.coins) / 1e9
        );

        console.log(
            'Raw bytes:',
            result.raw.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Proof bytes:',
            result.proof.length
        );

        console.log('');

        if (result.lastTx) {

            console.log(
                'Last TX LT:',
                result.lastTx.lt.toString()
            );

            console.log(
                'Last TX hash:',
                result.lastTx.hash.toString()
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                'Last TX: none'
            );
        }

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            '===== RAW ACCOUNT RESULT ====='
        );

        console.dir(
            result,
            {
                depth: 8
            }
        );

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '===== ERROR ====='
        );

        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Closing engines...'
        );

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '===== TEST FINISHED ====='
    );
}

FILE: scripts\inspectRouterCells.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER_ADDRESS =
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x';

function dumpCell(label: string, cell: Cell): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(`type        : ${cell.type}`);
    console.log(`bits        : ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`refs        : ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`hash        : ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC BASE64:');

    try {
        console.log(
            Cell.fromBoc(
                cell.toBoc({
                    idx: false,
                }),
            )[0]
                ? cell
                    .toBoc({
                        idx: false,
                    })
                    .toString('base64')
                : 'unavailable',
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('unavailable');
    }

    console.log('');

    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        console.log(`remaining bits : ${s.remainingBits}`);
        console.log(`remaining refs : ${s.remainingRefs}`);

        if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
            const bitCount = Math.min(
                s.remainingBits,
                264,
            );

            const bytes: number[] = [];

            const fullBytes = Math.floor(bitCount / 8);

            for (let i = 0; i < fullBytes; i++) {
                bytes.push(s.loadUint(8));
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('BYTES HEX:');

            console.log(
                bytes
                    .map((b) =>
                        b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'),
                    )
                    .join(' '),
            );

            console.log('');

            console.log('BYTES DECIMAL:');

            console.log(bytes.join(', '));
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Bit inspection failed:',
            e instanceof Error
                ? e.message
                : String(e),
        );
    }

    if (cell.refs.length > 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('REFERENCES:');

        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            const ref = cell.refs[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`REF [${i}]`);
            console.log(`type : ${ref.type}`);
            console.log(`bits : ${ref.bits.length}`);
            console.log(`refs : ${ref.refs.length}`);
            console.log(
                `hash : ${ref.hash().toString('hex')}`,
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI ROUTER CELL INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(`ROUTER: ${ROUTER_ADDRESS}`);
    console.log(`ENDPOINT: ${ENDPOINT}`);
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const router = Address.parse(ROUTER_ADDRESS);

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        `MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${latest.last.seqno}`,
    );

    console.log('');

    const result = await client.runMethod(
        latest.last.seqno,
        router,
        'get_router_data',
    );

    console.log(`EXIT CODE: ${result.exitCode}`);
    console.log('');

    if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
        console.log('GETTER FAILED');
        console.log(result.resultRaw);
        return;
    }

    const reader = result.reader;

    // [0]
    const routerId = reader.readBigNumber();

    // [1]
    const dexType = reader.readString();

    // [2]
    const locked = reader.readBigNumber();

    // [3]
    const admin = reader.readAddress();

    console.log('ROUTER DATA');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log(`router id : ${routerId}`);
    console.log(`dex type  : ${dexType}`);
    console.log(`locked    : ${locked}`);
    console.log(`admin     : ${admin.toString()}`);

    // [4]
    const field4 = reader.readCell();

    // [5]
    const field5 = reader.readCell();

    // [6]
    const field6 = reader.readCell();

    // [7]
    const field7 = reader.readCell();

    // [8]
    const field8 = reader.readCell();

    dumpCell(
        'FIELD [4]',
        field4,
    );

    dumpCell(
        'FIELD [5]',
        field5,
    );

    dumpCell(
        'FIELD [6]',
        field6,
    );

    dumpCell(
        'FIELD [7]',
        field7,
    );

    dumpCell(
        'FIELD [8]',
        field8,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}
