FILE: scripts\inspectRouterCode.ts
====================================================================================================
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import {
    Address,
    Cell,
} from '@ton/core';

async function main() {

    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER CODE STRUCTURE INSPECTION');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const engine =
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host:
                    `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey:
                    Buffer.from(
                        server.key,
                        'base64'
                    ),
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

        engines.push(engine);
    }

    const rr =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(
            engines
        );

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine: rr,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        // ====================================
        // MASTERCHAIN
        // ====================================

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('MASTERCHAIN');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        // ====================================
        // ACCOUNT
        // ====================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER ACCOUNT');

        const account =
            await client.getAccountState(
                router,
                master.last
            );

        if (
            account.state === null
        ) {

            console.log(
                'ERROR: account state is null'
            );

            return;
        }

        console.log(
            'State:',
            account.state.storage.state.type
        );

        if (
            account.state.storage.state.type !==
            'active'
        ) {

            console.log(
                'ERROR: Router is not active'
            );

            return;
        }

        const active =
            account.state.storage.state.state;

        const code:
            Cell | null | undefined =
            active.code;

        if (!code) {

            console.log(
                'ERROR: code is missing'
            );

            return;
        }

        // ====================================
        // ROOT
        // ====================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROOT CODE CELL');

        printCell(
            code,
            'ROOT'
        );

        // ====================================
        // RECURSIVE TREE
        // ====================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('FULL CODE TREE');

        inspectCell(
            code,
            'ROOT',
            0,
            new Set<string>()
        );

        // ====================================
        // CODE BOC
        // ====================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('CODE BOC');

        console.log(
            Buffer.from(
                code.toBoc({
                    idx: false,
                })
            ).toString('base64')
        );

        // ====================================
        // FINAL
        // ====================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' INSPECTION FINISHED');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');

    } finally {

        for (const engine of engines) {
            engine.close();
        }
    }
}

function printCell(
    cell: Cell,
    name: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(name);

    console.log(
        'Type:',
        cell.type
    );

    console.log(
        'Bits:',
        cell.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Refs:',
        cell.refs.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Hash:',
        cell.hash().toString('hex')
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC:',
        Buffer.from(
            cell.toBoc({
                idx: false,
            })
        ).toString('base64')
    );

    console.log(
        'Bits binary:',
        cell.bits.toString()
    );
}

function inspectCell(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    depth: number,
    visited: Set<string>
) {

    if (depth > 8) {
        console.log(
            `${path}: MAX DEPTH`
        );
        return;
    }

    const hash =
        cell.hash().toString('hex');

    if (visited.has(hash)) {

        console.log(
            `${path}: ALREADY VISITED`
        );

        return;
    }

    visited.add(hash);

    printCell(
        cell,
        path
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {

        inspectCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref${i}`,
            depth + 1,
            visited
        );
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch(
    (error) => {

        console.error('');
        console.error('========================================');
        console.error('ERROR');
        console.error('========================================');

        console.error(error);

        process.exit(1);
    }
);

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER CODE CELLS');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('\nRouter:', ROUTER.toString());

    const result = await client.runMethod(
        ROUTER,
        'get_router_data'
    );

    console.log('\nGas used:', result.gas_used);

    const stack = result.stack;

    const routerId = stack.readNumber();
    const dexType = stack.readString();
    const isLocked = stack.readBoolean();
    const adminAddress = stack.readAddress();

    const tempUpgrade = stack.readCell();
    const poolCode = stack.readCell();
    const jettonLpWalletCode = stack.readCell();
    const lpAccountCode = stack.readCell();
    const vaultCode = stack.readCell();

    console.log('\nRouter data');
    console.log('-----------');
    console.log('routerId:', routerId);
    console.log('dexType:', dexType);
    console.log('isLocked:', isLocked);
    console.log('admin:', adminAddress?.toString());

    function inspectCell(name: string, cell: any) {
        console.log(`\n${name}`);
        console.log('-'.repeat(name.length));

        if (!cell) {
            console.log('NULL');
            return;
        }

        console.log('bits:', cell.bits.length);
        console.log('refs:', cell.refs.length);

        try {
            console.log(
                'hash:',
                cell.hash().toString('hex')
            );
        } catch {
            console.log('hash: <unavailable>');
        }

        try {
            console.log(
                'boc:',
                cell.toBoc({ idx: false }).toString('base64')
            );
        } catch {
            console.log('boc: <unavailable>');
        }
    }

    inspectCell('tempUpgrade', tempUpgrade);
    inspectCell('poolCode', poolCode);
    inspectCell('jettonLpWalletCode', jettonLpWalletCode);
    inspectCell('lpAccountCode', lpAccountCode);
    inspectCell('vaultCode', vaultCode);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    console.log('===== ROUTER CODE/DATA TEST =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());

    try {
        const state = await provider.provider(router).getState();

        console.log('\nSTATE:');
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance);
        console.log('Type:', state.state.type);

        if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
            console.log('Router is not active.');
            return;
        }

        const code = state.state.code;
        const data = state.state.data;

        if (!code || !data) {
            console.log('ERROR: Active account has missing code or data.');
            console.log('Code:', code);
            console.log('Data:', data);
            return;
        }

        console.log('Code bytes:', code.length);
        console.log('Data bytes:', data.length);

        console.log('\nCODE HEX:');
        console.log(code.toString('hex'));

        console.log('\nDATA HEX:');
        console.log(data.toString('hex'));
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nERROR:');
        console.log(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectRouterData.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== ROUTER DATA INSPECTION =====');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
            'base64'
        ),
        reconnectTimeout: 3000
    });

    console.log('Connecting...');

    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            break;
        }

        await sleep(500);

        console.log(
            `Waiting... ${i + 1}/20 | closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
        );
    }

    if (!engine.isReady()) {
        console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
        engine.close();
        return;
    }

    console.log('ENGINE READY');

    try {
        const client = new LiteClient({ engine });

        const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            info.last.seqno
        );

        const result = await client.runMethod(
            ROUTER,
            'get_router_data',
            Buffer.alloc(0),
            info.last
        );

        console.log('Exit code:', result.exitCode);

        if (result.result) {
            const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(result.result, 'base64')
            )[0];

            console.log(
                'Result BOC:',
                cell.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log(
                'Result bits:',
                cell.bits.toString()
            );

            console.log(
                'Result refs:',
                cell.refs.length
            );

if (cell.refs.length > 0) {
    console.log(
        'Ref[0] BOC:',
        cell.refs[0].toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    console.log(
        'Ref[0] bits:',
        cell.refs[0].bits.toString()
    );
}
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR:');
        console.log(
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Functions } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';

const router = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const engines = [
    new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
            'base64'
        ),
    }),
    new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
            'base64'
        ),
    }),
];

async function main() {
    console.log('ROUTER RAW STATE TEST');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('');

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine: new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines),
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    const block = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    const routerHash = Buffer.from(router.hash);

    console.log('Masterchain:', block.last.seqno);
    console.log('Router workchain:', router.workChain);
    console.log('Router hash:', routerHash.toString('hex'));
    console.log('Router hash bytes:', routerHash.length);

    const request = {
        kind: 'liteServer.getAccountState' as const,

        id: {
            kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt' as const,
            seqno: block.last.seqno,
            shard: block.last.shard,
            workchain: block.last.workchain,
            fileHash: block.last.fileHash,
            rootHash: block.last.rootHash,
        },

        account: {
            kind: 'liteServer.accountId' as const,
            workchain: router.workChain,
            id: routerHash,
        },
    };

    console.log('');
    console.log('Sending liteServer.getAccountState...');
    console.log('');

    const result = await client.engine.query(
        Functions.liteServer_getAccountState,
        request
    );

    console.log('RESULT KIND:', result.kind);
    console.log('RESULT:');
    console.dir(result, { depth: 3 });
}

main().catch(console.error);

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {
    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        '5A34C333BCDCAC70D41F9AFCA79AA6E782F1C80A5E4B2EF896EC5E20E9182C86',
        'hex'
    );

    const servers = [
        {
            host: 'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842',
            key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=',
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    console.log('===== ROUTER LIBRARY INSPECTION =====');
    console.log('Library hash:', libraryHash.toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    const engines = servers.map(
        s =>
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: s.host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(s.key, 'base64'),
            })
    );

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Masterchain seqno:', master.last.seqno);
        console.log('');

        console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');
        const result = await client.getLibraries([libraryHash]);

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== RAW RESULT =====');
        console.dir(result, { depth: null });

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== SUMMARY =====');

        if (!result || !result.result) {
            console.log('No result field.');
            return;
        }

        console.log('Result count:', result.result.length);

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            console.log('LIBRARY NOT FOUND');
        } else {
            console.log('LIBRARY FOUND');

            for (let i = 0; i < result.result.length; i++) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('ENTRY', i);
                console.dir(result.result[i], { depth: null });
            }
        }
    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

main().catch(e => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(e);
    process.exit(1);
});

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

   const router = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

    console.log('===== ROUTER ON-CHAIN CHECK =====');

    console.log(
        'Address:',
        router.toString({
            urlSafe: true,
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        })
    );

    try {
        const state = await client.getContractState(router);

        console.log('\n===== CONTRACT STATE =====');
        console.log('state:', state.state);
        console.log('balance:', state.balance.toString());
        console.log('code:', state.code ? 'PRESENT' : 'EMPTY');
        console.log('data:', state.data ? 'PRESENT' : 'EMPTY');

        if (state.code) {
            console.log('code bytes:', state.code.length);
        }

        if (state.data) {
            console.log('data bytes:', state.data.length);
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'STATE ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }

    try {
        console.log('\n===== GET ROUTER DATA =====');

        const result = await client.runMethod(
            router,
            'get_router_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log(result.stack);

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'GET_ROUTER_DATA ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\inspectRouterPoolAddress.ts

import {
    Address,
    beginCell,
    Cell,
    serializeTuple
} from '@ton/core';

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQAmO8BvKx4ta9jkCCDx197VqrUrsaC42Dv6yhDAyKzFpi2S'
);

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBy4zGgSS4opE7HGNu767ikjUALNJ23busLGv-CuE8fPQ66'
);

const server = {
    host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
    key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk='
};

async function test(
    client: LiteClient,
    name: string,
    token0: Address,
    token1: Address,
    block: any
) {
    const args = serializeTuple([
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(token0)
                .endCell()
        },
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(token1)
                .endCell()
        }
    ]).toBoc({
        idx: false,
        crc32: false
    });

    const result = await client.runMethod(
        ROUTER,
        'get_pool_address',
        args,
        block
    );

    console.log(`${name}: exit=${result.exitCode}`);

    if (result.result) {
        const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(result.result, 'base64')
        )[0];

        console.log(
            `${name}: result=${cell.toBoc().toString('base64')}`
        );
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== POOL ADDRESS ORDER TEST =====');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: server.host,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
        reconnectTimeout: 3000
    });

    console.log('Connecting...');

    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            break;
        }

        await sleep(500);

        console.log(
            `Waiting... ${i + 1}/20 | closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
        );
    }

    if (!engine.isReady()) {
        console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
        engine.close();
        return;
    }

    console.log('ENGINE READY');

    try {
        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine
        });

        const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            info.last.seqno
        );

        await test(
            client,
            'ROOSTER -> pTON',
            ROOSTER_WALLET,
            PTON_WALLET,
            info.last
        );

        await test(
            client,
            'pTON -> ROOSTER',
            PTON_WALLET,
            ROOSTER_WALLET,
            info.last
        );

        console.log('===== DONE =====');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ERROR:');
        console.log(
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import {
    Address,
    beginCell,
    Cell,
    parseTuple,
    serializeTuple,
} from '@ton/core';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

async function main() {

    console.log('===== ROUTER POOL INPUT INSPECTION =====');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON master:');
    console.log(PTON.toString());

    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(
                    server.key,
                    'base64'
                ),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Adding:',
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(
        engines
    );

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== MASTERCHAIN =====');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        /*
         * get_wallet_address(owner)
         *
         * LiteServer expects the method parameters
         * as a serialized TVM Tuple.
         */
        const args = [
            {
                type: 'slice' as const,
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(ROUTER)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ];

        const paramsCell =
            serializeTuple(args);

        const paramsBoc =
            paramsCell.toBoc({
                idx: false,
                crc32: false,
            });

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== GET WALLET ADDRESS =====');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Serialized VmStack BOC:');

        console.log(
            Buffer.from(paramsBoc).toString('base64')
        );

        /*
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         * ROOSTER
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         */

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== ROOSTER =====');

        const roosterResult =
            await client.runMethod(
                ROOSTER,
                'get_wallet_address',
                Buffer.from(paramsBoc),
                master.last
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Exit code:');

        console.log(
            roosterResult.exitCode
        );

        if (roosterResult.result === null) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('ROOSTER result: null');

        } else {

            console.log('');
            console.log('ROOSTER result base64:');

            console.log(
                roosterResult.result
            );

            const roosterCell =
                Cell.fromBoc(
                    Buffer.from(
                        roosterResult.result,
                        'base64'
                    )
                )[0];

            const roosterTuple =
                parseTuple(roosterCell);

            console.log('');
            console.log('ROOSTER tuple:');

            console.log(
                roosterTuple
            );

            if (
                roosterTuple.length > 0 &&
                roosterTuple[0].type === 'slice'
            ) {

                const roosterWallet =
                    Address.parse(
                        roosterTuple[0].cell
                            .beginParse()
                            .loadAddress()
                            .toString()
                    );

                console.log('');
                console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet:');

                console.log(
                    roosterWallet.toString()
                );
            }
        }

        /*
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         * pTON
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         */

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== pTON =====');

        const ptonResult =
            await client.runMethod(
                PTON,
                'get_wallet_address',
                Buffer.from(paramsBoc),
                master.last
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Exit code:');

        console.log(
            ptonResult.exitCode
        );

        if (ptonResult.result === null) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('pTON result: null');

        } else {

            console.log('');
            console.log('pTON result base64:');

            console.log(
                ptonResult.result
            );

            const ptonCell =
                Cell.fromBoc(
                    Buffer.from(
                        ptonResult.result,
                        'base64'
                    )
                )[0];

            const ptonTuple =
                parseTuple(ptonCell);

            console.log('');
            console.log('pTON tuple:');

            console.log(
                ptonTuple
            );

            if (
                ptonTuple.length > 0 &&
                ptonTuple[0].type === 'slice'
            ) {

                const ptonWallet =
                    Address.parse(
                        ptonTuple[0].cell
                            .beginParse()
                            .loadAddress()
                            .toString()
                    );

                console.log('');
                console.log('pTON Router Wallet:');

                console.log(
                    ptonWallet.toString()
                );
            }
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== DONE =====');

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'No transaction was sent.'
        );

    } finally {

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {

    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
});

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { Address } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {

    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const ROUTER =
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER ACCOUNT STATE TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const host =
            `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

        const engine =
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(
                    server.key,
                    'base64'
                ),
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

        engines.push(engine);
    }

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        console.log('Getting masterchain info...');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Masterchain root hash:',
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString('hex')
        );

        const router =
            Address.parse(ROUTER);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Router:');
        console.log(router.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('Running getAccountState...');

        const state =
            await client.getAccountState(
                router,
                master.last
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' ACCOUNT STATE');
        console.log('========================================');

        console.dir(
            state,
            { depth: 8 }
        );

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('ERROR');
        console.error(error);

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }

        console.log('Done.');
    }

    process.exit(0);
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== ROUTER STATIC INTERNALS =====');

    const routers: any = {
        CPI: DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI,
        Stable: DEX.v2_1.Router.Stable,
        WCPI: DEX.v2_1.Router.WCPI,
        WStable: DEX.v2_1.Router.WStable,
    };

    for (const [name, R] of Object.entries(routers)) {
        console.log(`\n===== ${name} =====`);

        const router: any = R;

        console.log('dexType:', router.dexType);

        console.log(
            'prototype properties:',
            Object.getOwnPropertyNames(router.prototype)
        );

        console.log(
            'constructor source:',
            Function.prototype.toString.call(router)
        );

        console.log(
            '__initStatic:',
            typeof router.__initStatic === 'function'
                ? Function.prototype.toString.call(router.__initStatic)
                : 'none'
        );

        console.log(
            '__initStatic2:',
            typeof router.__initStatic2 === 'function'
                ? Function.prototype.toString.call(router.__initStatic2)
                : 'none'
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectRouterTargetTxV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const TARGET_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_VALUE = 238458238n;

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';

    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function formatTon(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1_000_000_000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')
            .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toString();
}

async function main() {
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER -> POOL TARGET TX SEARCH v2.2');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log('==========================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET POOL:');
    console.log(TARGET_POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET VALUE:');
    console.log(
        formatTon(TARGET_VALUE),
        'TON'
    );

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        ROUTER,
        {
            limit: 100,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER TRANSACTIONS FETCHED:');
    console.log(txs.length);

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        let outIndex = 0;

        for (
            const outMessage
            of tx.outMessages.values()
        ) {
            if (
                outMessage.info.type !==
                'internal'
            ) {
                outIndex++;
                continue;
            }

            const destination =
                outMessage.info.dest;

            const value =
                outMessage.info.value.coins;

            if (
                destination.equals(TARGET_POOL) &&
                value === TARGET_VALUE
            ) {
                found++;

                const body =
                    outMessage.body;

                const op =
                    getOpcode(body);

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '=========================================='
                );
                console.log(
                    `MATCH #${found}`
                );
                console.log(
                    '=========================================='
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('ROUTER TX LT:');
                console.log(
                    tx.lt.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('ROUTER TX HASH:');
                console.log(
                    tx.hash().toString('hex')
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
                console.log(tx.now);

                console.log(
                    new Date(
                        tx.now * 1000
                    ).toISOString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('OUT INDEX:');
                console.log(outIndex);

                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(
                    outMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(
                    destination.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    formatTon(value),
                    'TON'
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('OPCODE:');
                console.log(
                    opcodeHex(op)
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('BODY:');
                console.log(
                    body.bits.length,
                    'bits /',
                    body.refs.length,
                    'refs'
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('BOC:');
                console.log(
                    body
                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'THIS IS THE ROUTER MESSAGE'
                );
                console.log(
                    'THAT CREATED POOL LT 99491281000009.'
                );
            }

            outIndex++;
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '=========================================='
    );
    console.log(
        'MATCHES FOUND:',
        found
    );
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY COMPLETE'
    );
    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        '=========================================='
    );
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('ERROR:');
    console.error(error);
});

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000006';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

function rawAddress(address: string | null | undefined): string {
    if (!address) return 'null';

    try {
        return Address.parse(address).toRawString();
    } catch {
        return address;
    }
}

function printMessage(label: string, msg: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    if (!msg) {
        console.log('NO MESSAGE');
        return;
    }

    console.log('source:', rawAddress(msg.source));
    console.log('destination:', rawAddress(msg.destination));
    console.log('value:', msg.value ?? 'null');
    console.log('created_lt:', msg.created_lt ?? 'null');

    if (!msg.message_content) {
        console.log('NO MESSAGE CONTENT');
        return;
    }

    const body = msg.message_content;

    console.log('body type:', body['@type']);

    if (body.body) {
        const boc = body.body;

        console.log('body BOC:');
        console.log(boc);

        try {
            const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc);
            const slice = cell.beginParse();

            if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
                const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

                const hex =
                    '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');

                console.log('opcode:', hex);

                switch (opcode) {
                    case 0x37c096df:
                        console.log(
                            'opcode name: stonfi_provide_lp_v2'
                        );
                        break;

                    case 0x50c6a654:
                        console.log(
                            'opcode name: stonfi_add_liquidity_v2'
                        );
                        break;

                    case 0x7362d09c:
                        console.log(
                            'opcode name: jetton_notify'
                        );
                        break;

                    case 0x01f3835d:
                        console.log(
                            'opcode name: pton_ton_transfer'
                        );
                        break;

                    case 0x06ecd527:
                        console.log(
                            'opcode name: stonfi_cb_add_liquidity_v2'
                        );
                        break;

                    default:
                        console.log('opcode name: unknown');
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'opcode decode failed:',
                String(e)
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER TX INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' TARGET LT: 99809465000006');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router friendly:');
    console.log(ROUTER);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router raw:');
    console.log(rawAddress(ROUTER));

    const url =
        `${TONCENTER}?address=${encodeURIComponent(ROUTER)}` +
        `&limit=20`;

    console.log('');
    console.log('Reading Toncenter...');

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter error: ${JSON.stringify(json)}`
        );
    }

    const transactions = json.result;

    console.log('');
    console.log('Transactions returned:', transactions.length);

    const tx = transactions.find(
        (t: any) => String(t.transaction_id?.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Returned LTs:');

        for (const t of transactions) {
            console.log(
                String(t.transaction_id?.lt)
            );
        }

        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LT:');
    console.log(tx.transaction_id.lt);

    console.log('');
    console.log('HASH:');
    console.log(tx.transaction_id.hash);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(tx.account);

    console.log('');
    console.log('UTIME:');
    console.log(tx.utime);

    console.log('');
    console.log('SUCCESS / ABORTED:');
    console.log('aborted:', tx.description?.aborted);
    console.log(
        'compute exit:',
        tx.description?.compute_ph?.exit_code
    );
    console.log(
        'action success:',
        tx.description?.action?.success
    );
    console.log(
        'action result:',
        tx.description?.action?.result_code
    );

    printMessage(
        'IN MESSAGE',
        tx.in_msg
    );

    const outMessages =
        Array.isArray(tx.out_msgs)
            ? tx.out_msgs
            : [];

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' OUT MESSAGES');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(
        'count:',
        outMessages.length
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < outMessages.length;
        i++
    ) {
        printMessage(
            `OUT MESSAGE #${i}`,
            outMessages[i]
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' HISTORICAL EXPECTED VALUES');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount : 100000000000'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount    : 249908'
    );

    console.log(
        'min LP out     : 150109265'
    );

    console.log(
        'deadline       : 1790714871'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER ON-CHAIN INSPECTION');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = (provider as any)._tc;

    if (!client) {
        throw new Error('TonClient not found');
    }

    console.log('Network:', provider.network());
    console.log('Endpoint:', client.parameters?.endpoint);
    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 1. DEPLOYMENT =====');

    const deployed = await client.isContractDeployed(ROUTER);

    console.log('Deployed:', deployed);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 2. BALANCE =====');

    const balance = await client.getBalance(ROUTER);

    console.log('Balance nanoTON:', balance.toString());
    console.log(
        'Balance TON:',
        Number(balance) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 3. CONTRACT STATE =====');

    const state = await client.getContractState(ROUTER);

    console.log('State:', state.state);
    console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());

    console.log(
        'Code present:',
        !!state.code
    );

    console.log(
        'Data present:',
        !!state.data
    );

    if (state.code) {
        console.log('Code BOC length:', state.code.length);
    }

    if (state.data) {
        console.log('Data BOC length:', state.data.length);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 4. GET_ROUTER_DATA =====');

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethodWithError(
            ROUTER,
            'get_router_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('GET_ROUTER_DATA SUCCESS');
        console.log('Exit code:', result.exitCode);
        console.log('Stack:', result.stack);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('GET_ROUTER_DATA ERROR');

        if (error instanceof Error) {
            console.log('Message:', error.message);
        } else {
            console.log(error);
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('==================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectRouterV22Cells.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

function inspectCell(cell: Cell, name: string) {
    console.log(`===== ${name} =====`);

    console.log('Cell:', cell.toString());
    console.log('Bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', cell.refs.length);

    // Exotic-cell detection.
    console.log('Exotic:', cell.isExotic);

    console.log('');

    console.log('Bits as hex:');

    try {
        console.log(cell.bits.toString());
    } catch {
        console.log('HEX_UNAVAILABLE');
    }

    console.log('');

    if (cell.isExotic) {
        console.log('EXOTIC CELL - beginParse() intentionally skipped');
        console.log('');

        console.log('Refs:');

        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            const ref = cell.refs[i];

            console.log(`REF ${i}:`);
            console.log('Bits:', ref.bits.length);
            console.log('Refs:', ref.refs.length);
            console.log('Exotic:', ref.isExotic);

            try {
                console.log('Cell:', ref.toString());
            } catch {
                console.log('CELL_STRING_UNAVAILABLE');
            }

            console.log('');
        }

        return;
    }

    try {
        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        console.log('Remaining bits:', slice.remainingBits);
        console.log('Remaining refs:', slice.remainingRefs);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('beginParse ERROR');

        if (error instanceof Error) {
            console.log('Message:', error.message);
        } else {
            console.log(error);
        }
    }

    console.log('');

    if (cell.refs.length > 0) {
        console.log('Refs:');

        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            const ref = cell.refs[i];

            console.log(`REF ${i}:`);
            console.log('Bits:', ref.bits.length);
            console.log('Refs:', ref.refs.length);
            console.log('Exotic:', ref.isExotic);

            try {
                console.log('Cell:', ref.toString());
            } catch {
                console.log('CELL_STRING_UNAVAILABLE');
            }

            console.log('');
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER CELL STRUCTURE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' SAFE EXOTIC-CELL MODE');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = (provider as any)._tc;

    if (!client) {
        throw new Error('TonClient not found');
    }

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('Network:', provider.network());
    console.log('');

    const result = await client.runMethodWithError(
        ROUTER,
        'get_router_data',
        []
    );

    const items = result.stack.items;

    console.log('Stack item count:', items.length);
    console.log('');

    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        const item = items[i];

        if (item.type === 'int') {
            console.log(`===== ITEM ${i} =====`);
            console.log('Type:', item.type);
            console.log('Value:', item.value.toString());
            console.log('');
            continue;
        }

        if (item.type === 'cell') {
            inspectCell(item.cell, `ITEM ${i}`);
            console.log('');
            continue;
        }

        console.log(`===== ITEM ${i} =====`);
        console.log('Unknown type:', item.type);
        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('==================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectRouterVersion.ts
====================================================================================================
import {
    Address
} from '@ton/core';

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const server = {
   host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
    key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk='
};

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== ROUTER VERSION TEST =====');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: server.host,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
        reconnectTimeout: 3000
    });

    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        if (engine.isReady()) break;
        await sleep(500);
    }

    if (!engine.isReady()) {
        console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
        engine.close();
        return;
    }

    console.log('ENGINE READY');

    try {
        const client = new LiteClient({ engine });
        const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        const result = await client.runMethod(
            ROUTER,
            'get_router_version',
            Buffer.alloc(0),
            info.last
        );

        console.log('Exit code:', result.exitCode);
        console.log(
            'Result BOC:',
            result.result || 'NONE'
        );

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

async function inspectAccount(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    name: string,
    address: Address,
) {

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${name}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('');

        console.log(state.state.type);

        if (state.state.type === 'active') {
            console.log('');
            console.log('ACTIVE CONTRACT');

            console.log('Balance:');
            console.log(state.balance.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('Code present:');
            console.log(!!state.state.code);

            console.log('Data present:');
            console.log(!!state.state.data);

            if (state.state.code) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('Code bytes:');
                console.log(state.state.code.length / 2);

                console.log('Code BOC:');
                console.log(state.state.code);
            }

            if (state.state.data) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('Data bytes:');
                console.log(state.state.data.length / 2);

                console.log('Data BOC:');
                console.log(state.state.data);
            }
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('CONTRACT IS NOT ACTIVE');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('GET STATE ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
    }
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parseRaw(
    '0:92e1411ae546892f33b2c8a89ea90390d8ff4cfbb917a643b91e73f706fdb9d2'

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'

const ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(

function printSeparator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

async function inspectJettonWallet(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    label: string,
    wallet: Address,
    expectedMaster: Address
) {
    printSeparator(label);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Wallet:');
    console.log(wallet.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected Jetton Master:');
    console.log(expectedMaster.toString());

    const walletProvider =
        provider.provider(wallet);

    try {
        const result =
            await walletProvider.get(
                'get_wallet_data',
                []
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('GET_WALLET_DATA: SUCCESS');
        console.log('');

        console.log('Raw result:');
        console.log(result);

        const stack = result.stack;

        const balance =
            stack.readBigNumber();

        const owner =
            stack.readAddress();

        const jettonMaster =
            stack.readAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('----- DECODED WALLET DATA -----');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Balance raw:');
        console.log(balance.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('Balance display:');
        console.log(
            Number(balance) / 1e9
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Owner:');
        console.log(
            owner.toString()
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Jetton Master:');
        console.log(
            jettonMaster.toString()
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Master matches expected:');
        console.log(
            jettonMaster.equals(expectedMaster)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Remaining stack:');
        console.log(stack);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('GET_WALLET_DATA: ERROR');
        console.log(error);
    }
}

console.log('Locked:');
console.log(

FILE: scripts\inspectSdkBodyStructureV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as crypto from "crypto";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const USER =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;
const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

const BOC_FILE =
  "./final_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

function sha256(data: Buffer): string {
  return crypto
    .createHash("sha256")
    .update(data)
    .digest("hex");
}

function printCell(
  name: string,
  cell: Cell,
  depth = 0,
): void {
  const prefix = "  ".repeat(depth);

  const boc = cell.toBoc({ idx: false });

  console.log(`${prefix}${name}`);
  console.log(`${prefix}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
  console.log(`${prefix}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
  console.log(`${prefix}hash: ${cell.hash().toString("hex")}`);
  console.log(`${prefix}BOC bytes: ${boc.length}`);
  console.log(`${prefix}BOC SHA-256: ${sha256(boc)}`);

  for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
    printCell(`ref[${i}]`, cell.refs[i], depth + 1);
  }
}

function inspectJettonTransfer(
  label: string,
  cell: Cell,
): void {
  console.log();
  console.log(`===== ${label}: ROOT PARSE =====`);

  const s = cell.beginParse();

  console.log("Remaining bits:", s.remainingBits);
  console.log("Remaining refs:", s.remainingRefs);

  if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    console.log(
      "Opcode:",
      "0x" + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
    );
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
    console.log("query_id:", s.loadUintBig(64).toString());
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 124) {
    console.log("amount:", s.loadCoins().toString());
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 2) {
    const destination = s.loadAddress();
    console.log(
      "destination:",
      destination?.toString() ?? "null",
    );
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 2) {
    const response = s.loadAddress();
    console.log(
      "response:",
      response?.toString() ?? "null",
    );
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 1) {
    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();
    console.log(
      "custom payload:",
      hasCustomPayload ? "present" : "none",
    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
      console.log(
        "custom payload is in a reference/slice structure",
      );
    }
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 1) {
    const forwardPayloadInRef = s.loadBit();

    console.log(
      "forward payload:",
      forwardPayloadInRef
        ? "reference"
        : "inline",
    );

    if (forwardPayloadInRef) {
      if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        const forward = s.loadRef();

        console.log();
        console.log(
          "===== FORWARD PAYLOAD ROOT =====",
        );

        printCell("forwardPayload", forward);

        const fslice = forward.beginParse();

        console.log(
          "Forward remaining bits:",
          fslice.remainingBits,
        );
        console.log(
          "Forward remaining refs:",
          fslice.remainingRefs,
        );

        if (fslice.remainingBits >= 32) {
          const forwardOpcode = fslice.loadUint(32);

          console.log(
            "Forward opcode:",
            "0x" +
              forwardOpcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0"),
          );
        }

        if (fslice.remainingBits >= 2) {
          const routerWallet =
            fslice.loadAddress();

          console.log(
            "routerWalletAddress:",
            routerWallet?.toString() ?? "null",
          );
        }

        if (fslice.remainingBits >= 124) {
          console.log(
            "minLpOut:",
            fslice.loadCoins().toString(),
          );
        }

        console.log(
          "Forward trailing bits:",
          fslice.remainingBits,
        );

        console.log(
          "Forward trailing refs:",
          fslice.remainingRefs,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "ROOT trailing bits:",
    s.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "ROOT trailing refs:",
    s.remainingRefs,
  );
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 SDK BODY STRUCTURE INSPECTION");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // PREPARED BOC
  // --------------------------------------------------

  if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_FILE)) {
    throw new Error(`Missing ${BOC_FILE}`);
  }

  const preparedBase64 =
    fs.readFileSync(BOC_FILE, "utf8").trim();

  const preparedBytes =
    Buffer.from(preparedBase64, "base64");

  const preparedRoots =
    Cell.fromBoc(preparedBytes);

  if (preparedRoots.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
      `Expected one prepared root, got ${preparedRoots.length}`,
    );
  }

  const preparedBody = preparedRoots[0];

  console.log("===== PREPARED BOC =====");
  console.log(
    "Serialized BOC bytes:",
    preparedBytes.length,
  );
  console.log(
    "Canonical BOC bytes:",
    preparedBody.toBoc({ idx: false }).length,
  );
  console.log(
    "Root hash:",
    preparedBody.hash().toString("hex"),
  );
  console.log(
    "Canonical SHA-256:",
    sha256(
      preparedBody.toBoc({ idx: false }),
    ),
  );

  printCell("Prepared root", preparedBody);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // SDK
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== SDK PARAMETER BUILD =====");

  const routerAddress = Address.parse(ROUTER);
  const userAddress = Address.parse(USER);
  const roosterMaster = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);
  const ptonMaster = Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);

  const params =
    await routerContract.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
      provider.provider(routerAddress),
      {
        userWalletAddress: userAddress,
        sendTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
        otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
        sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
        minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
        gasAmount: GAS_AMOUNT,
        forwardGasAmount: FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,
        transferExcessAddress: userAddress,
      },
    );

  console.log("SDK method: PASS");
  console.log(
    "SDK to:",
    params.to.toString(),
  );
  console.log(
    "SDK value:",
    params.value.toString(),
  );

  if (!params.body) {
    throw new Error("SDK body is empty");
  }

  const sdkBody = params.body;

  console.log();
  console.log("===== SDK BODY TREE =====");

  printCell("SDK root", sdkBody);

  const sdkBytes =
    sdkBody.toBoc({ idx: false });

  console.log();
  console.log("SDK canonical bytes:", sdkBytes.length);
  console.log(
    "SDK canonical SHA-256:",
    sha256(sdkBytes),
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("===== SDK BODY BASE64 =====");
  console.log(sdkBytes.toString("base64"));

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Decode both structures
  // --------------------------------------------------

  inspectJettonTransfer(
    "SDK BODY",
    sdkBody,
  );

  inspectJettonTransfer(
    "PREPARED BOC",
    preparedBody,
  );

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Final
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STRUCTURE INSPECTION COMPLETE");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");
}
