import { Address, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { NetworkProvider } from "@ton/blueprint";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3"
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx"
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S"
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
);

const SEND_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;
const DEADLINE = 1790588068;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log("==================================================");
    console.log(" STON.FI v2.2 SDK SINGLE-SIDE JETTON PREFLIGHT");
    console.log(" READ-ONLY");
    console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
    console.log(" NO via.send()");
    console.log("==================================================");

    console.log("\n===== CONSTANTS =====");

    console.log("Router:", ROUTER.toString());
    console.log("ROOSTER:", ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log("pTON:", PTON.toString());
    console.log("User:", USER.toString());

    console.log("\nSend amount:", SEND_AMOUNT.toString());
    console.log("Min LP out:", MIN_LP_OUT.toString());
    console.log("Deadline:", DEADLINE.toString());

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     *
     * DEX SDK expects ContractProvider.
     *
     * NetworkProvider.provider(address)
     * supplies the correct ContractProvider.
     *
     * No transaction is sent here.
     */

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
        ROUTER
    );

    console.log("\n===== SDK OBJECT =====");
    console.log("Router class:", router.constructor.name);

    console.log("\n===== CALLING SDK =====");
    console.log(
        "getSingleSideProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams()"
    );

    const txParams =
        await router.getSingleSideProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            contractProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,

                sendAmount: SEND_AMOUNT,

                userWalletAddress: USER,

                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,

                deadline: DEADLINE,

                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,

                queryId: 0n,
            }
        );

    console.log("\n===== SDK TRANSACTION PARAMS =====");

    console.log("\nTO:");
    console.log(txParams.to.toString());

    console.log("\nVALUE:");
    console.log(txParams.value.toString());

    console.log(
        "TON:",
        Number(txParams.value) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    if (!txParams.body) {
        throw new Error(
            "SDK returned empty transaction body"
        );
    }

    const body = txParams.body;

    console.log("\nBODY BOC:");

    const bodyBoc =
        body.toBoc().toString("base64");

    console.log(bodyBoc);

    console.log("\n===== BODY SIZE =====");

    console.log("bits:", body.bits.length);
    console.log("refs:", body.refs.length);

    /*
     * Decode Jetton Transfer envelope.
     */

    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    const destination = s.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination =
        s.loadAddress();

    console.log("\n===== JETTON TRANSFER DECODE =====");

    console.log(
        "opcode:",
        "0x" +
        opcode
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")
    );

    console.log(
        "queryId:",
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "amount:",
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "destination:",
        destination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "responseDestination:",
        responseDestination.toString()
    );

    const customPayloadPresent =
        s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        "customPayload:",
        customPayloadPresent
            ? "PRESENT"
            : "NONE"
    );

    if (customPayloadPresent) {
        s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTonAmount =
        s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        "forward TON:",
        forwardTonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "forward TON display:",
        Number(forwardTonAmount) /
        1_000_000_000
    );

    const forwardPayloadIsRef =
        s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        "forward payload:",
        forwardPayloadIsRef
            ? "REFERENCE"
            : "INLINE"
    );

    let forwardPayload;

    if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {

        forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

    } else {

        forwardPayload = beginCell()
            .storeSlice(s)
            .endCell();

    }

    console.log("\n===== FORWARD PAYLOAD =====");

    const forwardBoc =
        forwardPayload
            .toBoc()
            .toString("base64");

    console.log("BOC:");
    console.log(forwardBoc);

    console.log(
        "bits:",
        forwardPayload.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        "refs:",
        forwardPayload.refs.length
    );

    /*
     * Decode PROVIDE_LP.
     */

    const p =
        forwardPayload.beginParse();

    const provideOpcode =
        p.loadUint(32);

    console.log("\n===== PROVIDE_LP =====");

    console.log(
        "opcode:",
        "0x" +
        provideOpcode
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")
    );

    const routerWallet =
        p.loadAddress();

    const refundAddress =
        p.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress =
        p.loadAddress();

    const deadline =
        p.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        "routerWalletAddress:",
        routerWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "refundAddress:",
        refundAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "excessesAddress:",
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "deadline:",
        deadline.toString()
    );

    const paramsRef =
        p.loadRef();

    const q =
        paramsRef.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        q.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress =
        q.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive =
        q.loadBit();

    const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
        q.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        "\n===== PROVIDE_LP PARAMS ====="
    );

    console.log(
        "minLpOut:",
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "receiverAddress:",
        receiverAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "bothPositive:",
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        "dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount:",
        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
    );

    const customDexPayload =
        q.loadMaybeRef();

    console.log(
        "dexCustomPayload:",
        customDexPayload
            ? "PRESENT"
            : "NONE"
    );

    console.log(
        "\n===== SAFETY CHECKS ====="
    );

    console.log(
        "destination == ROUTER:",
        destination.equals(ROUTER)
    );

    console.log(
        "amount == expected:",
        amount === SEND_AMOUNT
    );

    console.log(
        "bothPositive == false:",
        bothPositive === false
    );

    console.log(
        "minLpOut == expected:",
        minLpOut === MIN_LP_OUT
    );

    console.log(
        "deadline == expected:",
        deadline === BigInt(DEADLINE)
    );

    console.log(
        "receiver == USER:",
        receiverAddress.equals(USER)
    );

    console.log(
        "refund == USER:",
        refundAddress.equals(USER)
    );

    console.log(
        "excesses == USER:",
        excessesAddress.equals(USER)
    );

    console.log("\n==================================================");
    console.log(" READ-ONLY PREFLIGHT FINISHED");
    console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT");
    console.log("==================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectSegment412Consumers.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([2053, 2101, 2612]);

const SEG412_START = 0x00187708;
const SEG412_END   = 0x00187874;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018f0d8;

function u32(buf: Buffer, off: number): number {
    if (off < 0 || off + 4 > buf.length) return 0xffffffff;
    return buf.readUInt32LE(off);
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function isSeg412Ptr(v: number): boolean {
    return v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END;
}

function isLikelyMemoryPtr(v: number): boolean {
    return (
        v >= 0x00180000 &&
        v < 0x00200000 &&
        (v & 3) === 0
    );
}

function collectFunctions(buf: Buffer): number[] {
    const found = new Set<number>();

    /*
     * Candidate function-index values observed in the
     * Segment 413 structures.
     *
     * We deliberately do not assume that every u32 in the
     * data area is a function index.
     */
    for (let off = SEG413_START; off < SEG413_START + 0x400; off += 4) {
        const v = u32(buf, off);

        if (
            v >= 2000 &&
            v < 5000
        ) {
            found.add(v);
        }
    }

    return [...found].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

function main() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("SEGMENT 412 CONSUMER INSPECTOR");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
        console.error("");
        console.error("WASM NOT FOUND:");
        console.error(WASM);
        process.exit(1);
    }

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log("");

    /*
     * Search all 32-bit aligned data locations for the
     * three target table indexes.
     */
    const hits: {
        target: number;
        offset: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let off = 0; off + 4 <= buf.length; off += 4) {
        const v = u32(buf, off);

        if (TARGETS.has(v)) {
            hits.push({
                target: v,
                offset: off,
            });
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET HITS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(
            `TARGET ${h.target} @ ${hex(h.offset)}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("STRUCTURE CONSUMER ANALYSIS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `TARGET ${h.target} @ ${hex(h.offset)}`
        );
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const base = h.offset;

        /*
         * Inspect the surrounding structure.
         */
        for (let rel = 0; rel <= 0x9c; rel += 4) {
            const off = base + rel;
            if (off + 4 > buf.length) break;

            const v = u32(buf, off);

            let tag = "";

            if (isSeg412Ptr(v)) {
                tag = " <<< SEG412";
            } else if (v === SEG413_START) {
                tag = " <<< SEG413";
            } else if (isLikelyMemoryPtr(v)) {
                tag = " <<< PTR?";
            }

            if (
                rel < 0x20 ||
                isSeg412Ptr(v) ||
                v === SEG413_START ||
                (v >= 2000 && v < 5000)
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `+${rel.toString().padStart(3, " ")} ` +
                    `${hex(off)}  ${hex(v)}  ${v}${tag}`
                );
            }
        }

        /*
         * Examine each SEG412 pointer from this structure.
         */
        console.log("");
        console.log("SEG412 POINTERS:");

        const seg412Ptrs = new Set<number>();

        for (let rel = 0; rel <= 0x9c; rel += 4) {
            const off = base + rel;
            if (off + 4 > buf.length) break;

            const v = u32(buf, off);

            if (isSeg412Ptr(v)) {
                seg412Ptrs.add(v);
                console.log(
                    `  +${rel.toString().padStart(3, " ")} -> ${hex(v)}`
                );
            }
        }

        /*
         * For each SEG412 pointer inspect a compact window.
         */
        for (const ptr of seg412Ptrs) {
            console.log("");
            console.log(`SEG412 WINDOW @ ${hex(ptr)}`);

            const start = Math.max(0, ptr - 0x20);
            const end = Math.min(buf.length, ptr + 0x80);

            for (let off = start; off < end; off += 4) {
                const v = u32(buf, off);

                let tag = "";

                if (isSeg412Ptr(v)) {
                    tag = " SEG412";
                } else if (v === SEG413_START) {
                    tag = " SEG413";
                } else if (v >= 2000 && v < 5000) {
                    tag = " FUNC?";
                }

                if (
                    isSeg412Ptr(v) ||
                    v === SEG413_START ||
                    (v >= 2000 && v < 5000)
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `  ${hex(off)} = ${hex(v)} ${v}${tag}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /*
     * Collect the function-index candidates from SEG413.
     */
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("SEG413 FUNCTION INDEX CANDIDATES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const functions = collectFunctions(buf);

    console.log(`Count : ${functions.length}`);
    console.log("");

    for (const fn of functions) {
        console.log(`  FUNCTION ${fn}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("IMPORTANT");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(
        "This pass identifies structural/data relationships only."
    );
    console.log(
        "No WASM instruction execution or modification is performed."
    );
    console.log("============================================================");
}

main();

FILE: scripts\inspectSegment412Structure.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    0x00187724,
    0x0018744c,
    0x00189dec,
    0x00189f9c,
    0x0018a8b0,
    0x0018ab5c
];

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f512;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse data section and reconstruct linear memory
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function uleb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readU32(mem: Buffer, addr: number) {
    if (addr < 0 || addr + 4 > mem.length)
        return 0;

    return mem.readUInt32LE(addr);
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function region(v: number) {
    if (v >= SEG412_START && v < SEG412_END)
        return "SEG412";

    if (v >= SEG413_START && v < SEG413_END)
        return "SEG413";

    if (v === 2053 || v === 2101 || v === 2612)
        return "TARGET_TABLE_INDEX";

    if (v >= 2700 && v <= 9000)
        return "FUNCTION_INDEX?";

    return "";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find data section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let dataStart = -1;
let dataEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {
        dataStart = start;
        dataEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (dataStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Data section not found");

const segCount = uleb(wasm, dataStart);
p = segCount.next;

const mem = Buffer.alloc(32 * 1024 * 1024);

let mapped = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount.value; seg++) {

    const flags = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value === 0) {

        if (wasm[p] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

        const off = uleb(wasm, p + 1);
        p = off.next;

        if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");

        p++;

        const size = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = size.next;

        wasm.copy(mem, off.value, p, p + size.value);

        mapped += size.value;

        p += size.value;

    } else if (flags.value === 2) {

        const memoryIndex = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = memoryIndex.next;

        if (wasm[p] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

        const off = uleb(wasm, p + 1);
        p = off.next;

        if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");

        p++;

        const size = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = size.next;

        wasm.copy(mem, off.value, p, p + size.value);

        mapped += size.value;

        p += size.value;

    } else {

        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported data flags ${flags.value} at ${hex(p)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT 412 STRUCTURE INSPECTOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Data segments :", segCount.value);
console.log("Mapped bytes  :", mapped);
console.log();

for (const base of TARGETS) {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`STRUCTURE @ ${hex(base)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (let off = 0; off < 160; off += 4) {

        const addr = base + off;
        const v = readU32(mem, addr);

        const r = region(v);

        let extra = "";

        if (r)
            extra = `  <<< ${r}`;

        console.log(
            `+${off.toString().padStart(3)}  ${hex(addr)}  ${hex(v)}  ${v}${extra}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Follow pointer-looking fields into Segment 413
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    for (let off = 0; off < 160; off += 4) {

        const addr = base + off;
        const v = readU32(mem, addr);

        if (
            v >= SEG413_START &&
            v < SEG413_END &&
            (v & 3) === 0
        ) {

            console.log(
                `SEG413 POINTER: +${off} -> ${hex(v)}`
            );

            console.log("  First 32 u32 values:");

            for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {

                const a = v + i * 4;
                const x = readU32(mem, a);

                let tag = "";

                if (x === 2053 ||
                    x === 2101 ||
                    x === 2612)
                    tag = " <<< TARGET";

                console.log(
                    `    [${i.toString().padStart(2)}] ${hex(a)} = ${x}${tag}`
                );
            }

            console.log();
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET SEARCH IN SEGMENT 412");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of [2053, 2101, 2612]) {

    console.log();
    console.log(`TARGET ${target}`);

    for (let addr = SEG412_START; addr <= SEG412_END - 4; addr += 4) {

        if (readU32(mem, addr) === target) {
            console.log(
                `  FOUND @ ${hex(addr)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 0x0018ab5c;

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find Data section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let dataStart = -1;
let dataEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {
        dataStart = start;
        dataEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (dataStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Data section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Reconstruct linear memory
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const memory = new Uint8Array(32 * 1024 * 1024);

let q = dataStart;

const segmentCount = uleb(q);
q = segmentCount.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT 412 TAIL / DISPATCH STRUCTURE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("DATA SEGMENTS =", segmentCount.value);

for (let i = 0; i < segmentCount.value; i++) {

    const flags = uleb(q);
    q = flags.next;

    let memoryOffset = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {

        // i32.const
        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Unexpected offset opcode");

        const off = uleb(q);
        memoryOffset = off.value;
        q = off.next;

        // end
        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");
    }
    else if (flags.value === 2) {

        const memidx = uleb(q);
        q = memidx.next;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Unexpected offset opcode");

        const off = uleb(q);
        memoryOffset = off.value;
        q = off.next;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");
    }
    else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported data flags ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const sizeInfo = uleb(q);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    q = sizeInfo.next;

    memory.set(
        wasm.subarray(q, q + size),
        memoryOffset
    );

    q += size;
}

console.log(
    "DATA PARSER END =",
    hex(q)
);

console.log(
    "DATA EXPECTED   =",
    hex(dataEnd)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readU32(addr: number) {
    return (
        memory[addr] |
        (memory[addr + 1] << 8) |
        (memory[addr + 2] << 16) |
        (memory[addr + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function region(addr: number) {

    if (addr >= 0x0018744c && addr < 0x0018ab8d)
        return "SEG412";

    if (addr >= 0x0018ab98 && addr < 0x0018f512)
        return "SEG413";

    return "OTHER";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Target structure
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");

    let count = 0;

    console.log();

            console.log(
                hex(a),

FILE: scripts\inspectSegment669.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

const sec = findSection(11);
let p = readULEB(sec.start).next;

for (let seg = 0; seg < 670; seg++) {
    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value === 1) {
        const size = readULEB(p);
        p = size.next + size.value;
        continue;
    }

    let memoryIndex = 0;
    let offset = 0;

    if (flags.value === 2) {
        const mem = readULEB(p);
        memoryIndex = mem.value;
        p = mem.next;
    } else if (flags.value !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported flags ${flags.value} at segment ${seg}`
        );
    }

    if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected offset opcode at segment ${seg}`);
    }

    const off = readSLEB(p);
    offset = off.value >>> 0;
    p = off.next;

    if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(`Missing offset end at segment ${seg}`);
    }

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const dataStart = p;
    const dataEnd = dataStart + sizeInfo.value;

    if (seg === 669) {
        console.log("SEGMENT 669");
        console.log(`MEMORY = ${memoryIndex}`);
        console.log(`LINEAR OFFSET = 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`SIZE = ${sizeInfo.value}`);
        console.log(`WASM FILE OFFSET = 0x${dataStart.toString(16)}`);
        console.log("");

        for (let i = 0; i < sizeInfo.value; i += 8) {
            const end = Math.min(i + 8, sizeInfo.value);
            const bytes = Array.from(
                wasm.subarray(dataStart + i, dataStart + end)
            );

            console.log(
                `+${i.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}: ` +
                bytes.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")
            );
        }

        console.log("");
        console.log("TARGET WORDS:");

        for (const target of [0x195350, 0x195354]) {
            const rel = target - offset;
            const value = wasm.readUInt32LE(dataStart + rel);

            console.log(
                `0x${target.toString(16)} = ${value} (0x${value.toString(16)})`
            );
        }

        break;
    }

    p = dataEnd;
}

console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectSegments412_413.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT 412 / 413 RAW INSPECTOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

function u32(pos: number): number {
  return (
    (wasm[pos] |
      (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
      (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
      (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)) >>>
    0
  );
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB128");
}

function findDataSection(): { payload: number; size: number } {
  if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
  ) {
    throw new Error("Not a valid WASM file");
  }

  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    if (sectionId === 11) {
      return {
        payload: p,
        size: sectionSize,
      };
    }

    p += sectionSize;
  }

  throw new Error("WASM data section not found");
}

const dataSection = findDataSection();

console.log("DATA SECTION");
console.log(`  payload : 0x${dataSection.payload.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`  size    : ${dataSection.size.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

let p = dataSection.payload;

const segmentCountInfo = readULEB(p);
const segmentCount = segmentCountInfo.value;
p = segmentCountInfo.next;

console.log(`Data segments : ${segmentCount.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

interface SegmentInfo {
  index: number;
  flags: number;
  memoryIndex?: number;
  offsetExpr?: number;
  dataOffset: number;
  size: number;
  end: number;
}

const segments: SegmentInfo[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < segmentCount; i++) {
  const flagsInfo = readULEB(p);
  const flags = flagsInfo.value;
  p = flagsInfo.next;

  let memoryIndex: number | undefined;
  let offsetExpr: number | undefined;

  /*
   * Passive segment:
   *   flags bit 0 = 1
   *
   * Active segment:
   *   flags bit 0 = 0
   *
   * For the normal active form:
   *   flags = 0:
   *      offset expression
   *
   *   flags = 2:
   *      memory index
   *      offset expression
   */

  if ((flags & 1) === 0) {
    if (flags === 2) {
      const memInfo = readULEB(p);
      memoryIndex = memInfo.value;
      p = memInfo.next;
    }

    /*
     * Read the offset expression.
     * We only need the resulting i32.const value.
     *
     * Expected form:
     *   0x41 <signed/unsigned LEB> 0x0b
     */
    const opcode = wasm[p++];

    if (opcode === 0x41) {
      const valueInfo = readULEB(p);
      offsetExpr = valueInfo.value;
      p = valueInfo.next;
    } else {
      /*
       * Non-constant offset expression.
       * Skip instructions until END (0x0b).
       */
      while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        if (b === 0x0b) {
          break;
        }

        /*
         * This branch is only a fallback.
         * We do not attempt to evaluate the expression.
         */
      }
    }

    /*
     * If the expression was constant, consume END.
     * If it was non-constant, END was already consumed above.
     */
    if (offsetExpr !== undefined && wasm[p] === 0x0b) {
      p++;
    }
  }

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const dataOffset = p;
  const end = dataOffset + size;

  segments.push({
    index: i,
    flags,
    memoryIndex,
    offsetExpr,
    dataOffset,
    size,
    end,
  });

  p = end;
}

function printSegment(index: number) {
  const s = segments[index];

  if (!s) {
    console.log(`Segment ${index}: NOT FOUND`);
    return;
  }

  console.log(`SEGMENT ${index}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`index       : ${s.index}`);
  console.log(`flags       : ${s.flags}`);
  console.log(
    `memoryIndex : ${
      s.memoryIndex === undefined ? "n/a" : s.memoryIndex
    }`
  );
  console.log(
    `memory addr : ${
      s.offsetExpr === undefined
        ? "non-constant"
        : "0x" + s.offsetExpr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    }`
  );
  console.log(
    `data offset : 0x${s.dataOffset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
  console.log(`data size   : ${s.size.toLocaleString()}`);
  console.log(
    `data end    : 0x${s.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
  console.log("");
}

printSegment(412);
printSegment(413);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT RELATION");
console.log("============================================================");

const s412 = segments[412];
const s413 = segments[413];

if (s412 && s413) {
  console.log(
    `412 memory range : 0x${(s412.offsetExpr ?? 0)
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${((s412.offsetExpr ?? 0) + s412.size)
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  console.log(
    `413 memory range : 0x${(s413.offsetExpr ?? 0)
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${((s413.offsetExpr ?? 0) + s413.size)
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  console.log("");

  if (
    s412.offsetExpr !== undefined &&
    s413.offsetExpr !== undefined
  ) {
    const end412 = s412.offsetExpr + s412.size;

    if (end412 === s413.offsetExpr) {
      console.log("RESULT : SEGMENT 413 STARTS IMMEDIATELY AFTER SEGMENT 412");
    } else if (end412 < s413.offsetExpr) {
      console.log(
        `RESULT : GAP = ${(s413.offsetExpr - end412).toLocaleString()} bytes`
      );
    } else {
      console.log(
        `RESULT : OVERLAP = ${(end412 - s413.offsetExpr).toLocaleString()} bytes`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW FIRST 64 BYTES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const index of [412, 413]) {
  const s = segments[index];

  if (!s) continue;

  console.log(`SEGMENT ${index}:`);

  const count = Math.min(64, s.size);
  let line = "";

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    line += wasm[s.dataOffset + i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";

    if ((i + 1) % 16 === 0) {
      console.log(line.trim());
      line = "";
    }
  }

  if (line.length > 0) {
    console.log(line.trim());
  }

  console.log("");
}
